gateway.py
python
"""
AI 网关服务 - 统一对话入口、流式响应、工具调用分发
"""

import json
import asyncio
import uuid
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional, AsyncIterator
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime

from fastapi.responses import StreamingResponse
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.config import settings
from app.core.ai_digital_base.inference import model_registry
from app.core.ai_digital_base.conversation import ConversationManager
from app.core.ai_digital_base.rag import RAGService
from app.core.agent_engine.orchestrator import get_agent_orchestrator
from app.core.agent_engine.tools.registry import ToolRegistry
from app.models.ai_conversation import ContextType
from app.utils.security import mask_sensitive_data


@dataclass
class ChatRequest:
    """对话请求数据结构"""
    messages: List[Dict[str, str]]
    model: str = settings.DEFAULT_LLM_MODEL
    stream: bool = True
    context_type: Optional[str] = None  # design/query/approval/integration/system/workbench
    context_ref: Optional[str] = None   # 关联的业务对象 ID
    tenant_id: str = None
    user_id: str = None
    temperature: float = 0.7
    max_tokens: int = 4096
    tools: Optional[List[str]] = None   # 指定可用工具列表


class AIGateway:
    """AI 网关服务 - 处理所有 AI 对话请求"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.conv_manager = ConversationManager()
        self.rag_service = RAGService()
        self._tool_registry: Optional[ToolRegistry] = None
    
    @property
    def tool_registry(self) -> ToolRegistry:
        """延迟获取工具注册中心（避免循环导入）"""
        if self._tool_registry is None:
            from app.core.agent_engine.tools.registry import tool_registry
            self._tool_registry = tool_registry
        return self._tool_registry
    
    async def chat_stream(self, request: ChatRequest) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
        """
        流式对话处理
        1. 创建/获取会话
        2. 检索相关知识（RAG）
        3. 调用大模型生成
        4. 检测并执行工具调用
        5. 保存对话记录
        """
        
        # 1. 获取或创建会话
        conv = await self.conv_manager.get_or_create(
            tenant_id=request.tenant_id,
            user_id=request.user_id,
            context_type=request.context_type,
            context_ref=request.context_ref,
        )
        
        # 保存用户消息
        user_message = request.messages[-1]["content"] if request.messages else ""
        await self.conv_manager.add_message(
            conv.id, 
            "user", 
            user_message,
            model_used=request.model
        )
        
        # 2. RAG 知识检索
        knowledge_chunks = []
        if request.context_type in ["design", "query", "workflow", "integration"]:
            knowledge_chunks = await self.rag_service.search(
                query=user_message,
                tenant_id=request.tenant_id,
                context_type=request.context_type,
                top_k=5
            )
        
        # 3. 构建系统提示
        system_prompt = self._build_system_prompt(
            request.context_type, 
            knowledge_chunks,
            request.tenant_id
        )
        
        # 构建完整消息列表
        full_messages = [{"role": "system", "content": system_prompt}]
        
        # 添加历史消息（最近10轮）
        history_messages = await self.conv_manager.get_recent_messages(conv.id, limit=10)
        for msg in history_messages:
            full_messages.append({"role": msg["role"], "content": msg["content"]})
        
        # 添加当前用户消息（如果不在历史中）
        if not history_messages or history_messages[-1]["role"] != "user":
            full_messages.append({"role": "user", "content": user_message})
        
        # 4. 获取模型后端
        backend = model_registry.get(request.model)
        if not backend:
            yield f"data: {json.dumps({'error': f'Model {request.model} not found'})}\n\n"
            return
        
        # 5. 准备工具定义（如果需要）
        tools = None
        if request.tools or self._should_use_tools(request.context_type):
            tools = self.tool_registry.get_openai_schema(
                filter_by_context=request.context_type
            )
        
        # 6. 流式生成
        full_response = ""
        tool_calls = []
        
        try:
            async for chunk in backend.stream_generate(
                messages=full_messages,
                temperature=request.temperature,
                max_tokens=request.max_tokens,
                tools=tools,
                tool_choice="auto" if tools else None
            ):
                # 解析流式响应
                if chunk.get("type") == "content":
                    content = chunk.get("content", "")
                    full_response += content
                    yield f"data: {json.dumps({'delta': content})}\n\n"
                
                elif chunk.get("type") == "tool_call":
                    tool_calls.append(chunk.get("tool_call"))
                    yield f"data: {json.dumps({'tool_call': chunk.get('tool_call')})}\n\n"
                
                elif chunk.get("type") == "error":
                    yield f"data: {json.dumps({'error': chunk.get('error')})}\n\n"
                    return
            
            # 7. 执行工具调用
            tool_results = []
            if tool_calls:
                for tool_call in tool_calls:
                    result = await self._execute_tool(
                        tool_name=tool_call["name"],
                        arguments=tool_call.get("arguments", {}),
                        context={
                            "tenant_id": request.tenant_id,
                            "user_id": request.user_id,
                            "conversation_id": str(conv.id)
                        }
                    )
                    tool_results.append({
                        "name": tool_call["name"],
                        "result": result
                    })
                    yield f"data: {json.dumps({'tool_result': tool_results[-1]})}\n\n"
                
                # 如果有工具调用，可能需要二次生成（ReAct）
                if self._should_continue_with_tool_results(tool_results):
                    final_response = await self._generate_final_response(
                        backend, full_messages, tool_results, request
                    )
                    full_response = final_response
                    yield f"data: {json.dumps({'delta': final_response})}\n\n"
            
            # 8. 保存助手消息
            await self.conv_manager.add_message(
                conv.id,
                "assistant",
                full_response,
                tool_calls=tool_calls if tool_calls else None,
                model_used=request.model
            )
            
            # 9. 发送完成信号
            yield f"data: {json.dumps({'done': True, 'conversation_id': str(conv.id)})}\n\n"
            
        except Exception as e:
            error_msg = f"AI 服务错误: {str(e)}"
            yield f"data: {json.dumps({'error': error_msg})}\n\n"
            # 保存错误消息
            await self.conv_manager.add_message(
                conv.id,
                "system",
                error_msg
            )
    
    def _build_system_prompt(
        self, 
        context_type: Optional[str], 
        knowledge_chunks: List[str],
        tenant_id: str
    ) -> str:
        """构建系统提示词"""
        
        base_prompt = """你是 Kflower 企业智能管理低代码平台的 AI 助手。
你的职责是帮助用户完成以下任务：
1. 创建和管理业务模板（表单、列表、报表）
2. 设计和优化工作流程
3. 数据分析和可视化
4. 系统配置和运维
5. 解答平台使用问题

回答要求：
- 专业、准确、简洁
- 当用户需要创建模板时，提供具体的字段建议
- 当用户询问数据时，引导用户使用自然语言查询功能
- 当涉及敏感操作时，提醒用户注意权限和数据安全
"""
        
        context_prompts = {
            "design": "你正在协助用户进行模板设计。你可以帮助创建表单字段、设置数据规范、配置业务规则。",
            "query": "你正在协助用户进行数据分析。用户可以用自然语言提问，你会生成对应的查询并返回结果。",
            "approval": "你正在协助用户进行流程审批。你可以总结申请内容、评估风险、提供审批建议。",
            "integration": "你正在协助用户进行数据集成。你可以推荐字段映射、检测数据质量问题。",
            "system": "你正在协助用户进行系统运维。你可以查看系统状态、执行运维操作。",
            "workbench": "你正在协助用户完成日常工作。你可以管理待办、搜索信息、快速发起流程。"
        }
        
        if context_type and context_type in context_prompts:
            base_prompt += f"\n\n{context_prompts[context_type]}"
        
        # 添加知识库内容
        if knowledge_chunks:
            base_prompt += "\n\n## 参考知识\n"
            for i, chunk in enumerate(knowledge_chunks[:3]):
                base_prompt += f"\n{i+1}. {chunk[:500]}"
        
        # 添加当前时间
        base_prompt += f"\n\n当前时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}"
        
        return base_prompt
    
    def _should_use_tools(self, context_type: Optional[str]) -> bool:
        """判断是否应该启用工具调用"""
        tool_enabled_contexts = ["design", "query", "workflow", "integration", "system"]
        return context_type in tool_enabled_contexts
    
    async def _execute_tool(
        self, 
        tool_name: str, 
        arguments: Dict[str, Any],
        context: Dict[str, Any]
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """执行工具调用"""
        try:
            result = await self.tool_registry.execute(tool_name, arguments, context)
            return {"success": True, "data": result}
        except Exception as e:
            return {"success": False, "error": str(e)}
    
    def _should_continue_with_tool_results(self, tool_results: List[Dict]) -> bool:
        """判断是否需要用工具结果继续生成"""
        # 如果工具执行成功且不是最终输出类型，则继续生成
        return any(r.get("result", {}).get("success") for r in tool_results)
    
    async def _generate_final_response(
        self,
        backend,
        messages: List[Dict],
        tool_results: List[Dict],
        request: ChatRequest
    ) -> str:
        """基于工具结果生成最终响应"""
        # 添加工具结果到消息列表
        for tr in tool_results:
            messages.append({
                "role": "tool",
                "content": json.dumps(tr["result"]),
                "tool_call_id": tr.get("id", str(uuid.uuid4()))
            })
        
        response = await backend.generate(
            messages=messages,
            temperature=request.temperature,
            max_tokens=request.max_tokens
        )
        return response
    
    async def chat_sync(self, request: ChatRequest) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """同步对话（非流式）"""
        full_response = ""
        async for chunk in self.chat_stream(request):
            data = json.loads(chunk.replace("data: ", ""))
            if "delta" in data:
                full_response += data["delta"]
            elif "done" in data:
                return {
                    "response": full_response,
                    "conversation_id": data["conversation_id"]
                }
        return {"response": full_response}


# 全局实例
ai_gateway = AIGateway()


async def init_ai_gateway():
    """初始化 AI 网关（注册模型等）"""
    # 注册云端模型
    if settings.DEEPSEEK_API_KEY:
        from app.core.ai_digital_base.inference import DeepSeekBackend
        model_registry.register(
            "deepseek-chat",
            DeepSeekBackend(
                api_key=settings.DEEPSEEK_API_KEY,
                base_url=settings.DEEPSEEK_BASE_URL,
                model_name="deepseek-chat"
            )
        )
        model_registry.register(
            "deepseek-coder",
            DeepSeekBackend(
                api_key=settings.DEEPSEEK_API_KEY,
                base_url=settings.DEEPSEEK_BASE_URL,
                model_name="deepseek-coder"
            )
        )
    
    # 注册本地模型
    if settings.LOCAL_LLM_ENDPOINT:
        from app.core.ai_digital_base.inference import VLLMBackend
        model_registry.register(
            "local-llm",
            VLLMBackend(endpoint=settings.LOCAL_LLM_ENDPOINT)
        )
    
    # 可添加更多模型...
conversation.py
python
"""
对话管理服务 - 会话持久化、上下文维护
"""

import json
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from datetime import datetime
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy import select, update, desc

from app.database import AsyncSessionLocal
from app.models.ai_conversation import AIConversation, AIMessage, AIToolCall, ContextType
from app.core.ai_digital_base.embeddings import embedding_service


class ConversationManager:
    """对话管理器"""
    
    async def get_or_create(
        self,
        tenant_id: str,
        user_id: str,
        context_type: Optional[str] = None,
        context_ref: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> AIConversation:
        """获取或创建会话"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            # 查找最近的活跃会话（同一上下文下）
            stmt = select(AIConversation).where(
                AIConversation.tenant_id == tenant_id,
                AIConversation.user_id == user_id,
                AIConversation.context_type == context_type,
                AIConversation.context_ref == context_ref,
                AIConversation.is_archived == False
            ).order_by(desc(AIConversation.updated_at)).limit(1)
            
            result = await session.execute(stmt)
            conv = result.scalar_one_or_none()
            
            if conv:
                # 更新更新时间
                conv.updated_at = datetime.now()
                await session.commit()
                return conv
            
            # 创建新会话
            conv = AIConversation(
                tenant_id=tenant_id,
                user_id=user_id,
                context_type=ContextType(context_type) if context_type else ContextType.WORKBENCH,
                context_ref=context_ref,
                title=None  # 后续根据第一条消息生成
            )
            session.add(conv)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(conv)
            return conv
    
    async def add_message(
        self,
        conversation_id: str,
        role: str,
        content: str,
        tool_calls: Optional[List[Dict]] = None,
        model_used: Optional[str] = None,
        token_usage: Optional[Dict] = None,
        latency_ms: Optional[int] = None
    ) -> AIMessage:
        """添加消息到会话"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            message = AIMessage(
                conversation_id=conversation_id,
                role=role,
                content=content,
                tool_calls=tool_calls,
                model_used=model_used,
                token_usage=token_usage,
                latency_ms=latency_ms
            )
            session.add(message)
            
            # 更新会话时间和标题（如果是第一条用户消息）
            conv = await session.get(AIConversation, conversation_id)
            if conv:
                conv.updated_at = datetime.now()
                if conv.title is None and role == "user" and content:
                    # 生成会话标题（取前30字符）
                    conv.title = content[:30] + ("..." if len(content) > 30 else "")
            
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(message)
            return message
    
    async def add_tool_call(
        self,
        message_id: str,
        tool_name: str,
        arguments: Dict[str, Any],
        result: Any = None,
        status: str = "completed",
        error_message: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> AIToolCall:
        """记录工具调用"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            tool_call = AIToolCall(
                message_id=message_id,
                tool_name=tool_name,
                arguments=arguments,
                result=result,
                status=status,
                error_message=error_message,
                started_at=datetime.now(),
                completed_at=datetime.now() if status == "completed" else None
            )
            session.add(tool_call)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(tool_call)
            return tool_call
    
    async def get_recent_messages(
        self, 
        conversation_id: str, 
        limit: int = 20
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """获取最近的对话消息"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            stmt = select(AIMessage).where(
                AIMessage.conversation_id == conversation_id
            ).order_by(AIMessage.created_at).limit(limit)
            
            result = await session.execute(stmt)
            messages = result.scalars().all()
            
            return [
                {
                    "id": str(msg.id),
                    "role": msg.role,
                    "content": msg.content,
                    "tool_calls": msg.tool_calls,
                    "created_at": msg.created_at.isoformat() if msg.created_at else None
                }
                for msg in messages
            ]
    
    async def get_conversation_history(
        self,
        conversation_id: str,
        include_tool_calls: bool = True
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """获取完整会话历史"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            conv = await session.get(AIConversation, conversation_id)
            if not conv:
                return {}
            
            stmt = select(AIMessage).where(
                AIMessage.conversation_id == conversation_id
            ).order_by(AIMessage.created_at)
            
            result = await session.execute(stmt)
            messages = result.scalars().all()
            
            history = {
                "id": str(conv.id),
                "title": conv.title,
                "context_type": conv.context_type.value if conv.context_type else None,
                "created_at": conv.created_at.isoformat() if conv.created_at else None,
                "updated_at": conv.updated_at.isoformat() if conv.updated_at else None,
                "messages": []
            }
            
            for msg in messages:
                msg_data = {
                    "id": str(msg.id),
                    "role": msg.role,
                    "content": msg.content,
                    "created_at": msg.created_at.isoformat() if msg.created_at else None
                }
                
                if include_tool_calls and msg.tool_calls:
                    msg_data["tool_calls"] = msg.tool_calls
                
                history["messages"].append(msg_data)
            
            return history
    
    async def archive_conversation(self, conversation_id: str):
        """归档会话"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            stmt = update(AIConversation).where(
                AIConversation.id == conversation_id
            ).values(is_archived=True)
            await session.execute(stmt)
            await session.commit()
    
    async def delete_conversation(self, conversation_id: str):
        """删除会话（软删除）"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            conv = await session.get(AIConversation, conversation_id)
            if conv:
                conv.is_deleted = True
                await session.commit()
    
    async def list_user_conversations(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        tenant_id: str,
        limit: int = 50
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """列出用户的会话列表"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            stmt = select(AIConversation).where(
                AIConversation.tenant_id == tenant_id,
                AIConversation.user_id == user_id,
                AIConversation.is_archived == False,
                AIConversation.is_deleted == False
            ).order_by(desc(AIConversation.updated_at)).limit(limit)
            
            result = await session.execute(stmt)
            convs = result.scalars().all()
            
            return [
                {
                    "id": str(conv.id),
                    "title": conv.title or "新对话",
                    "context_type": conv.context_type.value if conv.context_type else "workbench",
                    "message_count": 0,  # 可以异步统计
                    "created_at": conv.created_at.isoformat() if conv.created_at else None,
                    "updated_at": conv.updated_at.isoformat() if conv.updated_at else None
                }
                for conv in convs
            ]


# 全局实例
conv_manager = ConversationManager()
rag.py
python
"""
RAG 检索增强生成服务
"""

import json
import hashlib
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from datetime import datetime

from sqlalchemy import text, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.database import AsyncSessionLocal
from app.config import settings
from app.core.ai_digital_base.embeddings import embedding_service
from app.models.knowledge import KnowledgeDocument, KnowledgeVector


class RAGService:
    """RAG 检索服务"""
    
    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
        tenant_id: str,
        context_type: Optional[str] = None,
        source_type: Optional[str] = None,
        top_k: int = 5,
        similarity_threshold: float = 0.7
    ) -> List[str]:
        """
        语义检索相关知识
        返回内容列表（已按相似度排序）
        """
        # 1. 获取查询向量
        query_embedding = await embedding_service.embed(query)
        
        # 2. 构建过滤条件
        filter_conditions = ["tenant_id = :tenant_id"]
        params = {"tenant_id": tenant_id, "embedding": query_embedding, "limit": top_k}
        
        if source_type:
            filter_conditions.append("source_type = :source_type")
            params["source_type"] = source_type
        
        # 3. 向量检索（使用 pgvector 或 Qdrant）
        if settings.VECTOR_DB_TYPE == "pgvector":
            return await self._search_pgvector(filter_conditions, params, similarity_threshold)
        elif settings.VECTOR_DB_TYPE == "qdrant":
            return await self._search_qdrant(query_embedding, tenant_id, filter_conditions, top_k)
        else:
            # 降级：简单关键词匹配
            return await self._search_fallback(query, tenant_id, top_k)
    
    async def _search_pgvector(
        self,
        filter_conditions: List[str],
        params: Dict[str, Any],
        similarity_threshold: float
    ) -> List[str]:
        """使用 pgvector 检索"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            where_clause = " AND ".join(filter_conditions)
            stmt = text(f"""
                SELECT content, 1 - (embedding <=> :embedding) AS similarity
                FROM knowledge_vectors
                WHERE {where_clause}
                  AND (embedding <=> :embedding) < :threshold
                ORDER BY embedding <=> :embedding
                LIMIT :limit
            """)
            
            params["threshold"] = 1 - similarity_threshold
            result = await session.execute(stmt, params)
            rows = result.fetchall()
            
            return [row[0] for row in rows]
    
    async def _search_qdrant(
        self,
        query_embedding: List[float],
        tenant_id: str,
        filter_conditions: List[str],
        top_k: int
    ) -> List[str]:
        """使用 Qdrant 检索"""
        # 简化实现，实际需要安装 qdrant-client
        return []
    
    async def _search_fallback(
        self,
        query: str,
        tenant_id: str,
        top_k: int
    ) -> List[str]:
        """降级检索（关键词匹配）"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            stmt = select(KnowledgeVector.content).where(
                KnowledgeVector.tenant_id == tenant_id,
                KnowledgeVector.content.contains(query[:50])
            ).limit(top_k)
            
            result = await session.execute(stmt)
            return [row[0] for row in result.fetchall()]
    
    async def index_document(
        self,
        content: str,
        metadata: Dict[str, Any],
        tenant_id: str,
        chunk_size: int = 500,
        chunk_overlap: int = 50
    ) -> str:
        """
        索引文档
        自动分块、向量化并存储
        """
        # 1. 分块
        chunks = self._split_text(content, chunk_size, chunk_overlap)
        
        # 2. 创建文档记录
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            doc = KnowledgeDocument(
                tenant_id=tenant_id,
                source_type=metadata.get("source_type", "unknown"),
                source_id=metadata.get("source_id", ""),
                title=metadata.get("title", "Untitled"),
                content=content[:10000],  # 存储前 10000 字符
                summary=metadata.get("summary", ""),
                file_type=metadata.get("file_type"),
                file_url=metadata.get("file_url"),
                chunk_count=len(chunks),
                is_indexed=False,
                metadata=metadata
            )
            session.add(doc)
            await session.flush()
            
            # 3. 批量向量化并存储
            for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks):
                # 生成内容哈希（用于增量更新）
                content_hash = hashlib.sha256(chunk.encode()).hexdigest()
                
                # 检查是否已存在相同哈希的向量（增量更新）
                existing = await session.execute(
                    select(KnowledgeVector).where(
                        KnowledgeVector.document_id == doc.id,
                        KnowledgeVector.content_hash == content_hash
                    )
                )
                if existing.scalar_one_or_none():
                    continue
                
                # 向量化
                embedding = await embedding_service.embed(chunk)
                
                vector = KnowledgeVector(
                    document_id=doc.id,
                    tenant_id=tenant_id,
                    chunk_index=i,
                    content=chunk,
                    content_hash=content_hash,
                    embedding=embedding,
                    metadata={
                        **metadata,
                        "chunk_index": i,
                        "chunk_total": len(chunks)
                    }
                )
                session.add(vector)
            
            doc.is_indexed = True
            doc.indexed_at = datetime.now()
            await session.commit()
            
            return str(doc.id)
    
    def _split_text(
        self, 
        text: str, 
        chunk_size: int = 500, 
        chunk_overlap: int = 50
    ) -> List[str]:
        """简单的文本分块"""
        if len(text) <= chunk_size:
            return [text]
        
        chunks = []
        start = 0
        while start < len(text):
            end = start + chunk_size
            
            # 尽量在句号或换行处断开
            if end < len(text):
                # 寻找最近的断句点
                for sep in ["\n\n", "\n", "。", "！", "？", ".", "!", "?"]:
                    last_sep = text.rfind(sep, start, end)
                    if last_sep > start + chunk_size // 2:
                        end = last_sep + len(sep)
                        break
            
            chunk = text[start:end].strip()
            if chunk:
                chunks.append(chunk)
            
            start = end - chunk_overlap
        
        return chunks
    
    async def delete_document(self, document_id: str):
        """删除文档及其向量"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            doc = await session.get(KnowledgeDocument, document_id)
            if doc:
                doc.is_deleted = True
                # 同时软删除所有向量
                stmt = text("UPDATE knowledge_vectors SET is_deleted = true WHERE document_id = :doc_id")
                await session.execute(stmt, {"doc_id": document_id})
                await session.commit()
    
    async def update_document(self, document_id: str, content: str, metadata: Dict[str, Any] = None):
        """更新文档（删除旧的，重新索引）"""
        await self.delete_document(document_id)
        return await self.index_document(content, metadata or {}, "")
    
    async def get_index_stats(self, tenant_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """获取索引统计"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            # 文档数量
            doc_count = await session.scalar(
                select(KnowledgeDocument).where(
                    KnowledgeDocument.tenant_id == tenant_id,
                    KnowledgeDocument.is_deleted == False
                ).count()
            )
            
            # 向量数量
            vec_count = await session.scalar(
                select(KnowledgeVector).where(
                    KnowledgeVector.tenant_id == tenant_id,
                    KnowledgeVector.is_deleted == False
                ).count()
            )
            
            return {
                "document_count": doc_count,
                "vector_count": vec_count,
                "total_chunks": vec_count
            }


# 全局实例
rag_service = RAGService()
inference.py
python
"""
模型推理服务 - 多后端适配器
"""

import json
import asyncio
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from typing import List, Dict, Any, AsyncIterator, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass

import httpx
from openai import AsyncOpenAI

from app.config import settings


class BaseLLMBackend(ABC):
    """LLM 后端抽象基类"""
    
    @abstractmethod
    async def generate(
        self,
        messages: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        temperature: float = 0.7,
        max_tokens: int = 4096,
        tools: Optional[List[Dict]] = None,
        tool_choice: Optional[str] = None,
        **kwargs
    ) -> str:
        """同步生成（非流式）"""
        pass
    
    @abstractmethod
    async def stream_generate(
        self,
        messages: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        temperature: float = 0.7,
        max_tokens: int = 4096,
        tools: Optional[List[Dict]] = None,
        tool_choice: Optional[str] = None,
        **kwargs
    ) -> AsyncIterator[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """流式生成"""
        pass


class DeepSeekBackend(BaseLLMBackend):
    """DeepSeek API 后端"""
    
    def __init__(self, api_key: str, base_url: str = "https://api.deepseek.com/v1", model_name: str = "deepseek-chat"):
        self.client = AsyncOpenAI(api_key=api_key, base_url=base_url)
        self.model_name = model_name
    
    async def generate(
        self,
        messages: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        temperature: float = 0.7,
        max_tokens: int = 4096,
        tools: Optional[List[Dict]] = None,
        tool_choice: Optional[str] = None,
        **kwargs
    ) -> str:
        response = await self.client.chat.completions.create(
            model=self.model_name,
            messages=messages,
            temperature=temperature,
            max_tokens=max_tokens,
            tools=tools,
            tool_choice=tool_choice,
            **kwargs
        )
        
        if response.choices[0].message.tool_calls:
            # 如果有工具调用，返回工具调用信息
            return json.dumps({
                "tool_calls": [
                    {
                        "id": tc.id,
                        "type": tc.type,
                        "function": {
                            "name": tc.function.name,
                            "arguments": tc.function.arguments
                        }
                    }
                    for tc in response.choices[0].message.tool_calls
                ]
            })
        
        return response.choices[0].message.content or ""
    
    async def stream_generate(
        self,
        messages: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        temperature: float = 0.7,
        max_tokens: int = 4096,
        tools: Optional[List[Dict]] = None,
        tool_choice: Optional[str] = None,
        **kwargs
    ) -> AsyncIterator[Dict[str, Any]]:
        stream = await self.client.chat.completions.create(
            model=self.model_name,
            messages=messages,
            temperature=temperature,
            max_tokens=max_tokens,
            tools=tools,
            tool_choice=tool_choice,
            stream=True,
            **kwargs
        )
        
        # 用于累积工具调用参数
        tool_calls_buffer = {}
        
        async for chunk in stream:
            delta = chunk.choices[0].delta
            
            if delta.content:
                yield {"type": "content", "content": delta.content}
            
            if delta.tool_calls:
                for tc in delta.tool_calls:
                    idx = tc.index
                    if idx not in tool_calls_buffer:
                        tool_calls_buffer[idx] = {
                            "id": tc.id or "",
                            "type": "function",
                            "function": {"name": "", "arguments": ""}
                        }
                    
                    if tc.id:
                        tool_calls_buffer[idx]["id"] = tc.id
                    if tc.function:
                        if tc.function.name:
                            tool_calls_buffer[idx]["function"]["name"] = tc.function.name
                        if tc.function.arguments:
                            tool_calls_buffer[idx]["function"]["arguments"] += tc.function.arguments
            
            if chunk.choices[0].finish_reason:
                # 输出完整的工具调用
                for tc in tool_calls_buffer.values():
                    yield {"type": "tool_call", "tool_call": tc}


class VLLMBackend(BaseLLMBackend):
    """vLLM 本地部署后端"""
    
    def __init__(self, endpoint: str, model_name: str = "default"):
        self.client = AsyncOpenAI(base_url=endpoint, api_key="EMPTY")
        self.model_name = model_name
    
    async def generate(
        self,
        messages: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        temperature: float = 0.7,
        max_tokens: int = 4096,
        tools: Optional[List[Dict]] = None,
        tool_choice: Optional[str] = None,
        **kwargs
    ) -> str:
        response = await self.client.chat.completions.create(
            model=self.model_name,
            messages=messages,
            temperature=temperature,
            max_tokens=max_tokens,
            **kwargs
        )
        return response.choices[0].message.content or ""
    
    async def stream_generate(
        self,
        messages: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        temperature: float = 0.7,
        max_tokens: int = 4096,
        tools: Optional[List[Dict]] = None,
        tool_choice: Optional[str] = None,
        **kwargs
    ) -> AsyncIterator[Dict[str, Any]]:
        stream = await self.client.chat.completions.create(
            model=self.model_name,
            messages=messages,
            temperature=temperature,
            max_tokens=max_tokens,
            stream=True,
            **kwargs
        )
        
        async for chunk in stream:
            if chunk.choices[0].delta.content:
                yield {"type": "content", "content": chunk.choices[0].delta.content}


class OllamaBackend(BaseLLMBackend):
    """Ollama 本地部署后端"""
    
    def __init__(self, base_url: str = "http://localhost:11434", model_name: str = "llama3"):
        self.base_url = base_url
        self.model_name = model_name
        self.client = httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=120.0)
    
    async def generate(
        self,
        messages: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        temperature: float = 0.7,
        max_tokens: int = 4096,
        tools: Optional[List[Dict]] = None,
        tool_choice: Optional[str] = None,
        **kwargs
    ) -> str:
        response = await self.client.post(
            f"{self.base_url}/api/chat",
            json={
                "model": self.model_name,
                "messages": messages,
                "stream": False,
                "options": {
                    "temperature": temperature,
                    "num_predict": max_tokens
                }
            }
        )
        data = response.json()
        return data.get("message", {}).get("content", "")
    
    async def stream_generate(
        self,
        messages: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        temperature: float = 0.7,
        max_tokens: int = 4096,
        tools: Optional[List[Dict]] = None,
        tool_choice: Optional[str] = None,
        **kwargs
    ) -> AsyncIterator[Dict[str, Any]]:
        async with self.client.stream(
            "POST",
            f"{self.base_url}/api/chat",
            json={
                "model": self.model_name,
                "messages": messages,
                "stream": True,
                "options": {
                    "temperature": temperature,
                    "num_predict": max_tokens
                }
            }
        ) as response:
            async for line in response.aiter_lines():
                if line:
                    try:
                        data = json.loads(line)
                        if "message" in data and "content" in data["message"]:
                            yield {"type": "content", "content": data["message"]["content"]}
                    except json.JSONDecodeError:
                        pass


class ModelRegistry:
    """模型注册中心"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self._models: Dict[str, BaseLLMBackend] = {}
        self._default_model: Optional[str] = None
    
    def register(self, name: str, backend: BaseLLMBackend, is_default: bool = False):
        """注册模型"""
        self._models[name] = backend
        if is_default or self._default_model is None:
            self._default_model = name
    
    def get(self, name: Optional[str] = None) -> Optional[BaseLLMBackend]:
        """获取模型后端"""
        if name is None:
            name = self._default_model
        return self._models.get(name)
    
    def list_models(self) -> List[str]:
        """列出所有可用模型"""
        return list(self._models.keys())
    
    def set_default(self, name: str):
        """设置默认模型"""
        if name in self._models:
            self._default_model = name


# 全局实例
model_registry = ModelRegistry()
embeddings.py
python
"""
嵌入向量服务
"""

import asyncio
from typing import List, Union
from functools import lru_cache

from app.config import settings


class EmbeddingService:
    """嵌入向量服务"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self._model = None
        self._model_name = settings.EMBEDDING_MODEL
        self._dimension = settings.EMBEDDING_DIMENSION
    
    def _load_model(self):
        """延迟加载模型"""
        if self._model is None:
            try:
                from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer
                self._model = SentenceTransformer(self._model_name)
            except ImportError:
                # 降级：使用简单的哈希向量（仅用于开发测试）
                self._model = "fallback"
    
    async def embed(self, text: Union[str, List[str]]) -> List[float]:
        """
        将文本转换为嵌入向量
        支持单个文本或批量文本
        """
        self._load_model()
        
        if self._model == "fallback":
            # 降级方案：使用简单的哈希向量
            return await self._fallback_embed(text)
        
        # 在线程池中运行同步的 SentenceTransformer
        loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
        
        if isinstance(text, str):
            embeddings = await loop.run_in_executor(
                None, 
                lambda: self._model.encode([text], normalize_embeddings=True)
            )
            return embeddings[0].tolist()
        else:
            embeddings = await loop.run_in_executor(
                None,
                lambda: self._model.encode(text, normalize_embeddings=True)
            )
            return [emb.tolist() for emb in embeddings]
    
    async def embed_batch(self, texts: List[str], batch_size: int = 32) -> List[List[float]]:
        """批量嵌入"""
        return await self.embed(texts)
    
    async def _fallback_embed(self, text: Union[str, List[str]]) -> List[float]:
        """
        降级方案：使用文本哈希生成伪向量
        仅用于开发测试，生产环境应使用真实的嵌入模型
        """
        import hashlib
        
        if isinstance(text, str):
            text = [text]
        
        results = []
        for t in text:
            # 使用 SHA256 生成哈希
            hash_obj = hashlib.sha256(t.encode('utf-8'))
            hash_bytes = hash_obj.digest()
            
            # 将哈希值转换为浮点数列表
            vector = []
            for i in range(min(self._dimension, len(hash_bytes) * 8)):
                byte_idx = i // 8
                bit_idx = i % 8
                if byte_idx < len(hash_bytes):
                    value = (hash_bytes[byte_idx] >> bit_idx) & 1
                    vector.append(float(value) * 2 - 1)  # 映射到 [-1, 1]
                else:
                    vector.append(0.0)
            
            # 填充到目标维度
            while len(vector) < self._dimension:
                vector.append(0.0)
            
            results.append(vector)
        
        if len(results) == 1:
            return results[0]
        return results
    
    def get_dimension(self) -> int:
        """获取向量维度"""
        return self._dimension


# 全局实例
embedding_service = EmbeddingService()

"""
智能体引擎核心模块
提供多智能体协作编排、任务规划、记忆管理和工具调用能力
"""

from .orchestrator import AgentOrchestrator, get_agent_orchestrator, init_agent_engine
from .planner import PlannerAgent, TaskPlan, Task
from .memory import MemoryManager, ConversationMemory, LongTermMemory
from .bus import AgentBus, MessageType, AgentMessage
from .tools import ToolRegistry, Tool, tool_registry, register_all_tools

__all__ = [
    "AgentOrchestrator",
    "get_agent_orchestrator",
    "init_agent_engine",
    "PlannerAgent",
    "TaskPlan",
    "Task",
    "MemoryManager",
    "ConversationMemory",
    "LongTermMemory",
    "AgentBus",
    "MessageType",
    "AgentMessage",
    "ToolRegistry",
    "Tool",
    "tool_registry",
    "register_all_tools",
]
orchestrator.py
python
"""
智能体编排器 - 任务调度与多智能体协作
"""

import asyncio
import uuid
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional, Set
from enum import Enum
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime
import logging

from app.core.ai_digital_base.inference import model_registry
from app.core.agent_engine.planner import PlannerAgent, TaskPlan, Task, TaskStatus
from app.core.agent_engine.memory import MemoryManager
from app.core.agent_engine.bus import AgentBus, MessageType
from app.core.agent_engine.tools.registry import ToolRegistry
from app.config import settings

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class OrchestrationMode(str, Enum):
    """编排模式"""
    SINGLE = "single"       # 单智能体
    CHAIN = "chain"         # 链式协作
    PARALLEL = "parallel"   # 并行协作
    VOTE = "vote"           # 投票决策
    LOOP = "loop"           # 循环迭代


@dataclass
class ExecutionContext:
    """执行上下文"""
    tenant_id: str
    user_id: str
    conversation_id: Optional[str] = None
    session_data: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    trace_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))


class AgentOrchestrator:
    """智能体编排器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.planner = PlannerAgent()
        self.memory = MemoryManager()
        self.bus = AgentBus()
        self.tool_registry: Optional[ToolRegistry] = None
        self._executing_tasks: Dict[str, asyncio.Task] = {}
    
    def set_tool_registry(self, registry: ToolRegistry):
        """设置工具注册中心"""
        self.tool_registry = registry
        self.planner.set_tool_registry(registry)
    
    async def execute(
        self,
        user_request: str,
        context: Dict[str, Any],
        mode: Optional[OrchestrationMode] = None,
        max_steps: int = 10
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        执行用户请求
        """
        exec_ctx = ExecutionContext(
            tenant_id=context.get("tenant_id", "default"),
            user_id=context.get("user_id", "system"),
            conversation_id=context.get("conversation_id"),
            session_data=context.get("session_data", {})
        )
        
        logger.info(f"[Trace:{exec_ctx.trace_id}] 开始执行: {user_request[:100]}")
        
        # 1. 规划任务
        plan = await self.planner.plan(user_request, exec_ctx, mode)
        logger.info(f"[Trace:{exec_ctx.trace_id}] 规划完成: {plan.mode}, {len(plan.tasks)} 个任务")
        
        # 2. 执行任务
        results = await self._execute_plan(plan, exec_ctx, max_steps)
        
        # 3. 汇总结果
        final_result = await self._aggregate_results(plan, results, user_request, exec_ctx)
        
        # 4. 存储记忆
        await self.memory.store_execution(
            user_request=user_request,
            plan=plan,
            results=results,
            final_result=final_result,
            context=exec_ctx
        )
        
        # 5. 广播完成事件
        await self.bus.publish(
            topic="orchestrator.execution.completed",
            message={
                "trace_id": exec_ctx.trace_id,
                "user_request": user_request,
                "result": final_result
            }
        )
        
        logger.info(f"[Trace:{exec_ctx.trace_id}] 执行完成")
        return final_result
    
    async def _execute_plan(
        self,
        plan: TaskPlan,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        max_steps: int
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """执行任务计划"""
        results = {}
        
        if plan.mode == OrchestrationMode.SINGLE:
            # 单任务执行
            task = plan.tasks[0]
            results[task.id] = await self._execute_task(task, context)
            
        elif plan.mode == OrchestrationMode.CHAIN:
            # 链式执行
            for task in plan.tasks:
                # 注入前置任务结果
                for dep in task.dependencies:
                    if dep in results:
                        task.input_data[f"_prev_{dep}"] = results[dep]
                results[task.id] = await self._execute_task(task, context)
                
        elif plan.mode == OrchestrationMode.PARALLEL:
            # 并行执行
            tasks_coros = [self._execute_task(t, context) for t in plan.tasks]
            parallel_results = await asyncio.gather(*tasks_coros, return_exceptions=True)
            for task, res in zip(plan.tasks, parallel_results):
                if isinstance(res, Exception):
                    results[task.id] = {"error": str(res)}
                else:
                    results[task.id] = res
                    
        elif plan.mode == OrchestrationMode.VOTE:
            # 投票模式：多个智能体并行执行，投票决定最终结果
            tasks_coros = [self._execute_task(t, context) for t in plan.tasks]
            parallel_results = await asyncio.gather(*tasks_coros, return_exceptions=True)
            for task, res in zip(plan.tasks, parallel_results):
                if isinstance(res, Exception):
                    results[task.id] = {"error": str(res)}
                else:
                    results[task.id] = res
            results["_vote_result"] = await self._vote_aggregate(parallel_results)
            
        elif plan.mode == OrchestrationMode.LOOP:
            # 循环执行（ReAct 模式）
            step = 0
            current_task = plan.tasks[0]
            while step < max_steps:
                result = await self._execute_task(current_task, context)
                results[f"{current_task.id}_step_{step}"] = result
                
                # 检查是否需要继续
                if self._should_stop_loop(result, context):
                    break
                
                # 更新任务输入
                current_task.input_data["_prev_result"] = result
                step += 1
        
        return results
    
    async def _execute_task(self, task: Task, context: ExecutionContext) -> Any:
        """执行单个任务"""
        logger.info(f"[Trace:{context.trace_id}] 执行任务: {task.agent_type}.{task.id}")
        
        try:
            if task.agent_type == "tool":
                return await self._execute_tool_task(task, context)
            elif task.agent_type == "llm":
                return await self._execute_llm_task(task, context)
            elif task.agent_type == "agent":
                return await self._execute_agent_task(task, context)
            else:
                raise ValueError(f"Unknown agent type: {task.agent_type}")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"[Trace:{context.trace_id}] 任务执行失败: {e}")
            return {"error": str(e), "task_id": task.id}
    
    async def _execute_tool_task(self, task: Task, context: ExecutionContext) -> Any:
        """执行工具任务"""
        tool_name = task.input_data.get("tool_name")
        if not tool_name:
            raise ValueError("Tool task missing tool_name")
        
        arguments = task.input_data.get("arguments", {})
        
        # 注入上下文
        arguments["_context"] = {
            "tenant_id": context.tenant_id,
            "user_id": context.user_id,
            "conversation_id": context.conversation_id
        }
        
        return await self.tool_registry.execute(tool_name, arguments, context.session_data)
    
    async def _execute_llm_task(self, task: Task, context: ExecutionContext) -> str:
        """执行 LLM 任务"""
        messages = task.input_data.get("messages", [])
        model_name = task.input_data.get("model", settings.DEFAULT_LLM_MODEL)
        temperature = task.input_data.get("temperature", 0.7)
        max_tokens = task.input_data.get("max_tokens", 4096)
        
        backend = model_registry.get(model_name)
        if not backend:
            raise ValueError(f"Model not found: {model_name}")
        
        return await backend.generate(
            messages=messages,
            temperature=temperature,
            max_tokens=max_tokens
        )
    
    async def _execute_agent_task(self, task: Task, context: ExecutionContext) -> Any:
        """执行子智能体任务（递归调用）"""
        # 子智能体可以有不同的工具集或规划策略
        sub_request = task.input_data.get("request", "")
        sub_mode = task.input_data.get("mode", OrchestrationMode.SINGLE)
        
        return await self.execute(
            user_request=sub_request,
            context={
                "tenant_id": context.tenant_id,
                "user_id": context.user_id,
                "conversation_id": context.conversation_id,
                "session_data": task.input_data.get("session_data", {})
            },
            mode=sub_mode
        )
    
    async def _aggregate_results(
        self,
        plan: TaskPlan,
        results: Dict[str, Any],
        user_request: str,
        context: ExecutionContext
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """汇总执行结果"""
        # 使用 LLM 生成最终回答
        summary_prompt = f"""
用户请求: {user_request}

执行计划: {plan.mode}
任务结果: {results}

请基于以上信息，生成一个清晰、完整的回答。
"""
        backend = model_registry.get()
        if backend:
            summary = await backend.generate([{"role": "user", "content": summary_prompt}])
        else:
            summary = f"执行完成，共 {len(plan.tasks)} 个任务"
        
        return {
            "summary": summary,
            "plan_mode": plan.mode.value,
            "task_count": len(plan.tasks),
            "results": results,
            "trace_id": context.trace_id
        }
    
    async def _vote_aggregate(self, results: List[Any]) -> Any:
        """投票聚合"""
        # 简单多数投票
        from collections import Counter
        str_results = [str(r) for r in results if not isinstance(r, Exception)]
        if not str_results:
            return None
        counter = Counter(str_results)
        return counter.most_common(1)[0][0]
    
    def _should_stop_loop(self, result: Any, context: ExecutionContext) -> bool:
        """判断是否停止循环"""
        if isinstance(result, dict):
            if result.get("done", False):
                return True
            if result.get("error"):
                return True
        return False
    
    async def stream_execute(
        self,
        user_request: str,
        context: Dict[str, Any]
    ) -> AsyncIterator[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """流式执行（用于实时反馈）"""
        exec_ctx = ExecutionContext(
            tenant_id=context.get("tenant_id", "default"),
            user_id=context.get("user_id", "system"),
            conversation_id=context.get("conversation_id")
        )
        
        yield {"type": "start", "trace_id": exec_ctx.trace_id}
        
        # 规划
        plan = await self.planner.plan(user_request, exec_ctx)
        yield {"type": "plan", "mode": plan.mode.value, "tasks": len(plan.tasks)}
        
        # 执行
        for task in plan.tasks:
            yield {"type": "task_start", "task_id": task.id, "agent_type": task.agent_type}
            result = await self._execute_task(task, exec_ctx)
            yield {"type": "task_complete", "task_id": task.id, "result": result}
        
        # 汇总
        final = await self._aggregate_results(plan, {t.id: f"result_{i}" for i, t in enumerate(plan.tasks)}, user_request, exec_ctx)
        yield {"type": "complete", "result": final}


# 全局实例
_agent_orchestrator: Optional[AgentOrchestrator] = None


async def init_agent_engine() -> AgentOrchestrator:
    """初始化智能体引擎"""
    global _agent_orchestrator
    _agent_orchestrator = AgentOrchestrator()
    
    # 注册工具
    from app.core.agent_engine.tools import tool_registry, register_all_tools
    register_all_tools(tool_registry)
    _agent_orchestrator.set_tool_registry(tool_registry)
    
    # 初始化记忆
    await _agent_orchestrator.memory.initialize()
    
    # 初始化消息总线
    await _agent_orchestrator.bus.initialize()
    
    logger.info("智能体引擎初始化完成")
    return _agent_orchestrator


def get_agent_orchestrator() -> AgentOrchestrator:
    """获取全局编排器实例"""
    if _agent_orchestrator is None:
        raise RuntimeError("Agent engine not initialized. Call init_agent_engine() first.")
    return _agent_orchestrator
planner.py
python
"""
任务规划器 - 将自然语言请求拆解为可执行任务
"""

import json
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from enum import Enum
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import uuid

from app.core.ai_digital_base.inference import model_registry
from app.core.agent_engine.orchestrator import OrchestrationMode
from app.config import settings

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class TaskStatus(str, Enum):
    PENDING = "pending"
    RUNNING = "running"
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    FAILED = "failed"


@dataclass
class Task:
    """任务定义"""
    id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    agent_type: str = "llm"  # tool, llm, agent
    input_data: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    dependencies: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    description: str = ""
    status: TaskStatus = TaskStatus.PENDING
    result: Any = None


@dataclass
class TaskPlan:
    """任务计划"""
    mode: OrchestrationMode
    tasks: List[Task]
    reasoning: str = ""
    confidence: float = 1.0


class PlannerAgent:
    """任务规划器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.tool_registry = None
        self._planning_prompt_template = """
你是一个任务规划专家。根据用户请求，将其拆解为可执行的任务序列。

## 可用工具
{tools_description}

## 编排模式说明
- single: 单个任务，直接执行
- chain: 链式任务，前一个任务的输出作为后一个的输入
- parallel: 并行任务，多个任务同时执行
- vote: 投票模式，多个智能体并行，投票决定结果

## 用户请求
{user_request}

## 上下文
{context}

请以 JSON 格式输出计划，格式如下：
{{
    "mode": "single|chain|parallel|vote",
    "reasoning": "规划推理过程",
    "tasks": [
        {{
            "agent_type": "tool|llm|agent",
            "input_data": {{
                "tool_name": "工具名称（仅 tool 类型需要）",
                "arguments": {{}},
                "messages": []（仅 llm 类型需要）
            }},
            "dependencies": [],
            "description": "任务描述"
        }}
    ]
}}

只输出 JSON，不要包含其他内容。
"""
    
    def set_tool_registry(self, registry):
        self.tool_registry = registry
    
    async def plan(
        self,
        user_request: str,
        context: Any,
        mode_hint: Optional[OrchestrationMode] = None
    ) -> TaskPlan:
        """生成任务计划"""
        # 获取可用工具描述
        tools_description = self._get_tools_description()
        
        # 构建提示
        prompt = self._planning_prompt_template.format(
            tools_description=tools_description,
            user_request=user_request,
            context=f"租户: {context.tenant_id}, 用户: {context.user_id}"
        )
        
        # 调用 LLM 规划
        backend = model_registry.get()
        if not backend:
            # 降级：返回简单计划
            return self._fallback_plan(user_request)
        
        response = await backend.generate(
            messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
            temperature=0.1,
            max_tokens=2000
        )
        
        # 解析 JSON
        try:
            # 提取 JSON 部分
            json_str = self._extract_json(response)
            data = json.loads(json_str)
            
            mode = OrchestrationMode(data.get("mode", "single"))
            if mode_hint:
                mode = mode_hint
            
            tasks = []
            for t in data.get("tasks", []):
                task = Task(
                    agent_type=t.get("agent_type", "llm"),
                    input_data=t.get("input_data", {}),
                    dependencies=t.get("dependencies", []),
                    description=t.get("description", "")
                )
                tasks.append(task)
            
            return TaskPlan(
                mode=mode,
                tasks=tasks,
                reasoning=data.get("reasoning", ""),
                confidence=0.9
            )
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to parse plan: {e}")
            return self._fallback_plan(user_request)
    
    def _get_tools_description(self) -> str:
        """获取工具描述"""
        if self.tool_registry:
            tools = self.tool_registry.list_tools()
            descriptions = []
            for t in tools:
                descriptions.append(f"- {t['name']}: {t['description']}")
            return "\n".join(descriptions)
        return "暂无可用工具"
    
    def _extract_json(self, text: str) -> str:
        """从文本中提取 JSON"""
        # 查找 JSON 块
        import re
        # 尝试匹配 ```json ... ```
        match = re.search(r'```json\s*([\s\S]*?)\s*```', text)
        if match:
            return match.group(1)
        # 尝试匹配 { ... }
        match = re.search(r'\{[\s\S]*\}', text)
        if match:
            return match.group(0)
        return text
    
    def _fallback_plan(self, user_request: str) -> TaskPlan:
        """降级计划"""
        return TaskPlan(
            mode=OrchestrationMode.SINGLE,
            tasks=[
                Task(
                    agent_type="llm",
                    input_data={
                        "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": user_request}]
                    },
                    description="直接回答用户问题"
                )
            ],
            reasoning="降级模式：直接调用 LLM 回答"
        )
memory.py
python
"""
记忆管理器 - 短期/长期记忆
"""

import json
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from collections import OrderedDict
import asyncio

from app.database import AsyncSessionLocal
from app.models.ai_conversation import AIConversation, AIMessage
from app.core.ai_digital_base.embeddings import embedding_service
from app.core.ai_digital_base.rag import rag_service

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class ConversationMemory:
    """会话短期记忆（LRU 缓存）"""
    
    def __init__(self, max_size: int = 100):
        self.max_size = max_size
        self._cache: OrderedDict[str, List[Dict]] = OrderedDict()
    
    def add(self, conversation_id: str, messages: List[Dict]):
        if conversation_id in self._cache:
            self._cache.move_to_end(conversation_id)
        self._cache[conversation_id] = messages
        if len(self._cache) > self.max_size:
            self._cache.popitem(last=False)
    
    def get(self, conversation_id: str) -> Optional[List[Dict]]:
        if conversation_id in self._cache:
            self._cache.move_to_end(conversation_id)
            return self._cache[conversation_id]
        return None
    
    def clear(self, conversation_id: str = None):
        if conversation_id:
            self._cache.pop(conversation_id, None)
        else:
            self._cache.clear()


class LongTermMemory:
    """长期记忆（基于向量检索）"""
    
    async def store(
        self,
        content: str,
        metadata: Dict[str, Any],
        tenant_id: str
    ) -> str:
        """存储长期记忆"""
        return await rag_service.index_document(
            content=content,
            metadata={
                "source_type": "long_term_memory",
                "memory_type": metadata.get("type", "general"),
                **metadata
            },
            tenant_id=tenant_id,
            chunk_size=1000
        )
    
    async def recall(
        self,
        query: str,
        tenant_id: str,
        limit: int = 5
    ) -> List[str]:
        """回忆相关记忆"""
        return await rag_service.search(
            query=query,
            tenant_id=tenant_id,
            source_type="long_term_memory",
            top_k=limit
        )


class MemoryManager:
    """记忆管理器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.conversation_memory = ConversationMemory()
        self.long_term_memory = LongTermMemory()
        self._execution_history: List[Dict] = []  # 最近执行历史
    
    async def initialize(self):
        """初始化"""
        pass
    
    async def get_conversation_context(
        self,
        conversation_id: str,
        max_messages: int = 20
    ) -> List[Dict]:
        """获取会话上下文"""
        # 先查缓存
        cached = self.conversation_memory.get(conversation_id)
        if cached:
            return cached[-max_messages:]
        
        # 查数据库
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            from sqlalchemy import select, desc
            stmt = select(AIMessage).where(
                AIMessage.conversation_id == conversation_id
            ).order_by(AIMessage.created_at).limit(max_messages)
            
            result = await session.execute(stmt)
            messages = result.scalars().all()
            
            context = [
                {"role": msg.role, "content": msg.content}
                for msg in messages
            ]
            self.conversation_memory.add(conversation_id, context)
            return context
    
    async def store_execution(
        self,
        user_request: str,
        plan: Any,
        results: Dict[str, Any],
        final_result: Dict[str, Any],
        context: Any
    ):
        """存储执行记录"""
        record = {
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "user_request": user_request,
            "plan_mode": plan.mode.value if hasattr(plan, 'mode') else str(plan.mode),
            "task_count": len(plan.tasks) if hasattr(plan, 'tasks') else 0,
            "success": "error" not in str(results),
            "trace_id": context.trace_id
        }
        self._execution_history.append(record)
        if len(self._execution_history) > 100:
            self._execution_history = self._execution_history[-100:]
        
        # 可选：存储为长期记忆
        if record["success"]:
            await self.long_term_memory.store(
                content=f"用户请求: {user_request}\n结果: {final_result.get('summary', '')}",
                metadata={"type": "execution", "trace_id": context.trace_id},
                tenant_id=context.tenant_id
            )
    
    async def recall_relevant_executions(
        self,
        query: str,
        tenant_id: str,
        limit: int = 3
    ) -> List[str]:
        """回忆相关执行记录"""
        return await self.long_term_memory.recall(query, tenant_id, limit)
bus.py
python
"""
多智能体总线 - 消息通信与事件广播
"""

import asyncio
from typing import Dict, List, Callable, Any, Set
from enum import Enum
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
import uuid
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class MessageType(str, Enum):
    """消息类型"""
    TASK_START = "task.start"
    TASK_COMPLETE = "task.complete"
    TASK_FAILED = "task.failed"
    AGENT_REGISTER = "agent.register"
    AGENT_UNREGISTER = "agent.unregister"
    TOOL_CALL = "tool.call"
    TOOL_RESULT = "tool.result"
    MEMORY_STORE = "memory.store"
    MEMORY_RECALL = "memory.recall"
    ERROR = "error"


@dataclass
class AgentMessage:
    """智能体消息"""
    id: str
    type: MessageType
    sender: str
    content: Any
    timestamp: datetime
    reply_to: str = None
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = None


class AgentBus:
    """智能体消息总线"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self._subscribers: Dict[str, List[Callable]] = {}
        self._agents: Dict[str, Dict] = {}
        self._message_queue: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue()
        self._running = False
        self._processor_task: asyncio.Task = None
    
    async def initialize(self):
        """初始化总线"""
        self._running = True
        self._processor_task = asyncio.create_task(self._process_messages())
        logger.info("AgentBus initialized")
    
    async def shutdown(self):
        """关闭总线"""
        self._running = False
        if self._processor_task:
            self._processor_task.cancel()
        logger.info("AgentBus shutdown")
    
    def subscribe(self, topic: str, callback: Callable):
        """订阅主题"""
        if topic not in self._subscribers:
            self._subscribers[topic] = []
        self._subscribers[topic].append(callback)
        logger.debug(f"Subscribed to topic: {topic}")
    
    def unsubscribe(self, topic: str, callback: Callable):
        """取消订阅"""
        if topic in self._subscribers:
            self._subscribers[topic].remove(callback)
    
    async def publish(self, topic: str, message: Any, sender: str = "system"):
        """发布消息"""
        msg = AgentMessage(
            id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
            type=MessageType(topic.split(".")[-1]) if topic in MessageType.__members__ else MessageType.ERROR,
            sender=sender,
            content=message,
            timestamp=datetime.now(),
            metadata={"topic": topic}
        )
        await self._message_queue.put(msg)
    
    async def _process_messages(self):
        """处理消息队列"""
        while self._running:
            try:
                msg = await asyncio.wait_for(self._message_queue.get(), timeout=0.1)
                await self._dispatch(msg)
            except asyncio.TimeoutError:
                continue
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error processing message: {e}")
    
    async def _dispatch(self, msg: AgentMessage):
        """分发消息"""
        topic = msg.metadata.get("topic", "")
        
        # 精确匹配
        if topic in self._subscribers:
            for callback in self._subscribers[topic]:
                try:
                    if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(callback):
                        await callback(msg)
                    else:
                        callback(msg)
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.error(f"Error in subscriber callback: {e}")
        
        # 通配符匹配
        for sub_topic, callbacks in self._subscribers.items():
            if sub_topic.endswith("*") and topic.startswith(sub_topic[:-1]):
                for callback in callbacks:
                    try:
                        if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(callback):
                            await callback(msg)
                        else:
                            callback(msg)
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.error(f"Error in subscriber callback: {e}")
    
    def register_agent(self, agent_id: str, capabilities: List[str]):
        """注册智能体"""
        self._agents[agent_id] = {
            "id": agent_id,
            "capabilities": capabilities,
            "registered_at": datetime.now(),
            "status": "active"
        }
        asyncio.create_task(self.publish(
            "agent.register",
            {"agent_id": agent_id, "capabilities": capabilities},
            sender=agent_id
        ))
    
    def unregister_agent(self, agent_id: str):
        """注销智能体"""
        if agent_id in self._agents:
            del self._agents[agent_id]
            asyncio.create_task(self.publish(
                "agent.unregister",
                {"agent_id": agent_id},
                sender=agent_id
            ))
    
    def get_agents_by_capability(self, capability: str) -> List[str]:
        """根据能力查找智能体"""
        return [
            agent_id for agent_id, info in self._agents.items()
            if capability in info["capabilities"]
        ]

    register_template_tools(registry)
    register_query_tools(registry)
    register_workflow_tools(registry)
    register_integration_tools(registry)
    register_system_tools(registry)

tools/registry.py
python
"""
工具注册中心
"""

import inspect
from typing import Dict, Callable, Any, Optional, List
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@dataclass
class Tool:
    """工具定义"""
    name: str
    description: str
    parameters: Dict[str, Any]  # JSON Schema
    func: Callable
    require_confirm: bool = False
    risk_level: str = "low"  # low, medium, high
    context_filter: Optional[str] = None  # 限定上下文类型
    tags: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)


class ToolRegistry:
    """工具注册中心"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self._tools: Dict[str, Tool] = {}
    
    def register(self, tool: Tool):
        """注册工具"""
        self._tools[tool.name] = tool
        logger.info(f"Registered tool: {tool.name}")
    
    def unregister(self, name: str):
        """注销工具"""
        if name in self._tools:
            del self._tools[name]
    
    def get(self, name: str) -> Optional[Tool]:
        """获取工具"""
        return self._tools.get(name)
    
    def list_tools(self, context_type: Optional[str] = None) -> List[Dict]:
        """列出工具"""
        tools = []
        for tool in self._tools.values():
            if context_type and tool.context_filter and tool.context_filter != context_type:
                continue
            tools.append({
                "name": tool.name,
                "description": tool.description,
                "parameters": tool.parameters,
                "require_confirm": tool.require_confirm,
                "risk_level": tool.risk_level,
                "tags": tool.tags
            })
        return tools
    
    def get_openai_schema(self, filter_by_context: Optional[str] = None) -> List[Dict]:
        """获取 OpenAI Function Calling 格式的工具定义"""
        tools = []
        for tool in self._tools.values():
            if filter_by_context and tool.context_filter and tool.context_filter != filter_by_context:
                continue
            tools.append({
                "type": "function",
                "function": {
                    "name": tool.name,
                    "description": tool.description,
                    "parameters": tool.parameters
                }
            })
        return tools
    
    async def execute(self, name: str, arguments: Dict, context: Dict = None) -> Any:
        """执行工具"""
        tool = self.get(name)
        if not tool:
            raise ValueError(f"Tool not found: {name}")
        
        # 注入上下文
        if context:
            arguments["_context"] = context
        
        # 执行
        if inspect.iscoroutinefunction(tool.func):
            return await tool.func(arguments)
        else:
            return tool.func(arguments)


# 全局实例
tool_registry = ToolRegistry()

"""
模板设计工具
"""

from typing import Dict, Any
from app.core.agent_engine.tools.registry import Tool, ToolRegistry
from app.services.template_service import TemplateService


async def create_template_tool(arguments: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """创建模板"""
    description = arguments.get("description", "")
    name = arguments.get("name", "新模板")
    context = arguments.get("_context", {})
    
    service = TemplateService()
    template = await service.create_from_description(
        description=description,
        name=name,
        tenant_id=context.get("tenant_id"),
        user_id=context.get("user_id")
    )
    
    return {
        "template_id": str(template.id),
        "name": template.name,
        "code": template.code,
        "message": f"成功创建模板: {template.name}"
    }


async def add_field_tool(arguments: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """添加字段"""
    template_id = arguments.get("template_id")
    field_name = arguments.get("field_name")
    field_type = arguments.get("field_type", "string")
    is_required = arguments.get("is_required", False)
    context = arguments.get("_context", {})
    
    service = TemplateService()
    field = await service.add_field(
        template_id=template_id,
        name=field_name,
        field_type=field_type,
        is_required=is_required,
        tenant_id=context.get("tenant_id")
    )
    
    return {
        "field_id": str(field.id),
        "name": field.name,
        "type": field.field_type.value if hasattr(field.field_type, 'value') else str(field.field_type)
    }


async def suggest_fields_tool(arguments: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """智能推荐字段"""
    template_type = arguments.get("template_type", "通用")
    existing_fields = arguments.get("existing_fields", [])
    
    # 基于模板类型的字段推荐
    field_suggestions = {
        "客户管理": ["客户名称", "联系电话", "所属行业", "客户来源", "客户等级", "联系人", "跟进记录"],
        "采购管理": ["供应商", "采购单号", "采购日期", "物料编码", "物料名称", "采购数量", "单价", "金额"],
        "销售管理": ["客户", "销售单号", "销售日期", "产品编码", "产品名称", "销售数量", "单价", "金额"],
        "库存管理": ["仓库", "物料编码", "物料名称", "库存数量", "安全库存", "单位", "库位"],
        "人力资源": ["员工姓名", "工号", "部门", "职位", "入职日期", "联系电话", "紧急联系人"],
        "项目管理": ["项目名称", "项目编号", "负责人", "开始日期", "结束日期", "状态", "预算"],
    }
    
    suggestions = field_suggestions.get(template_type, ["名称", "编号", "创建时间", "状态", "备注"])
    # 过滤已存在的字段
    suggestions = [f for f in suggestions if f not in existing_fields]
    
    return {
        "suggestions": suggestions,
        "template_type": template_type
    }


def register_template_tools(registry: ToolRegistry):
    """注册模板设计工具"""
    
    registry.register(Tool(
        name="create_template",
        description="根据自然语言描述创建一个新的业务模板",
        parameters={
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "description": {"type": "string", "description": "模板描述，如'创建一个客户管理系统'"},
                "name": {"type": "string", "description": "模板名称"}
            },
            "required": ["description"]
        },
        func=create_template_tool,
        require_confirm=True,
        risk_level="low",
        context_filter="design",
        tags=["template", "create"]
    ))
    
    registry.register(Tool(
        name="add_form_field",
        description="向指定模板添加表单字段",
        parameters={
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "template_id": {"type": "string", "description": "模板ID"},
                "field_name": {"type": "string", "description": "字段名称"},
                "field_type": {"type": "string", "enum": ["string", "integer", "decimal", "date", "datetime", "boolean", "text", "dict"], "description": "字段类型"},
                "is_required": {"type": "boolean", "description": "是否必填"}
            },
            "required": ["template_id", "field_name"]
        },
        func=add_field_tool,
        require_confirm=False,
        context_filter="design",
        tags=["template", "field"]
    ))
    
    registry.register(Tool(
        name="suggest_template_fields",
        description="根据模板类型智能推荐字段",
        parameters={
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "template_type": {"type": "string", "description": "模板类型，如客户管理、采购管理"},
                "existing_fields": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}, "description": "已存在的字段列表"}
            },
            "required": ["template_type"]
        },
        func=suggest_fields_tool,
        require_confirm=False,
        context_filter="design",
        tags=["template", "suggestion"]
    ))

"""
数据查询工具
"""

from typing import Dict, Any, List
from app.core.agent_engine.tools.registry import Tool, ToolRegistry
from app.services.dashboard_service import DashboardService


async def query_data_tool(arguments: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """执行自然语言数据查询"""
    query = arguments.get("query", "")
    context = arguments.get("_context", {})
    
    service = DashboardService()
    result = await service.execute_natural_query(
        query=query,
        tenant_id=context.get("tenant_id"),
        user_id=context.get("user_id")
    )
    
    return {
        "data": result.data[:100] if result.data else [],  # 限制返回行数
        "total_count": result.total_count if hasattr(result, 'total_count') else len(result.data) if result.data else 0,
        "chart_type": result.chart_type,
        "sql": result.sql if hasattr(result, 'sql') else None,
        "summary": result.summary
    }


async def generate_chart_tool(arguments: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """生成图表配置"""
    data_description = arguments.get("data_description", "")
    chart_type = arguments.get("chart_type", "auto")
    
    # 根据数据特征推荐图表类型
    chart_recommendations = {
        "趋势": "line",
        "对比": "bar",
        "占比": "pie",
        "分布": "scatter",
        "进度": "gauge"
    }
    
    recommended = "bar"
    for keyword, ctype in chart_recommendations.items():
        if keyword in data_description:
            recommended = ctype
            break
    
    return {
        "chart_type": chart_type if chart_type != "auto" else recommended,
        "config": {
            "title": {"text": "数据图表"},
            "xAxis": {"type": "category"},
            "yAxis": {"type": "value"},
            "series": [{"type": recommended, "data": []}]
        }
    }


async def analyze_trend_tool(arguments: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """分析数据趋势"""
    metric = arguments.get("metric", "")
    time_range = arguments.get("time_range", "最近30天")
    context = arguments.get("_context", {})
    
    service = DashboardService()
    analysis = await service.analyze_trend(
        metric=metric,
        time_range=time_range,
        tenant_id=context.get("tenant_id")
    )
    
    return {
        "trend": analysis.trend,  # up, down, stable
        "change_percent": analysis.change_percent,
        "anomalies": analysis.anomalies,
        "forecast": analysis.forecast,
        "insights": analysis.insights
    }


def register_query_tools(registry: ToolRegistry):
    """注册数据查询工具"""
    
    registry.register(Tool(
        name="query_data",
        description="使用自然语言查询业务数据，返回表格和图表",

            "type": "object",

        },

        require_confirm=False,
        context_filter="query",

    ))
    
    registry.register(Tool(
        name="generate_chart",

        parameters={
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "data_description": {"type": "string", "description": "数据描述"},
                "chart_type": {"type": "string", "enum": ["line", "bar", "pie", "scatter", "auto"], "description": "图表类型"}
            },
            "required": ["data_description"]
        },
        func=generate_chart_tool,
        require_confirm=False,
        context_filter="query",
        tags=["data", "visualization"]
    ))
    
    registry.register(Tool(
        name="analyze_trend",
        description="分析指标趋势，检测异常和预测",
        parameters={
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "metric": {"type": "string", "description": "要分析的指标，如销售额"},
                "time_range": {"type": "string", "description": "时间范围，如最近30天"}

            "required": ["metric"]
        },
        func=analyze_trend_tool,
        require_confirm=False,
        context_filter="query",
        tags=["data", "analytics", "forecast"]
    ))

"""
流程审批工具
