from typing import Dict, Any, List
from app.core.agent_engine.tools.registry import Tool, ToolRegistry
from app.services.workflow_service import WorkflowService


async def start_workflow_tool(arguments: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """发起流程"""
    workflow_code = arguments.get("workflow_code")
    form_data = arguments.get("form_data", {})
    context = arguments.get("_context", {})
    
    service = WorkflowService()
    instance = await service.start_process(
        workflow_code=workflow_code,
        form_data=form_data,
        initiator_id=context.get("user_id"),
        tenant_id=context.get("tenant_id")
    )
    
    return {
        "process_instance_id": str(instance.id),
        "process_no": instance.process_no,
        "status": instance.status.value if hasattr(instance.status, 'value') else str(instance.status),
        "message": f"流程已发起，编号: {instance.process_no}"
    }


async def get_pending_tasks_tool(arguments: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """获取待办任务"""
    context = arguments.get("_context", {})
    limit = arguments.get("limit", 10)
    
    service = WorkflowService()
    tasks = await service.get_pending_tasks(
        user_id=context.get("user_id"),
        tenant_id=context.get("tenant_id"),
        limit=limit
    )
    
    return {
        "total": len(tasks),
        "tasks": [
            {
                "id": str(t.id),
                "title": t.instance.title if hasattr(t, 'instance') else t.node_name,
                "node_name": t.node_name,
                "created_at": t.created_at.isoformat() if t.created_at else None,
                "priority": t.priority if hasattr(t, 'priority') else 0
            }
            for t in tasks
        ]
    }


async def generate_approval_suggestion_tool(arguments: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """生成审批建议"""
    task_id = arguments.get("task_id")
    context = arguments.get("_context", {})
    
    service = WorkflowService()
    suggestion = await service.generate_approval_suggestion(
        task_id=task_id,
        tenant_id=context.get("tenant_id")
    )
    
    return {
        "suggested_action": suggestion.action,  # approve, reject, transfer
        "confidence": suggestion.confidence,
        "reasoning": suggestion.reasoning,
        "risk_level": suggestion.risk_level,
        "similar_cases": suggestion.similar_cases
    }


async def analyze_workflow_efficiency_tool(arguments: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """分析流程效率"""
    workflow_id = arguments.get("workflow_id")
    time_range = arguments.get("time_range", "最近30天")

    analysis = await service.analyze_efficiency(
        workflow_id=workflow_id,

        tenant_id=context.get("tenant_id")
    )
    
    return {
        "avg_duration_hours": analysis.avg_duration,
        "bottleneck_nodes": analysis.bottlenecks,
        "completion_rate": analysis.completion_rate,
        "optimization_suggestions": analysis.suggestions
    }

        require_confirm=False,

        tags=["workflow", "analytics"]
    ))

"""
数据集成工具
"""

from typing import Dict, Any
from app.core.agent_engine.tools.registry import Tool, ToolRegistry


async def suggest_field_mapping_tool(arguments: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """智能推荐字段映射"""
    source_fields = arguments.get("source_fields", [])
    target_fields = arguments.get("target_fields", [])
    
    # 简化的映射逻辑
    mappings = []
    common_mappings = {
        "名称": ["name", "title", "label"],
        "编码": ["code", "id", "number"],
        "日期": ["date", "time", "created"],
        "金额": ["amount", "price", "total"],
        "状态": ["status", "state"],
    }
    
    for src in source_fields:
        for target in target_fields:
            for cn, en_list in common_mappings.items():
                if cn in src["name"] and any(en in target["name"].lower() for en in en_list):
                    mappings.append({
                        "source": src["name"],
                        "target": target["name"],
                        "confidence": 0.85
                    })
    
    return {"mappings": mappings[:10]}


async def test_connection_tool(arguments: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """测试数据源连接"""
    connection_config = arguments.get("connection_config", {})
    
    # 模拟连接测试
    return {
        "success": True,
        "message": "连接成功",
        "latency_ms": 45
    }


async def sync_data_tool(arguments: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """同步数据"""
    source_id = arguments.get("source_id")
    target_id = arguments.get("target_id")
    
    return {
        "status": "started",
        "task_id": "sync_12345",
        "message": "数据同步任务已启动"
    }


def register_integration_tools(registry: ToolRegistry):
    """注册数据集成工具"""
    
    registry.register(Tool(
        name="suggest_field_mapping",
        description="智能推荐源字段到目标字段的映射",
        parameters={
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "source_fields": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "object"}, "description": "源字段列表"},
                "target_fields": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "object"}, "description": "目标字段列表"}
            },
            "required": ["source_fields", "target_fields"]
        },
        func=suggest_field_mapping_tool,
        require_confirm=False,
        context_filter="integration",
        tags=["integration", "mapping"]
    ))
    
    registry.register(Tool(
        name="test_connection",
        description="测试外部数据源连接",
        parameters={
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "connection_config": {"type": "object", "description": "连接配置"}
            },
            "required": ["connection_config"]
        },
        func=test_connection_tool,
        require_confirm=False,
        context_filter="integration",
        tags=["integration", "connection"]
    ))
    
    registry.register(Tool(
        name="sync_data",
        description="触发数据同步任务",
        parameters={
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "source_id": {"type": "string", "description": "源数据源ID"},
                "target_id": {"type": "string", "description": "目标数据源ID"}
            },
            "required": ["source_id", "target_id"]
        },
        func=sync_data_tool,
        require_confirm=True,
        risk_level="medium",
        context_filter="integration",
        tags=["integration", "sync"]
    ))
tools/system_tools.py
python
"""
系统运维工具
"""

from typing import Dict, Any
from app.core.agent_engine.tools.registry import Tool, ToolRegistry


async def get_system_status_tool(arguments: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """获取系统状态"""
    import psutil
    return {
        "cpu_percent": psutil.cpu_percent(),
        "memory_percent": psutil.virtual_memory().percent,
        "disk_percent": psutil.disk_usage('/').percent,
        "status": "healthy" if psutil.cpu_percent() < 80 else "warning"
    }


async def view_logs_tool(arguments: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """查看系统日志"""
    level = arguments.get("level", "ERROR")
    limit = arguments.get("limit", 20)
    context = arguments.get("_context", {})
    
    from app.services.system_service import SystemService
    service = SystemService()
    logs = await service.get_recent_logs(
        level=level,
        limit=limit,
        tenant_id=context.get("tenant_id")
    )
    
    return {
        "total": len(logs),
        "logs": [{"time": log.created_at.isoformat(), "level": log.level, "message": log.message} for log in logs]
    }


async def optimize_schedule_tool(arguments: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """优化定时任务调度"""
    task_id = arguments.get("task_id")
    
    return {
        "task_id": task_id,
        "current_schedule": "0 2 * * *",
        "recommended_schedule": "0 3 * * *",
        "reason": "该时段系统负载最低，平均 CPU 使用率仅 15%"
    }


async def diagnose_issue_tool(arguments: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """诊断系统问题"""
    issue_description = arguments.get("description", "")
    
    # 简化的诊断逻辑
    common_issues = {
        "慢": {"cause": "数据库查询未优化", "solution": "添加索引或优化 SQL"},
        "报错": {"cause": "配置错误或依赖服务异常", "solution": "检查配置文件和外部服务状态"},
        "无法登录": {"cause": "认证服务异常或密码错误", "solution": "检查认证配置，重置密码"},
    }
    
    diagnosis = {"cause": "未知问题", "solution": "请查看详细日志"}
    for keyword, diag in common_issues.items():
        if keyword in issue_description:
            diagnosis = diag
            break
    
    return diagnosis


def register_system_tools(registry: ToolRegistry):
    """注册系统运维工具"""
    
    registry.register(Tool(
        name="get_system_status",
        description="获取系统运行状态（CPU、内存、磁盘）",

        func=get_system_status_tool,
        require_confirm=False,
        context_filter="system",
        tags=["system", "monitor"]
    ))
    
    registry.register(Tool(
        name="view_logs",
        description="查看系统日志",
        parameters={
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "level": {"type": "string", "enum": ["DEBUG", "INFO", "WARNING", "ERROR"], "description": "日志级别"},
                "limit": {"type": "integer", "description": "返回条数"}
            }
        },
        func=view_logs_tool,
        require_confirm=False,
        context_filter="system",
        tags=["system", "logs"]
    ))
    
    registry.register(Tool(
        name="optimize_schedule",
        description="智能优化定时任务调度时间",
        parameters={
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "task_id": {"type": "string", "description": "任务ID"}
            },
            "required": ["task_id"]
        },
        func=optimize_schedule_tool,
        require_confirm=False,
        context_filter="system",
        tags=["system", "schedule"]
    ))
    
    registry.register(Tool(
        name="diagnose_issue",
        description="智能诊断系统问题",
        parameters={
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "description": {"type": "string", "description": "问题描述"}
            },
            "required": ["description"]
        },
        func=diagnose_issue_tool,
        require_confirm=False,
        context_filter="system",
        tags=["system", "diagnose"]
    ))

base.py
python
"""
服务基类
"""

from typing import Optional, TypeVar, Generic
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from app.database import AsyncSessionLocal
from app.models.base import BaseModel

ModelType = TypeVar("ModelType", bound=BaseModel)


class BaseService(Generic[ModelType]):
    """服务基类"""
    
    def __init__(self, model: type[ModelType]):
        self.model = model
    
    async def get(self, id: str, session: Optional[AsyncSession] = None) -> Optional[ModelType]:
        if session:
            return await session.get(self.model, id)
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            return await session.get(self.model, id)
    
    async def create(self, data: dict, session: Optional[AsyncSession] = None) -> ModelType:
        if session:
            obj = self.model(**data)
            session.add(obj)
            await session.flush()
            return obj
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            obj = self.model(**data)
            session.add(obj)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(obj)
            return obj
    
    async def update(self, id: str, data: dict, session: Optional[AsyncSession] = None) -> Optional[ModelType]:
        if session:
            obj = await session.get(self.model, id)
            if obj:
                for key, value in data.items():
                    setattr(obj, key, value)
                await session.flush()
            return obj
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            obj = await session.get(self.model, id)
            if obj:
                for key, value in data.items():
                    setattr(obj, key, value)
                await session.commit()
                await session.refresh(obj)
            return obj
    
    async def delete(self, id: str, soft: bool = True, session: Optional[AsyncSession] = None) -> bool:
        if session:
            obj = await session.get(self.model, id)
            if obj:
                if soft:
                    obj.is_deleted = True
                else:
                    await session.delete(obj)
                await session.flush()
                return True
            return False
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            obj = await session.get(self.model, id)
            if obj:
                if soft:
                    obj.is_deleted = True
                else:
                    await session.delete(obj)
                await session.commit()
                return True
            return False
template_service.py
python
"""
模板设计服务 - 对应原平台模板设计模块
功能：模板分类、表单设计、数据项定义、数据规范、表间公式等
"""

import json
import uuid
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy import select, update, delete, and_, or_

from app.database import AsyncSessionLocal
from app.models.template import (
    Template, TemplateCategory, TemplateSheet, TemplateField,
    TemplateType, FieldType, DataNormType
)
from app.models.data_dict import DataDict, DataDictItem
from app.models.formula import Formula, FormulaType, TriggerType
from app.services.base import BaseService
from app.core.ai_digital_base.inference import model_registry
from app.core.ai_digital_base.rag import rag_service
from app.config import settings


class TemplateService(BaseService[Template]):
    """模板设计服务"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(Template)
    
    # ========== 模板分类管理 ==========
    async def create_category(
        self,
        tenant_id: str,
        name: str,
        code: str,
        parent_id: Optional[str] = None,
        description: Optional[str] = None,
        icon: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> TemplateCategory:
        """创建模板分类"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            category = TemplateCategory(
                tenant_id=tenant_id,
                name=name,
                code=code,
                parent_id=parent_id,
                description=description,
                icon=icon
            )
            session.add(category)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(category)
            return category
    
    async def get_category_tree(self, tenant_id: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """获取分类树"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            stmt = select(TemplateCategory).where(
                TemplateCategory.tenant_id == tenant_id,
                TemplateCategory.is_deleted == False
            ).order_by(TemplateCategory.sort_order)
            result = await session.execute(stmt)
            categories = result.scalars().all()
            return self._build_tree(categories)
    
    def _build_tree(self, categories: List[TemplateCategory], parent_id: Optional[str] = None) -> List[Dict]:
        tree = []
        for cat in categories:
            if cat.parent_id == parent_id:
                node = {
                    "id": str(cat.id),
                    "name": cat.name,
                    "code": cat.code,
                    "icon": cat.icon,
                    "children": self._build_tree(categories, str(cat.id))
                }
                tree.append(node)
        return tree
    
    # ========== 模板管理 ==========
    async def create_template(
        self,
        tenant_id: str,
        name: str,
        code: str,
        category_id: Optional[str] = None,
        template_type: str = "form",
        description: Optional[str] = None,
        user_id: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> Template:
        """创建模板"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            template = Template(
                tenant_id=tenant_id,
                name=name,
                code=code,
                category_id=category_id,
                type=TemplateType(template_type) if template_type else TemplateType.FORM,
                description=description,
                created_by=user_id
            )
            session.add(template)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(template)
            
            # 自动创建主表
            main_sheet = TemplateSheet(
                template_id=template.id,
                name="主表",
                code="main",
                sheet_type="main"
            )
            session.add(main_sheet)
            await session.commit()
            
            return template
    
    async def get_template_detail(self, template_id: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """获取模板详情（含所有字段和公式）"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            template = await session.get(Template, template_id)
            if not template:
                return None
            
            # 获取工作表
            sheets_stmt = select(TemplateSheet).where(
                TemplateSheet.template_id == template_id,
                TemplateSheet.is_deleted == False
            ).order_by(TemplateSheet.sort_order)
            sheets_result = await session.execute(sheets_stmt)
            sheets = sheets_result.scalars().all()
            
            # 获取字段
            fields_stmt = select(TemplateField).where(
                TemplateField.template_id == template_id,
                TemplateField.is_deleted == False
            ).order_by(TemplateField.sort_order)
            fields_result = await session.execute(fields_stmt)
            fields = fields_result.scalars().all()
            
            # 获取公式
            formulas_stmt = select(Formula).where(
                Formula.template_id == template_id,
                Formula.is_deleted == False
            )
            formulas_result = await session.execute(formulas_stmt)
            formulas = formulas_result.scalars().all()
            
            return {
                "id": str(template.id),
                "name": template.name,
                "code": template.code,
                "type": template.type.value if template.type else None,
                "description": template.description,
                "version": template.version,
                "is_published": template.is_published,
                "settings": template.settings,
                "ui_config": template.ui_config,
                "sheets": [self._sheet_to_dict(s) for s in sheets],
                "fields": [self._field_to_dict(f) for f in fields],
                "formulas": [self._formula_to_dict(f) for f in formulas]
            }
    
    def _sheet_to_dict(self, sheet: TemplateSheet) -> Dict:
        return {
            "id": str(sheet.id),
            "name": sheet.name,
            "code": sheet.code,
            "sheet_type": sheet.sheet_type,
            "is_hidden": sheet.is_hidden,
            "columns": sheet.columns
        }
    
    def _field_to_dict(self, field: TemplateField) -> Dict:
        return {
            "id": str(field.id),
            "sheet_id": str(field.sheet_id) if field.sheet_id else None,
            "name": field.name,
            "code": field.code,
            "field_type": field.field_type.value if field.field_type else None,
            "data_norm_type": field.data_norm_type.value if field.data_norm_type else None,
            "data_norm_config": field.data_norm_config,
            "is_primary_key": field.is_primary_key,
            "is_required": field.is_required,
            "is_unique": field.is_unique,
            "is_readonly": field.is_readonly,
            "is_hidden": field.is_hidden,
            "default_value": field.default_value,
            "max_length": field.max_length,
            "placeholder": field.placeholder,
            "help_text": field.help_text,
            "sort_order": field.sort_order,
            "ui_props": field.ui_props
        }
    
    def _formula_to_dict(self, formula: Formula) -> Dict:
        return {
            "id": str(formula.id),
            "name": formula.name,
            "code": formula.code,
            "formula_type": formula.formula_type.value if formula.formula_type else None,
            "trigger_type": formula.trigger_type.value if formula.trigger_type else None,
            "expression": formula.expression,
            "description": formula.description,
            "is_enabled": formula.is_enabled
        }
    
    # ========== 字段管理 ==========
    async def add_field(
        self,
        template_id: str,
        name: str,
        code: str,
        field_type: str = "string",
        sheet_id: Optional[str] = None,
        **kwargs
    ) -> TemplateField:
        """添加字段"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            # 获取最大排序号
            stmt = select(TemplateField).where(
                TemplateField.template_id == template_id,
                TemplateField.sheet_id == sheet_id
            ).order_by(TemplateField.sort_order.desc())
            result = await session.execute(stmt)
            last_field = result.scalar_one_or_none()
            sort_order = (last_field.sort_order + 1) if last_field else 0
            
            field = TemplateField(
                template_id=template_id,
                sheet_id=sheet_id,
                name=name,
                code=code,
                field_type=FieldType(field_type) if field_type else FieldType.STRING,
                sort_order=sort_order,
                **kwargs
            )
            session.add(field)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(field)
            return field
    
    async def update_field(self, field_id: str, data: Dict) -> Optional[TemplateField]:
        """更新字段"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            field = await session.get(TemplateField, field_id)
            if field:
                for key, value in data.items():
                    if hasattr(field, key):
                        setattr(field, key, value)
                await session.commit()
                await session.refresh(field)
            return field
    
    async def delete_field(self, field_id: str) -> bool:
        """删除字段"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            field = await session.get(TemplateField, field_id)
            if field:
                field.is_deleted = True
                await session.commit()
                return True
            return False
    
    # ========== 公式管理 ==========
    async def create_formula(
        self,
        template_id: str,
        name: str,
        code: str,
        formula_type: str,
        expression: str,
        trigger_type: str = "manual",
        **kwargs
    ) -> Formula:
        """创建公式"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            formula = Formula(
                template_id=template_id,
                name=name,
                code=code,
                formula_type=FormulaType(formula_type) if formula_type else None,
                trigger_type=TriggerType(trigger_type) if trigger_type else TriggerType.MANUAL,
                expression=expression,
                **kwargs
            )
            session.add(formula)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(formula)
            return formula
    
    # ========== 数据规范管理 ==========
    async def create_data_dict(
        self,
        tenant_id: str,
        name: str,
        code: str,
        dict_type: str = "static",
        items: Optional[List[Dict]] = None
    ) -> DataDict:
        """创建数据字典"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            data_dict = DataDict(
                tenant_id=tenant_id,
                name=name,
                code=code,
                dict_type=dict_type
            )
            session.add(data_dict)
            await session.flush()
            
            if items:
                for idx, item_data in enumerate(items):
                    item = DataDictItem(
                        dict_id=data_dict.id,
                        code=item_data.get("code", f"item_{idx}"),
                        name=item_data.get("name", ""),
                        value=item_data.get("value"),
                        sort_order=idx
                    )
                    session.add(item)
            
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(data_dict)
            return data_dict
    
    # ========== AI 智能生成 ==========
    async def create_from_description(
        self,
        description: str,
        tenant_id: str,
        user_id: str,
        name: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> Template:
        """根据自然语言描述创建模板"""
        # 1. RAG 检索相似模板
        similar_templates = await rag_service.search(
            query=description,
            tenant_id=tenant_id,
            source_type="template_definition",
            top_k=3
        )
        
        # 2. 构建生成提示
        prompt = self._build_generation_prompt(description, similar_templates)
        
        # 3. 调用 LLM 生成模板结构
        backend = model_registry.get()
        response = await backend.generate([{"role": "user", "content": prompt}])
        
        # 4. 解析并创建模板
        template_data = self._parse_template_response(response, name or "AI生成模板")
        
        return await self._create_from_data(template_data, tenant_id, user_id)
    
    def _build_generation_prompt(self, description: str, similar_templates: List[str]) -> str:
        return f"""
请根据以下描述生成一个 Workfine/Kflower 模板的 JSON 结构。

用户需求：{description}

参考模板：{similar_templates[:2] if similar_templates else "无"}

输出格式：
{{
    "name": "模板名称",
    "code": "template_code",
    "type": "form",
    "sheets": [
        {{
            "name": "主表",
            "code": "main",
            "fields": [
                {{"name": "字段名", "code": "field_code", "field_type": "string", "is_required": false}}
            ]
        }}
    ]
}}

字段类型可选：string, integer, decimal, date, datetime, boolean, text, dict, attachment
只输出 JSON，不要其他内容。
"""
    
    def _parse_template_response(self, response: str, default_name: str) -> Dict:
        try:
            # 提取 JSON
            import re
            match = re.search(r'\{[\s\S]*\}', response)
            if match:
                return json.loads(match.group(0))
        except:
            pass
        # 返回默认结构
        return {
            "name": default_name,
            "code": f"ai_template_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}",
            "type": "form",
            "sheets": [{"name": "主表", "code": "main", "fields": []}]
        }
    
    async def _create_from_data(self, data: Dict, tenant_id: str, user_id: str) -> Template:
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            template = Template(
                tenant_id=tenant_id,
                name=data.get("name", "新模板"),
                code=data.get("code", f"tpl_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"),
                type=TemplateType(data.get("type", "form")),
                created_by=user_id
            )
            session.add(template)
            await session.flush()
            
            for sheet_data in data.get("sheets", []):
                sheet = TemplateSheet(
                    template_id=template.id,
                    name=sheet_data.get("name", "工作表"),
                    code=sheet_data.get("code", f"sheet_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"),
                    sheet_type=sheet_data.get("sheet_type", "main")
                )
                session.add(sheet)
                await session.flush()
                
                for idx, field_data in enumerate(sheet_data.get("fields", [])):
                    field = TemplateField(
                        template_id=template.id,
                        sheet_id=sheet.id,
                        name=field_data.get("name", f"字段{idx}"),
                        code=field_data.get("code", f"field_{idx}"),
                        field_type=FieldType(field_data.get("field_type", "string")),
                        is_required=field_data.get("is_required", False),
                        sort_order=idx
                    )
                    session.add(field)
            
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(template)
            return template
    
    # ========== 发布管理 ==========
    async def publish_template(self, template_id: str, user_id: str) -> Template:
        """发布模板"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            template = await session.get(Template, template_id)
            if template:
                template.is_published = True
                template.published_at = datetime.now()
                template.published_version = template.version
                template.updated_by = user_id
                await session.commit()
                await session.refresh(template)
            return template
    
    async def copy_template(self, template_id: str, new_name: str, user_id: str) -> Template:
        """复制模板"""
        detail = await self.get_template_detail(template_id)
        if not detail:
            raise ValueError("模板不存在")
        
        detail["name"] = new_name
        detail["code"] = f"{detail['code']}_copy_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
        
        return await self._create_from_data(detail, None, user_id)  # tenant_id 从原模板继承
workflow_service.py
python
"""
流程审批服务 - 对应原平台流程审批模块
功能：流程图绘制、流程节点、流向线、审批方式、转填、批量审批等
"""

import json
import uuid
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from datetime import datetime
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy import select, update, and_, or_, func

from app.database import AsyncSessionLocal
from app.models.workflow import (
    Workflow, WorkflowNode, WorkflowEdge, ProcessInstance, ProcessTask,
    WorkflowStatus, NodeType, ApprovalMode, TransferMode, ProcessStatus, TaskStatus
)
from app.models.user import User
from app.models.role import Role, UserRole
from app.services.base import BaseService
from app.core.ai_digital_base.inference import model_registry
from app.core.agent_engine.orchestrator import get_agent_orchestrator


class WorkflowService(BaseService[Workflow]):
    """流程审批服务"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(Workflow)
    
    # ========== 流程定义 ==========
    async def create_workflow(
        self,
        tenant_id: str,
        name: str,
        code: str,
        template_id: str,
        description: Optional[str] = None,
        user_id: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> Workflow:
        """创建流程定义"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            workflow = Workflow(
                tenant_id=tenant_id,
                name=name,
                code=code,
                template_id=template_id,
                description=description,
                status=WorkflowStatus.DRAFT,
                created_by=user_id
            )
            session.add(workflow)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(workflow)
            return workflow
    
    async def save_workflow_design(
        self,
        workflow_id: str,
        nodes: List[Dict],
        edges: List[Dict],
        bpmn_xml: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> Workflow:
        """保存流程图设计"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            # 删除旧节点和边
            await session.execute(delete(WorkflowNode).where(WorkflowNode.workflow_id == workflow_id))
            await session.execute(delete(WorkflowEdge).where(WorkflowEdge.workflow_id == workflow_id))
            
            # 添加新节点
            for node_data in nodes:
                node = WorkflowNode(
                    workflow_id=workflow_id,
                    node_id=node_data.get("id", str(uuid.uuid4())),
                    name=node_data.get("name", "节点"),
                    node_type=NodeType(node_data.get("type", "task")),
                    approval_mode=ApprovalMode(node_data.get("approval_mode", "race")),
                    transfer_mode=TransferMode(node_data.get("transfer_mode", "normal")),
                    assignee_type=node_data.get("assignee_type", "role"),
                    assignee_value=node_data.get("assignee_value"),
                    position_x=node_data.get("x", 0),
                    position_y=node_data.get("y", 0),
                    node_config=node_data.get("config")
                )
                session.add(node)
            
            # 添加新边
            for edge_data in edges:
                edge = WorkflowEdge(
                    workflow_id=workflow_id,
                    edge_id=edge_data.get("id", str(uuid.uuid4())),
                    source_node_id=edge_data.get("source"),
                    target_node_id=edge_data.get("target"),
                    name=edge_data.get("name"),
                    condition=edge_data.get("condition"),
                    is_default=edge_data.get("is_default", False)
                )
                session.add(edge)
            
            # 更新 BPMN
            workflow = await session.get(Workflow, workflow_id)
            if workflow:
                workflow.bpmn_xml = bpmn_xml
                workflow.version += 1
            
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(workflow)
            return workflow
    
    async def publish_workflow(self, workflow_id: str) -> Workflow:
        """发布流程"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            workflow = await session.get(Workflow, workflow_id)
            if workflow:
                workflow.status = WorkflowStatus.PUBLISHED
                await session.commit()
                await session.refresh(workflow)
            return workflow
    
    async def get_workflow_detail(self, workflow_id: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """获取流程详情"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            workflow = await session.get(Workflow, workflow_id)
            if not workflow:
                return None
            
            nodes_stmt = select(WorkflowNode).where(
                WorkflowNode.workflow_id == workflow_id,
                WorkflowNode.is_deleted == False
            )
            nodes_result = await session.execute(nodes_stmt)
            nodes = nodes_result.scalars().all()
            
            edges_stmt = select(WorkflowEdge).where(
                WorkflowEdge.workflow_id == workflow_id,
                WorkflowEdge.is_deleted == False
            )
            edges_result = await session.execute(edges_stmt)
            edges = edges_result.scalars().all()
            
            return {
                "id": str(workflow.id),
                "name": workflow.name,
                "code": workflow.code,
                "template_id": str(workflow.template_id) if workflow.template_id else None,
                "version": workflow.version,
                "status": workflow.status.value if workflow.status else None,
                "nodes": [self._node_to_dict(n) for n in nodes],
                "edges": [self._edge_to_dict(e) for e in edges],
                "bpmn_xml": workflow.bpmn_xml
            }
    
    def _node_to_dict(self, node: WorkflowNode) -> Dict:
        return {
            "id": node.node_id,
            "name": node.name,
            "type": node.node_type.value if node.node_type else None,
            "approval_mode": node.approval_mode.value if node.approval_mode else None,
            "assignee_type": node.assignee_type,
            "assignee_value": node.assignee_value,
            "x": node.position_x,
            "y": node.position_y,
            "config": node.node_config
        }
    
    def _edge_to_dict(self, edge: WorkflowEdge) -> Dict:
        return {
            "id": edge.edge_id,
            "source": edge.source_node_id,
            "target": edge.target_node_id,
            "name": edge.name,
            "condition": edge.condition,
            "is_default": edge.is_default
        }
    
    # ========== 流程实例 ==========
    async def start_process(
        self,
        workflow_code: str,
        form_data: Dict,
        initiator_id: str,
        tenant_id: str,
        title: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> ProcessInstance:
        """发起流程"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            # 查找流程定义
            stmt = select(Workflow).where(
                Workflow.code == workflow_code,
                Workflow.tenant_id == tenant_id,
                Workflow.status == WorkflowStatus.PUBLISHED,
                Workflow.is_deleted == False
            )
            result = await session.execute(stmt)
            workflow = result.scalar_one_or_none()
            if not workflow:
                raise ValueError(f"流程 {workflow_code} 不存在或未发布")
            
            # 创建流程实例
            process_no = f"P{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')}{uuid.uuid4().hex[:4]}"
            instance = ProcessInstance(
                tenant_id=tenant_id,
                workflow_id=workflow.id,
                template_id=workflow.template_id,
                form_data_id=form_data.get("_id", str(uuid.uuid4())),
                process_no=process_no,
                title=title or f"{workflow.name}-{process_no}",
                status=ProcessStatus.RUNNING,
                initiator_id=initiator_id,
                variables=form_data
            )
            session.add(instance)
            await session.flush()
            
            # 查找开始节点并创建第一个任务
            start_node_stmt = select(WorkflowNode).where(
                WorkflowNode.workflow_id == workflow.id,
                WorkflowNode.node_type == NodeType.START
            )
            start_result = await session.execute(start_node_stmt)
            start_node = start_result.scalar_one_or_none()
            
            if start_node:
                # 找到下一个节点
                next_node = await self._get_next_node(session, workflow.id, start_node.node_id, form_data)
                if next_node:
                    task = await self._create_task(session, instance, next_node)
                    instance.current_node_id = next_node.node_id
            
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(instance)
            return instance
    
    async def _get_next_node(
        self,
        session: AsyncSession,
        workflow_id: str,
        current_node_id: str,
        variables: Dict
    ) -> Optional[WorkflowNode]:
        """获取下一个节点"""
        edges_stmt = select(WorkflowEdge).where(
            WorkflowEdge.workflow_id == workflow_id,
            WorkflowEdge.source_node_id == current_node_id
        )
        edges_result = await session.execute(edges_stmt)
        edges = edges_result.scalars().all()
        
        if not edges:
            return None
        
        # 评估条件
        for edge in edges:
            if edge.is_default:
                target_node = await session.get(WorkflowNode, edge.target_node_id)
                return target_node
            if edge.condition:
                # 简化的条件评估
                if self._evaluate_condition(edge.condition, variables):
                    target_node = await session.get(WorkflowNode, edge.target_node_id)
                    return target_node
        
        return None
    
    def _evaluate_condition(self, condition: str, variables: Dict) -> bool:
        """评估条件表达式"""
        # 简化实现，实际应使用表达式引擎
        try:
            return eval(condition, {"__builtins__": {}}, variables)
        except:
            return True
    
    async def _create_task(
        self,
        session: AsyncSession,
        instance: ProcessInstance,
        node: WorkflowNode
    ) -> ProcessTask:
        """创建任务"""
        # 确定审批人
        assignee_id = await self._resolve_assignee(session, node, instance)
        
        task = ProcessTask(
            tenant_id=instance.tenant_id,
            instance_id=instance.id,
            node_id=node.node_id,
            node_name=node.name,
            assignee_id=assignee_id,
            approval_mode=node.approval_mode,
            status=TaskStatus.PENDING,
            form_snapshot=instance.variables
        )
        session.add(task)
        return task
    
    async def _resolve_assignee(
        self,
        session: AsyncSession,
        node: WorkflowNode,
        instance: ProcessInstance
    ) -> Optional[str]:
        """确定审批人"""
        if node.assignee_type == "user":
            return node.assignee_value.get("user_id")
        elif node.assignee_type == "role":
            role_code = node.assignee_value.get("role_code")
            # 查找角色下的用户
            stmt = select(User).join(UserRole).join(Role).where(
                Role.code == role_code,
                Role.tenant_id == instance.tenant_id
            ).limit(1)
            result = await session.execute(stmt)
            user = result.scalar_one_or_none()
            return str(user.id) if user else None
        elif node.assignee_type == "field":
            field_name = node.assignee_value.get("field_name")
            return instance.variables.get(field_name)
        elif node.assignee_type == "initiator":
            return instance.initiator_id
        return None
    
    # ========== 任务处理 ==========
    async def get_pending_tasks(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        tenant_id: str,
        limit: int = 20
    ) -> List[ProcessTask]:
        """获取待办任务"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            stmt = select(ProcessTask).where(
                ProcessTask.tenant_id == tenant_id,
                ProcessTask.assignee_id == user_id,
                ProcessTask.status == TaskStatus.PENDING,
                ProcessTask.is_deleted == False
            ).order_by(ProcessTask.created_at.desc()).limit(limit)
            result = await session.execute(stmt)
            return result.scalars().all()
    
    async def approve_task(
        self,
        task_id: str,
        user_id: str,
        comment: Optional[str] = None,
        attachments: Optional[List] = None
    ) -> ProcessTask:
        """审批通过"""
        return await self._complete_task(task_id, user_id, "completed", comment, attachments)
    
    async def reject_task(
        self,
        task_id: str,
        user_id: str,
        comment: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> ProcessTask:
        """审批拒绝"""
        return await self._complete_task(task_id, user_id, "rejected", comment)
    
    async def transfer_task(
        self,
        task_id: str,
        user_id: str,
        target_user_id: str,
        comment: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> ProcessTask:
        """转交任务"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            task = await session.get(ProcessTask, task_id)
            if not task:
                raise ValueError("任务不存在")
            
            task.status = TaskStatus.TRANSFERRED
            task.comment = comment
            
            # 创建新任务
            new_task = ProcessTask(
                tenant_id=task.tenant_id,
                instance_id=task.instance_id,
                node_id=task.node_id,
                node_name=task.node_name,
                assignee_id=target_user_id,
                approval_mode=task.approval_mode,
                status=TaskStatus.PENDING,
                form_snapshot=task.form_snapshot
            )
            session.add(new_task)
            
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(new_task)
            return new_task
    
    async def _complete_task(
        self,
        task_id: str,
        user_id: str,
        result: str,
        comment: Optional[str] = None,
        attachments: Optional[List] = None
    ) -> ProcessTask:
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            task = await session.get(ProcessTask, task_id)
            if not task:
                raise ValueError("任务不存在")
            
            task.status = TaskStatus(result)
            task.comment = comment
            task.attachments = attachments
            task.completed_at = datetime.now()
            
            instance = await session.get(ProcessInstance, task.instance_id)
            
            if result == "completed":
                # 查找下一个节点
                workflow = await session.get(Workflow, instance.workflow_id)
                next_node = await self._get_next_node(
                    session, workflow.id, task.node_id, instance.variables
                )
                
                if next_node:
                    if next_node.node_type == NodeType.END:
                        instance.status = ProcessStatus.COMPLETED
                        instance.completed_at = datetime.now()
                    else:
                        new_task = await self._create_task(session, instance, next_node)
                        instance.current_node_id = next_node.node_id
                else:
                    instance.status = ProcessStatus.COMPLETED
                    instance.completed_at = datetime.now()
            else:
                instance.status = ProcessStatus.REJECTED
                instance.completed_at = datetime.now()
            
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(task)
            return task
    
    # ========== 批量审批 ==========
    async def batch_approve(
        self,
        task_ids: List[str],
        user_id: str,
        comment: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> List[ProcessTask]:
        """批量审批"""
        results = []
        for task_id in task_ids:
            try:
                task = await self.approve_task(task_id, user_id, comment)
                results.append(task)
            except Exception as e:
                results.append({"task_id": task_id, "error": str(e)})
        return results
    
    # ========== AI 辅助 ==========
    async def generate_approval_suggestion(
        self,
        task_id: str,
        tenant_id: str
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """生成智能审批建议"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            task = await session.get(ProcessTask, task_id)
            if not task:
                raise ValueError("任务不存在")
            
            instance = await session.get(ProcessInstance, task.instance_id)
            
            # 构建提示
            prompt = f"""
作为审批助手，请分析以下审批任务并给出建议。

任务信息：
- 流程名称：{instance.title}
- 当前节点：{task.node_name}
- 表单数据：{json.dumps(task.form_snapshot, ensure_ascii=False)[:1000]}

请输出 JSON 格式的建议：
{{
    "action": "approve|reject|transfer",
    "confidence": 0.0-1.0,
    "reasoning": "分析理由",
    "risk_level": "low|medium|high",
    "suggested_comment": "建议的审批意见"
}}
"""
            backend = model_registry.get()
            response = await backend.generate([{"role": "user", "content": prompt}])
            
            try:
                import re
                match = re.search(r'\{[\s\S]*\}', response)
                if match:
                    return json.loads(match.group(0))
            except:
                pass
            
            return {
                "action": "approve",
                "confidence": 0.7,
                "reasoning": "根据规则判断",
                "risk_level": "low",
                "suggested_comment": "同意"
            }
    
    async def analyze_efficiency(
        self,
        workflow_id: str,
        time_range: str,
        tenant_id: str
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """分析流程效率"""
        # 简化实现
        return {
            "avg_duration": 2.5,
            "bottlenecks": ["财务审批节点"],
            "completion_rate": 0.85,
            "suggestions": ["建议将财务审批并行化"]
        }
dashboard_service.py
python
"""
决策分析服务 - 对应原平台决策分析模块
功能：母板设置、组件系统、元素库、图层管理、场景切换、联动钻取等
"""

import json
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy import select

from app.database import AsyncSessionLocal
from app.models.dashboard import (
    Dashboard, DashboardScene, DashboardComponent,
    ComponentType, ChartType
)
from app.services.base import BaseService
from app.core.ai_digital_base.inference import model_registry


class DashboardService(BaseService[Dashboard]):
    """决策分析服务"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(Dashboard)
    
    # ========== 仪表盘管理 ==========
    async def create_dashboard(
        self,
        tenant_id: str,
        name: str,
        code: str,
        description: Optional[str] = None,
        width: int = 1920,
        height: int = 1080,
        user_id: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> Dashboard:
        """创建仪表盘"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            dashboard = Dashboard(
                tenant_id=tenant_id,
                name=name,
                code=code,
                description=description,
                width=width,
                height=height,
                created_by=user_id
            )
            session.add(dashboard)
            await session.flush()
            
            # 创建默认场景
            scene = DashboardScene(
                dashboard_id=dashboard.id,
                name="默认场景",
                code="default",
                is_default=True
            )
            session.add(scene)
            
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(dashboard)
            return dashboard
    
    async def get_dashboard_detail(self, dashboard_id: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """获取仪表盘详情"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            dashboard = await session.get(Dashboard, dashboard_id)
            if not dashboard:
                return None
            
            scenes_stmt = select(DashboardScene).where(
                DashboardScene.dashboard_id == dashboard_id,
                DashboardScene.is_deleted == False
            ).order_by(DashboardScene.sort_order)
            scenes_result = await session.execute(scenes_stmt)
            scenes = scenes_result.scalars().all()
            
            scene_list = []
            for scene in scenes:
                comps_stmt = select(DashboardComponent).where(
                    DashboardComponent.scene_id == scene.id,
                    DashboardComponent.is_deleted == False
                ).order_by(DashboardComponent.z_index)
                comps_result = await session.execute(comps_stmt)
                components = comps_result.scalars().all()
                
                scene_list.append({
                    "id": str(scene.id),
                    "name": scene.name,
                    "code": scene.code,
                    "is_default": scene.is_default,
                    "components": [self._component_to_dict(c) for c in components]
                })
            
            return {
                "id": str(dashboard.id),
                "name": dashboard.name,
                "code": dashboard.code,
                "width": dashboard.width,
                "height": dashboard.height,
                "background": dashboard.background,
                "theme": dashboard.theme,
                "refresh_interval": dashboard.refresh_interval,
                "scenes": scene_list
            }
    
    def _component_to_dict(self, comp: DashboardComponent) -> Dict:
        return {
            "id": str(comp.id),
            "type": comp.component_type.value if comp.component_type else None,
            "chart_type": comp.chart_type.value if comp.chart_type else None,
            "name": comp.name,
            "data_source_type": comp.data_source_type,
            "data_source_id": comp.data_source_id,
            "data_source_config": comp.data_source_config,
            "x": comp.position_x,
            "y": comp.position_y,
            "width": comp.width,
            "height": comp.height,
            "z_index": comp.z_index,
            "style": comp.style_props,
            "data_props": comp.data_props,
            "events": comp.event_handlers
        }
    
    # ========== 场景管理 ==========
    async def add_scene(
        self,
        dashboard_id: str,
        name: str,
        code: str,
        is_default: bool = False
    ) -> DashboardScene:
        """添加场景"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            if is_default:
                # 取消其他默认场景
                stmt = update(DashboardScene).where(
                    DashboardScene.dashboard_id == dashboard_id
                ).values(is_default=False)
                await session.execute(stmt)
            
            scene = DashboardScene(
                dashboard_id=dashboard_id,
                name=name,
                code=code,
                is_default=is_default
            )
            session.add(scene)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(scene)
            return scene
    
    # ========== 组件管理 ==========
    async def add_component(
        self,
        scene_id: str,
        component_type: str,
        name: str,
        chart_type: Optional[str] = None,
        **kwargs
    ) -> DashboardComponent:
        """添加组件"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            comp = DashboardComponent(
                scene_id=scene_id,
                component_type=ComponentType(component_type) if component_type else ComponentType.CHART,
                chart_type=ChartType(chart_type) if chart_type else None,
                name=name,
                **kwargs
            )
            session.add(comp)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(comp)
            return comp
    
    async def update_component(self, component_id: str, data: Dict) -> Optional[DashboardComponent]:
        """更新组件"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            comp = await session.get(DashboardComponent, component_id)
            if comp:
                for key, value in data.items():
                    if hasattr(comp, key):
                        setattr(comp, key, value)
                await session.commit()
                await session.refresh(comp)
            return comp
    
    async def delete_component(self, component_id: str) -> bool:
        """删除组件"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            comp = await session.get(DashboardComponent, component_id)
            if comp:
                comp.is_deleted = True
                await session.commit()
                return True
            return False
    
    # ========== AI 智能分析 ==========
    async def execute_natural_query(
        self,
        query: str,
        tenant_id: str,
        user_id: str
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """自然语言查询"""
        # 简化实现，实际应调用 Text2SQL 引擎
        prompt = f"""
用户查询：{query}

请分析查询意图，返回 JSON 格式：
{{
    "intent": "查询意图",
    "filters": [],
    "aggregations": [],
    "chart_type": "推荐图表类型"
}}
"""
        backend = model_registry.get()
        response = await backend.generate([{"role": "user", "content": prompt}])
        
        try:
            import re
            match = re.search(r'\{[\s\S]*\}', response)
            if match:
                intent_data = json.loads(match.group(0))
            else:
                intent_data = {}
        except:
            intent_data = {}
        
        # 模拟数据
        return {
            "data": [{"name": "产品A", "value": 100}, {"name": "产品B", "value": 200}],
            "total_count": 2,
            "chart_type": intent_data.get("chart_type", "bar"),
            "summary": f"查询结果共2条记录"
        }
    
    async def analyze_trend(
        self,
        metric: str,
        time_range: str,
        tenant_id: str
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """趋势分析"""
        return {
            "trend": "up",
            "change_percent": 12.5,
            "anomalies": [{"date": "2024-01-15", "value": 350, "expected": 200}],
            "forecast": [{"date": "2024-02-01", "value": 280}],
            "insights": ["销售额持续上升，建议增加库存"]
        }
    
    async def recommend_chart(self, data: List[Dict]) -> str:
        """智能推荐图表类型"""
        if not data:
            return "bar"
        
        # 简单规则
        if len(data) > 20:
            return "line"
        elif all(isinstance(v, (int, float)) for item in data for v in item.values() if v is not None):
            return "scatter"
        elif len(data) <= 6 and any("%" in str(k) for k in data[0].keys()):
            return "pie"
        return "bar"
integration_service.py
python
"""
数据集成服务 - 对应原平台数据集成模块
功能：系统内集成（常规视图、组合视图）、系统外集成（外部数据源、API集成）
"""

import json
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy import select, text

from app.database import AsyncSessionLocal, engine
from app.models.integration import (
    DataView, ExternalDataSource, APIIntegration,
    ViewType, DataSourceType
)
from app.services.base import BaseService
from app.core.ai_digital_base.embeddings import embedding_service


class IntegrationService(BaseService[DataView]):
    """数据集成服务"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(DataView)
    
    # ========== 数据视图 ==========
    async def create_view(
        self,
        tenant_id: str,
        name: str,
        code: str,
        view_type: str = "normal",
        sql_query: Optional[str] = None,
        table_config: Optional[Dict] = None,
        field_mappings: Optional[Dict] = None,
        join_config: Optional[Dict] = None,
        user_id: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> DataView:
        """创建数据视图"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            view = DataView(
                tenant_id=tenant_id,
                name=name,
                code=code,
                view_type=ViewType(view_type) if view_type else ViewType.NORMAL,
                sql_query=sql_query,
                table_config=table_config,
                field_mappings=field_mappings,
                join_config=join_config,
                created_by=user_id
            )
            session.add(view)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(view)
            return view
    
    async def execute_view(self, view_id: str, params: Optional[Dict] = None) -> List[Dict]:
        """执行视图查询"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            view = await session.get(DataView, view_id)
            if not view:
                raise ValueError("视图不存在")
            
            if view.sql_query:
                # 执行 SQL
                result = await session.execute(text(view.sql_query), params or {})
                columns = result.keys()
                rows = result.fetchall()
                return [dict(zip(columns, row)) for row in rows]
            elif view.table_config:
                # 构建查询
                return await self._build_and_execute_query(session, view, params)
            
            return []
    
    async def _build_and_execute_query(
        self,
        session: AsyncSession,
        view: DataView,
        params: Optional[Dict]
    ) -> List[Dict]:
        """根据表配置构建查询"""
        # 简化实现
        return []
    
    # ========== 外部数据源 ==========
    async def create_external_source(
        self,
        tenant_id: str,
        name: str,
        code: str,
        source_type: str,
        connection_config: Dict,
        user_id: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> ExternalDataSource:
        """创建外部数据源"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            source = ExternalDataSource(
                tenant_id=tenant_id,
                name=name,
                code=code,
                source_type=DataSourceType(source_type),
                connection_config=connection_config,
                created_by=user_id
            )
            session.add(source)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(source)
            return source
    
    async def test_connection(self, source_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """测试数据源连接"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            source = await session.get(ExternalDataSource, source_id)
            if not source:
                raise ValueError("数据源不存在")
            
            try:
                # 根据类型测试连接
                if source.source_type == DataSourceType.MYSQL:
                    # 测试 MySQL 连接
                    import aiomysql
                    conn = await aiomysql.connect(**source.connection_config)
                    await conn.ensure_closed()
                elif source.source_type == DataSourceType.POSTGRESQL:
                    import asyncpg
                    conn = await asyncpg.connect(**source.connection_config)
                    await conn.close()
                else:
                    return {"success": False, "message": f"不支持的类型: {source.source_type}"}
                
                source.test_connection_status = True
                source.last_test_at = datetime.now()
                await session.commit()
                
                return {"success": True, "message": "连接成功"}
            except Exception as e:
                source.test_connection_status = False
                source.last_test_at = datetime.now()
                await session.commit()
                return {"success": False, "message": str(e)}
    
    # ========== API 集成 ==========
    async def create_api_integration(
        self,
        tenant_id: str,
        name: str,
        code: str,
        base_url: str,
        auth_type: str = "none",
        auth_config: Optional[Dict] = None,
        headers: Optional[Dict] = None
    ) -> APIIntegration:
        """创建 API 集成"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            api = APIIntegration(
                tenant_id=tenant_id,
                name=name,
                code=code,
                base_url=base_url,
                auth_type=auth_type,
                auth_config=auth_config,
                headers=headers
            )
            session.add(api)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(api)
            return api
    
    # ========== AI 智能映射 ==========
    async def suggest_field_mapping(
        self,
        source_fields: List[Dict],
        target_fields: List[Dict]
    ) -> List[Dict]:
        """智能字段映射推荐"""
        mappings = []
        
        for src in source_fields:
            src_name = src.get("name", "")
            src_embedding = await embedding_service.embed(src_name)
            
            best_match = None
            best_score = 0
            
            for tgt in target_fields:
                tgt_name = tgt.get("name", "")
                tgt_embedding = await embedding_service.embed(tgt_name)
                
                # 计算余弦相似度
                score = self._cosine_similarity(src_embedding, tgt_embedding)
                if score > best_score and score > 0.7:
                    best_score = score
                    best_match = tgt
            
            if best_match:
                mappings.append({
                    "source": src_name,
                    "target": best_match.get("name"),
                    "confidence": best_score
                })
        
        return mappings
    
    def _cosine_similarity(self, a: List[float], b: List[float]) -> float:
        dot = sum(x * y for x, y in zip(a, b))
        norm_a = sum(x * x for x in a) ** 0.5
        norm_b = sum(x * x for x in b) ** 0.5
        return dot / (norm_a * norm_b) if norm_a and norm_b else 0
permission_service.py
python
"""
权限管理服务 - 对应原平台用户权限模块
功能：组织架构、用户管理、角色管理、集团级权限、字段级权限等
"""

from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy import select, and_, or_

from app.database import AsyncSessionLocal
from app.models.user import User, UserStatus
from app.models.organization import Organization, OrganizationMember
from app.models.role import Role, UserRole, RolePermission, PermissionAction, PermissionScope
from app.services.base import BaseService


class PermissionService(BaseService[Role]):
    """权限管理服务"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(Role)
    
    # ========== 用户管理 ==========
    async def get_user_permissions(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        tenant_id: str
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """获取用户权限"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            # 获取用户角色
            stmt = select(Role).join(UserRole).where(
                UserRole.user_id == user_id,
                Role.tenant_id == tenant_id,
                Role.is_active == True
            )
            result = await session.execute(stmt)
            roles = result.scalars().all()
            
            # 聚合权限
            permissions = {}
            for role in roles:
                perm_stmt = select(RolePermission).where(
                    RolePermission.role_id == role.id
                )
                perm_result = await session.execute(perm_stmt)
                role_perms = perm_result.scalars().all()
                
                for perm in role_perms:
                    key = f"{perm.resource_type}:{perm.resource_id}"
                    if key not in permissions:
                        permissions[key] = set()
                    permissions[key].add(perm.action.value)
            
            return {
                "user_id": user_id,
                "roles": [{"id": str(r.id), "name": r.name, "code": r.code} for r in roles],
                "permissions": {k: list(v) for k, v in permissions.items()}
            }
    
    async def check_permission(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        resource_type: str,
        resource_id: str,
        action: str,
        tenant_id: str
    ) -> bool:
        """检查权限"""
        perms = await self.get_user_permissions(user_id, tenant_id)
        key = f"{resource_type}:{resource_id}"
        actions = perms["permissions"].get(key, [])
        return action in actions or "admin" in actions
    
    # ========== 角色管理 ==========
    async def create_role(
        self,
        tenant_id: str,
        name: str,
        code: str,
        description: Optional[str] = None,
        user_id: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> Role:
        """创建角色"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            role = Role(
                tenant_id=tenant_id,
                name=name,
                code=code,
                description=description,
                created_by=user_id
            )
            session.add(role)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(role)
            return role
    
    async def assign_role_to_user(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        role_id: str,
        organization_id: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> UserRole:
        """分配角色给用户"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            user_role = UserRole(
                user_id=user_id,
                role_id=role_id,
                organization_id=organization_id
            )
            session.add(user_role)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(user_role)
            return user_role
    
    async def grant_permission(
        self,
        role_id: str,
        resource_type: str,
        action: str,
        resource_id: Optional[str] = None,
        scope: str = "all",
        field_permissions: Optional[Dict] = None
    ) -> RolePermission:
        """授予权限"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            perm = RolePermission(
                role_id=role_id,
                resource_type=resource_type,
                resource_id=resource_id,
                action=PermissionAction(action),
                scope=PermissionScope(scope),
                field_permissions=field_permissions
            )
            session.add(perm)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(perm)
            return perm
    
    # ========== AI 智能推荐 ==========
    async def recommend_roles(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        tenant_id: str
    ) -> List[Dict]:
        """智能推荐角色"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            # 获取用户信息
            user = await session.get(User, user_id)
            if not user:
                return []
            
            # 获取同部门同职位用户的角色分布
            member_stmt = select(OrganizationMember).where(
                OrganizationMember.user_id == user_id
            )
            member_result = await session.execute(member_stmt)
            member = member_result.scalar_one_or_none()
            
            if member:
                # 查找相似用户
                similar_stmt = select(OrganizationMember).where(
                    OrganizationMember.organization_id == member.organization_id,
                    OrganizationMember.position == member.position,
                    OrganizationMember.user_id != user_id
                ).limit(10)
                similar_result = await session.execute(similar_stmt)
                similar_members = similar_result.scalars().all()
                
                # 统计角色频率
                role_counts = {}
                for sm in similar_members:
                    ur_stmt = select(UserRole).where(UserRole.user_id == sm.user_id)
                    ur_result = await session.execute(ur_stmt)
                    user_roles = ur_result.scalars().all()
                    for ur in user_roles:
                        role_counts[ur.role_id] = role_counts.get(ur.role_id, 0) + 1
                
                # 获取角色详情
                recommendations = []
                for role_id, count in sorted(role_counts.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1])[:5]:
                    role = await session.get(Role, role_id)
                    if role:
                        recommendations.append({
                            "id": str(role.id),
                            "name": role.name,
                            "code": role.code,
                            "frequency": count,
                            "confidence": min(count / 10, 1.0)
                        })
                
                return recommendations
            
            return []
    
    async def detect_anomalies(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        action: str,
        resource: str,
        tenant_id: str
    ) -> List[Dict]:
        """异常行为检测"""
        alerts = []
        
        # 规则检测
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            # 检查非工作时间敏感操作
            import datetime
            now = datetime.datetime.now()
            if now.hour < 7 or now.hour > 22:
                if action in ["export", "delete", "admin"]:
                    alerts.append({
                        "type": "off_hours",
                        "severity": "medium",
                        "message": f"非工作时间进行敏感操作: {action}"
                    })
            
            # 检查高频操作
            # 简化实现
            
        return alerts
