python
"""
系统设置服务 - 对应原平台系统设置模块
功能：移动设置、定时任务、邮箱设置、短信设置、系统预警、系统日志等
"""

import json
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from datetime import datetime
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy import select

from app.database import AsyncSessionLocal
from app.models.system import (
    ScheduledTask, EmailConfig, SMSConfig, SystemAlert, SystemLog,
    TaskType, ScheduleType, AlertType
)
from app.services.base import BaseService
from app.core.ai_digital_base.inference import model_registry


class SystemService(BaseService[ScheduledTask]):
    """系统设置服务"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(ScheduledTask)
    
    # ========== 定时任务 ==========
    async def create_scheduled_task(
        self,
        tenant_id: str,
        name: str,
        code: str,
        task_type: str,
        schedule_type: str,
        task_config: Dict,
        cron_expression: Optional[str] = None,
        user_id: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> ScheduledTask:
        """创建定时任务"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            task = ScheduledTask(
                tenant_id=tenant_id,
                name=name,
                code=code,
                task_type=TaskType(task_type),
                schedule_type=ScheduleType(schedule_type),
                cron_expression=cron_expression,
                task_config=task_config,
                created_by=user_id
            )
            session.add(task)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(task)
            return task
    
    async def get_tasks(self, tenant_id: str) -> List[ScheduledTask]:
        """获取定时任务列表"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            stmt = select(ScheduledTask).where(
                ScheduledTask.tenant_id == tenant_id,
                ScheduledTask.is_deleted == False
            )
            result = await session.execute(stmt)
            return result.scalars().all()
    
    # ========== 邮箱配置 ==========
    async def create_email_config(
        self,
        tenant_id: str,
        name: str,
        smtp_host: str,
        smtp_port: int,
        smtp_user: str,
        smtp_password: str,
        from_email: str,
        is_default: bool = False,
        user_id: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> EmailConfig:
        """创建邮箱配置"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            if is_default:
                stmt = update(EmailConfig).where(
                    EmailConfig.tenant_id == tenant_id
                ).values(is_default=False)
                await session.execute(stmt)
            
            config = EmailConfig(
                tenant_id=tenant_id,
                name=name,
                smtp_host=smtp_host,
                smtp_port=smtp_port,
                smtp_user=smtp_user,
                smtp_password=smtp_password,
                from_email=from_email,
                is_default=is_default,
                created_by=user_id
            )
            session.add(config)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(config)
            return config
    
    # ========== 系统预警 ==========
    async def create_alert(
        self,
        tenant_id: str,
        name: str,
        alert_type: str,
        data_source_type: str,
        data_source_id: str,
        condition: Dict,
        message_template: str,
        recipients: List[str],
        user_id: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> SystemAlert:
        """创建系统预警"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            alert = SystemAlert(
                tenant_id=tenant_id,
                name=name,
                alert_type=AlertType(alert_type),
                data_source_type=data_source_type,
                data_source_id=data_source_id,
                condition=condition,
                message_template=message_template,
                recipients=recipients,
                created_by=user_id
            )
            session.add(alert)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(alert)
            return alert
    
    # ========== 系统日志 ==========
    async def log(
        self,
        tenant_id: str,
        action: str,
        resource_type: Optional[str] = None,
        resource_id: Optional[str] = None,
        message: Optional[str] = None,
        user_id: Optional[str] = None,
        level: str = "INFO",
        details: Optional[Dict] = None
    ) -> SystemLog:
        """记录系统日志"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            log = SystemLog(
                tenant_id=tenant_id,
                user_id=user_id,
                log_level=level,
                action=action,
                resource_type=resource_type,
                resource_id=resource_id,
                message=message,
                details=details
            )
            session.add(log)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(log)
            return log
    
    async def get_recent_logs(
        self,
        tenant_id: str,
        level: Optional[str] = None,
        limit: int = 100
    ) -> List[SystemLog]:
        """获取最近日志"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            stmt = select(SystemLog).where(
                SystemLog.tenant_id == tenant_id
            )
            if level:
                stmt = stmt.where(SystemLog.log_level == level)
            stmt = stmt.order_by(SystemLog.created_at.desc()).limit(limit)
            result = await session.execute(stmt)
            return result.scalars().all()
    
    # ========== AI 智能运维 ==========
    async def optimize_schedule(
        self,
        task_id: str,
        tenant_id: str
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """智能优化定时任务"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            task = await session.get(ScheduledTask, task_id)
            if not task:
                raise ValueError("任务不存在")
            
            # 分析历史执行记录
            # 简化实现
            return {
                "task_id": task_id,
                "current_schedule": task.cron_expression,
                "recommended_schedule": "0 3 * * *",
                "reason": "该时段系统负载最低"
            }
    
    async def diagnose_issue(
        self,
        description: str,
        tenant_id: str
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """智能诊断"""
        prompt = f"""
请诊断以下系统问题：
{description}

输出 JSON：
{{
    "cause": "根本原因",
    "solution": "解决方案",
    "steps": ["步骤1", "步骤2"]
}}
"""
        backend = model_registry.get()
        response = await backend.generate([{"role": "user", "content": prompt}])
        
        try:
            import re
            match = re.search(r'\{[\s\S]*\}', response)
            if match:
                return json.loads(match.group(0))
        except:
            pass
        
        return {
            "cause": "未知",
            "solution": "请联系技术支持",
            "steps": ["查看详细日志"]
        }
ai_service.py
python
"""
AI 服务封装 - 统一 AI 能力调用
"""

from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional, AsyncIterator
from app.core.ai_digital_base.gateway import ai_gateway, ChatRequest
from app.core.ai_digital_base.rag import rag_service
from app.core.agent_engine.orchestrator import get_agent_orchestrator


class AIService:
    """AI 服务封装"""
    
    async def chat(
        self,
        messages: List[Dict],
        tenant_id: str,
        user_id: str,
        context_type: Optional[str] = None,
        context_ref: Optional[str] = None,
        stream: bool = True,
        model: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
        """对话接口"""
        request = ChatRequest(
            messages=messages,
            model=model or "deepseek-chat",
            stream=stream,
            context_type=context_type,
            context_ref=context_ref,
            tenant_id=tenant_id,
            user_id=user_id
        )
        async for chunk in ai_gateway.chat_stream(request):
            yield chunk
    
    async def search_knowledge(
        self,
        query: str,
        tenant_id: str,
        context_type: Optional[str] = None,
        top_k: int = 5
    ) -> List[str]:
        """知识库检索"""
        return await rag_service.search(
            query=query,
            tenant_id=tenant_id,
            context_type=context_type,
            top_k=top_k
        )
    
    async def execute_agent(
        self,
        user_request: str,
        tenant_id: str,
        user_id: str,
        conversation_id: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """执行智能体"""
        orchestrator = get_agent_orchestrator()
        return await orchestrator.execute(
            user_request=user_request,
            context={
                "tenant_id": tenant_id,
                "user_id": user_id,
                "conversation_id": conversation_id
            }
        )
migration_service.py
python
"""
数据迁移服务 - 支持开发 SQLite 到生产 MySQL/PostgreSQL 迁移
"""

import asyncio
from typing import Dict, List, Any, Optional, Callable
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import create_async_engine, AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy import text, inspect

from app.database import Base, AsyncSessionLocal
from app.config import settings


class MigrationService:
    """数据迁移服务"""
    
    async def preview(
        self,
        source_url: str,
        target_url: str
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """预览迁移内容"""
        source_engine = create_async_engine(source_url)
        
        tables = []
        total_records = 0
        
        async with source_engine.connect() as conn:
            # 获取表列表
            if "sqlite" in source_url:
                result = await conn.execute(text("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'"))
            else:
                result = await conn.execute(text("SELECT table_name FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_schema='public'"))
            
            table_names = [row[0] for row in result.fetchall()]
            
            for table in table_names:
                count_result = await conn.execute(text(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {table}"))
                count = count_result.scalar()
                total_records += count
                tables.append({"name": table, "records": count})
        
        await source_engine.dispose()
        
        return {
            "total_tables": len(tables),
            "total_records": total_records,
            "tables": tables
        }
    
    async def execute(
        self,
        source_url: str,
        target_url: str,
        progress_callback: Optional[Callable] = None
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """执行迁移"""
        source_engine = create_async_engine(source_url)
        target_engine = create_async_engine(target_url)
        
        # 在目标数据库创建表
        async with target_engine.begin() as conn:
            await conn.run_sync(Base.metadata.create_all)
        
        # 迁移数据
        async with source_engine.connect() as src_conn:
            # 获取表列表
            if "sqlite" in source_url:
                result = await src_conn.execute(text("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'"))
            else:
                result = await src_conn.execute(text("SELECT table_name FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_schema='public'"))
            
            table_names = [row[0] for row in result.fetchall()]
            
            total = len(table_names)
            migrated = 0
            errors = []
            
            for i, table in enumerate(table_names):
                try:
                    # 读取源数据
                    rows_result = await src_conn.execute(text(f"SELECT * FROM {table}"))
                    rows = rows_result.fetchall()
                    
                    if rows:
                        columns = rows_result.keys()
                        
                        async with target_engine.connect() as tgt_conn:
                            # 清空目标表（可选）
                            await tgt_conn.execute(text(f"DELETE FROM {table}"))
                            
                            # 批量插入
                            for row in rows:
                                placeholders = ", ".join([f":{col}" for col in columns])
                                stmt = text(f"INSERT INTO {table} ({', '.join(columns)}) VALUES ({placeholders})")
                                await tgt_conn.execute(stmt, dict(zip(columns, row)))
                            
                            await tgt_conn.commit()
                    
                    migrated += 1
                    if progress_callback:
                        progress_callback((i + 1) / total * 100)
                        
                except Exception as e:
                    errors.append({"table": table, "error": str(e)})
        
        await source_engine.dispose()
        await target_engine.dispose()
        
        return {
            "success": len(errors) == 0,
            "migrated_tables": migrated,
            "total_tables": total,
            "errors": errors
        }
organization_service.py
python
"""
组织架构服务 - 对应原平台组织架构管理
"""

from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy import select, update

from app.database import AsyncSessionLocal
from app.models.organization import Organization, OrganizationMember, OrganizationType
from app.services.base import BaseService


class OrganizationService(BaseService[Organization]):
    """组织架构服务"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(Organization)
    
    async def create_organization(
        self,
        tenant_id: str,
        name: str,
        code: str,
        parent_id: Optional[str] = None,
        org_type: str = "department",
        manager_id: Optional[str] = None,
        user_id: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> Organization:
        """创建组织"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            # 计算路径和层级
            path = code
            level = 1
            if parent_id:
                parent = await session.get(Organization, parent_id)
                if parent:
                    path = f"{parent.path}/{code}"
                    level = parent.level + 1
            
            org = Organization(
                tenant_id=tenant_id,
                parent_id=parent_id,
                name=name,
                code=code,
                type=OrganizationType(org_type),
                manager_id=manager_id,
                path=path,
                level=level,
                created_by=user_id
            )
            session.add(org)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(org)
            return org
    
    async def get_organization_tree(self, tenant_id: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """获取组织架构树"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            stmt = select(Organization).where(
                Organization.tenant_id == tenant_id,
                Organization.is_deleted == False
            ).order_by(Organization.sort_order)
            result = await session.execute(stmt)
            orgs = result.scalars().all()
            
            return self._build_tree(orgs)
    
    def _build_tree(self, orgs: List[Organization], parent_id: Optional[str] = None) -> List[Dict]:
        tree = []
        for org in orgs:
            if str(org.parent_id) == str(parent_id) if parent_id else org.parent_id is None:
                node = {
                    "id": str(org.id),
                    "name": org.name,
                    "code": org.code,
                    "type": org.type.value if org.type else None,
                    "path": org.path,
                    "level": org.level,
                    "children": self._build_tree(orgs, str(org.id))
                }
                tree.append(node)
        return tree
    
    async def add_member(
        self,
        organization_id: str,
        user_id: str,
        position: Optional[str] = None,
        is_leader: bool = False
    ) -> OrganizationMember:
        """添加组织成员"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            member = OrganizationMember(
                organization_id=organization_id,
                user_id=user_id,
                position=position,
                is_leader=is_leader
            )
            session.add(member)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(member)
            return member
    
    async def get_members(self, organization_id: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """获取组织成员"""
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
            stmt = select(OrganizationMember).where(
                OrganizationMember.organization_id == organization_id,
                OrganizationMember.is_deleted == False
            )
            result = await session.execute(stmt)
            members = result.scalars().all()
            
            return [
                {
                    "id": str(m.id),
                    "user_id": str(m.user_id),
                    "position": m.position,
                    "is_leader": m.is_leader
                }
                for m in members
            ]

deps.py
python
"""
依赖注入 - 认证、权限、数据库会话
"""

from typing import Optional, Annotated
from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status, Header
from fastapi.security import HTTPBearer, HTTPAuthorizationCredentials
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from jose import JWTError, jwt

from app.database import get_db
from app.config import settings
from app.models.user import User, UserStatus
from app.services.permission_service import PermissionService

security = HTTPBearer(auto_error=False)


async def get_current_user(
    credentials: Optional[HTTPAuthorizationCredentials] = Depends(security),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)
) -> Optional[User]:
    """获取当前登录用户"""
    if not credentials:
        return None
    
    token = credentials.credentials
    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(token, settings.SECRET_KEY, algorithms=[settings.ALGORITHM])
        user_id: str = payload.get("sub")
        if not user_id:
            return None
    except JWTError:
        return None
    
    from sqlalchemy import select
    stmt = select(User).where(User.id == user_id, User.is_deleted == False)
    result = await db.execute(stmt)
    user = result.scalar_one_or_none()
    
    if user and user.status == UserStatus.ACTIVE:
        return user
    return None


async def get_current_active_user(
    current_user: Annotated[Optional[User], Depends(get_current_user)]
) -> User:
    """获取当前活跃用户（必须登录）"""
    if not current_user:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="未认证或令牌已过期"
        )
    return current_user


async def get_current_superuser(
    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_active_user)]
) -> User:
    """获取超级用户"""
    if not current_user.is_superuser:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="权限不足"
        )
    return current_user


async def get_tenant_id(
    x_tenant_id: Optional[str] = Header(None),
    current_user: Optional[User] = Depends(get_current_user)
) -> str:
    """获取租户 ID"""
    if x_tenant_id:
        return x_tenant_id
    if current_user:
        return current_user.tenant_id
    return "default"


def require_permission(resource_type: str, action: str):
    """权限检查装饰器工厂"""
    async def _check_permission(
        resource_id: Optional[str] = None,
        current_user: User = Depends(get_current_active_user),
        tenant_id: str = Depends(get_tenant_id),
        db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)
    ):
        perm_service = PermissionService()
        has_perm = await perm_service.check_permission(
            user_id=str(current_user.id),
            resource_type=resource_type,
            resource_id=resource_id or "*",
            action=action,
            tenant_id=tenant_id
        )
        if not has_perm:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                detail=f"缺少权限: {resource_type}.{action}"
            )
        return True
    return _check_permission


# 常用依赖
DBDep = Annotated[AsyncSession, Depends(get_db)]
CurrentUser = Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_active_user)]
TenantID = Annotated[str, Depends(get_tenant_id)]

v1/auth.py
python
"""
认证接口
"""

from datetime import timedelta, datetime
from typing import Optional
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status, Request
from fastapi.security import OAuth2PasswordRequestForm
from pydantic import BaseModel, EmailStr
from sqlalchemy import select
from jose import jwt
from passlib.context import CryptContext

from app.database import AsyncSessionLocal
from app.models.user import User, UserStatus, UserSession, UserLoginLog
from app.api.deps import DBDep, CurrentUser
from app.config import settings
from app.utils.security import create_access_token, create_refresh_token, verify_password

router = APIRouter()
pwd_context = CryptContext(schemes=["bcrypt"], deprecated="auto")


class LoginRequest(BaseModel):
    username: str
    password: str
    tenant_id: Optional[str] = None


class LoginResponse(BaseModel):
    access_token: str
    refresh_token: str
    token_type: str = "bearer"
    expires_in: int
    user: dict


class RegisterRequest(BaseModel):
    username: str
    email: EmailStr
    password: str
    full_name: str
    phone: Optional[str] = None
    tenant_id: Optional[str] = None


class TokenRefreshRequest(BaseModel):
    refresh_token: str


class ChangePasswordRequest(BaseModel):
    old_password: str
    new_password: str


@router.post("/login", response_model=LoginResponse)
async def login(
    request: Request,
    form_data: OAuth2PasswordRequestForm = Depends(),
    tenant_id: Optional[str] = None,
    db: DBDep = None
):
    """用户登录"""
    # 查找用户
    stmt = select(User).where(
        User.username == form_data.username,
        User.is_deleted == False
    )
    if tenant_id:
        stmt = stmt.where(User.tenant_id == tenant_id)
    
    result = await db.execute(stmt)
    user = result.scalar_one_or_none()
    
    if not user or not verify_password(form_data.password, user.hashed_password):
        # 记录登录失败日志
        await _log_login(user, request, False, "用户名或密码错误", db)
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="用户名或密码错误"
        )
    
    if user.status != UserStatus.ACTIVE:
        await _log_login(user, request, False, f"账户状态异常: {user.status}", db)
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail=f"账户状态异常: {user.status.value}"
        )
    
    # 生成令牌
    access_token = create_access_token(
        subject=str(user.id),
        expires_delta=timedelta(minutes=settings.ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES)
    )
    refresh_token = create_refresh_token(subject=str(user.id))
    
    # 保存会话
    session = UserSession(
        user_id=user.id,
        token=access_token,
        refresh_token=refresh_token,
        expires_at=datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(minutes=settings.ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES),
        ip_address=request.client.host,
        user_agent=request.headers.get("user-agent"),
        device_type=_get_device_type(request.headers.get("user-agent", ""))
    )
    db.add(session)
    
    # 更新最后登录信息
    user.last_login_at = datetime.utcnow()
    user.last_login_ip = request.client.host
    
    await db.commit()
    
    # 记录登录成功日志
    await _log_login(user, request, True, None, db)
    
    return LoginResponse(
        access_token=access_token,
        refresh_token=refresh_token,
        expires_in=settings.ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES * 60,
        user={
            "id": str(user.id),
            "username": user.username,
            "email": user.email,
            "full_name": user.full_name,
            "avatar_url": user.avatar_url,
            "tenant_id": user.tenant_id,
            "is_superuser": user.is_superuser,
            "is_tenant_admin": user.is_tenant_admin
        }
    )


@router.post("/register", response_model=LoginResponse)
async def register(data: RegisterRequest, request: Request, db: DBDep = None):
    """用户注册"""
    # 检查用户名是否已存在
    stmt = select(User).where(User.username == data.username)
    result = await db.execute(stmt)
    if result.scalar_one_or_none():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="用户名已存在")
    
    # 检查邮箱是否已存在
    stmt = select(User).where(User.email == data.email)
    result = await db.execute(stmt)
    if result.scalar_one_or_none():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="邮箱已被注册")
    
    # 创建用户
    user = User(
        tenant_id=data.tenant_id or "default",
        username=data.username,
        email=data.email,
        phone=data.phone,
        full_name=data.full_name,
        hashed_password=pwd_context.hash(data.password),
        status=UserStatus.ACTIVE
    )
    db.add(user)
    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(user)
    
    # 自动登录
    access_token = create_access_token(subject=str(user.id))
    refresh_token = create_refresh_token(subject=str(user.id))
    
    return LoginResponse(
        access_token=access_token,
        refresh_token=refresh_token,
        expires_in=settings.ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES * 60,
        user={
            "id": str(user.id),
            "username": user.username,
            "email": user.email,
            "full_name": user.full_name,
            "tenant_id": user.tenant_id
        }
    )


@router.post("/refresh")
async def refresh_token(data: TokenRefreshRequest, db: DBDep = None):
    """刷新访问令牌"""
    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(data.refresh_token, settings.SECRET_KEY, algorithms=[settings.ALGORITHM])
        user_id = payload.get("sub")
        if not user_id:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="无效的刷新令牌")
    except JWTError:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="无效的刷新令牌")
    
    user = await db.get(User, user_id)
    if not user or user.status != UserStatus.ACTIVE:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="用户不存在或已禁用")
    
    access_token = create_access_token(subject=user_id)
    return {"access_token": access_token, "token_type": "bearer", "expires_in": settings.ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES * 60}


@router.post("/logout")
async def logout(current_user: CurrentUser, db: DBDep = None):
    """退出登录"""
    from sqlalchemy import update
    stmt = update(UserSession).where(
        UserSession.user_id == current_user.id,
        UserSession.is_active == True
    ).values(is_active=False)
    await db.execute(stmt)
    await db.commit()
    return {"message": "退出成功"}


@router.post("/change-password")
async def change_password(data: ChangePasswordRequest, current_user: CurrentUser, db: DBDep = None):
    """修改密码"""
    if not verify_password(data.old_password, current_user.hashed_password):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="原密码错误")
    
    current_user.hashed_password = pwd_context.hash(data.new_password)
    current_user.password_changed_at = datetime.utcnow()
    current_user.must_change_password = False
    await db.commit()
    
    return {"message": "密码修改成功"}


@router.get("/me")
async def get_current_user_info(current_user: CurrentUser):
    """获取当前用户信息"""
    return {
        "id": str(current_user.id),
        "username": current_user.username,
        "email": current_user.email,
        "phone": current_user.phone,
        "full_name": current_user.full_name,
        "avatar_url": current_user.avatar_url,
        "tenant_id": current_user.tenant_id,
        "is_superuser": current_user.is_superuser,
        "is_tenant_admin": current_user.is_tenant_admin,
        "preferences": current_user.preferences
    }


async def _log_login(user: Optional[User], request: Request, success: bool, fail_reason: Optional[str], db: AsyncSessionLocal):
    log = UserLoginLog(
        user_id=user.id if user else None,
        login_at=datetime.utcnow(),
        ip_address=request.client.host,
        user_agent=request.headers.get("user-agent"),
        is_success=success,
        fail_reason=fail_reason
    )
    db.add(log)


def _get_device_type(user_agent: str) -> str:
    ua = user_agent.lower()
    if "mobile" in ua or "android" in ua or "iphone" in ua:
        return "mobile"
    elif "tablet" in ua or "ipad" in ua:
        return "tablet"
    return "desktop"
v1/users.py
python
"""
用户管理接口
"""

from typing import List, Optional
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query
from pydantic import BaseModel, EmailStr
from sqlalchemy import select, func

from app.api.deps import DBDep, CurrentUser, TenantID
from app.models.user import User, UserStatus
from app.services.permission_service import PermissionService

router = APIRouter()


class UserCreate(BaseModel):
    username: str
    email: EmailStr
    full_name: str
    password: str
    phone: Optional[str] = None
    tenant_id: Optional[str] = None
    is_tenant_admin: bool = False


class UserUpdate(BaseModel):
    email: Optional[EmailStr] = None
    full_name: Optional[str] = None
    phone: Optional[str] = None
    status: Optional[UserStatus] = None
    is_tenant_admin: Optional[bool] = None


class UserResponse(BaseModel):
    id: str
    username: str
    email: str
    full_name: str
    phone: Optional[str]
    avatar_url: Optional[str]
    status: str
    is_tenant_admin: bool
    last_login_at: Optional[str]
    created_at: str


@router.get("/", response_model=dict)
async def list_users(
    db: DBDep,
    current_user: CurrentUser,
    tenant_id: TenantID,
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),
    keyword: Optional[str] = None,
    status: Optional[UserStatus] = None
):
    """获取用户列表"""
    stmt = select(User).where(
        User.tenant_id == tenant_id,
        User.is_deleted == False
    )
    
    if keyword:
        stmt = stmt.where(
            (User.username.contains(keyword)) |
            (User.email.contains(keyword)) |
            (User.full_name.contains(keyword))
        )
    
    if status:
        stmt = stmt.where(User.status == status)
    
    # 计数
    count_stmt = select(func.count()).select_from(stmt.subquery())
    total = await db.scalar(count_stmt)
    
    # 分页
    stmt = stmt.offset((page - 1) * page_size).limit(page_size)
    result = await db.execute(stmt)
    users = result.scalars().all()
    
    return {
        "total": total,
        "page": page,
        "page_size": page_size,
        "items": [
            {
                "id": str(u.id),
                "username": u.username,
                "email": u.email,
                "full_name": u.full_name,
                "phone": u.phone,
                "avatar_url": u.avatar_url,
                "status": u.status.value if u.status else None,
                "is_tenant_admin": u.is_tenant_admin,
                "last_login_at": u.last_login_at.isoformat() if u.last_login_at else None,
                "created_at": u.created_at.isoformat() if u.created_at else None
            }
            for u in users
        ]
    }


@router.post("/", response_model=UserResponse)
async def create_user(
    data: UserCreate,
    db: DBDep,
    current_user: CurrentUser,
    tenant_id: TenantID
):
    """创建用户"""
    # 检查权限
    perm_service = PermissionService()
    if not await perm_service.check_permission(str(current_user.id), "user", "*", "create", tenant_id):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="无权限创建用户")
    
    # 检查用户名
    stmt = select(User).where(User.username == data.username)
    result = await db.execute(stmt)
    if result.scalar_one_or_none():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="用户名已存在")
    
    from passlib.context import CryptContext
    pwd_context = CryptContext(schemes=["bcrypt"], deprecated="auto")
    
    user = User(
        tenant_id=data.tenant_id or tenant_id,
        username=data.username,
        email=data.email,
        full_name=data.full_name,
        phone=data.phone,
        hashed_password=pwd_context.hash(data.password),
        is_tenant_admin=data.is_tenant_admin,
        created_by=current_user.id
    )
    db.add(user)
    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(user)
    
    return _user_to_response(user)


@router.get("/{user_id}", response_model=UserResponse)
async def get_user(user_id: str, db: DBDep, current_user: CurrentUser, tenant_id: TenantID):
    """获取用户详情"""
    user = await db.get(User, user_id)
    if not user or user.tenant_id != tenant_id or user.is_deleted:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="用户不存在")
    return _user_to_response(user)


@router.put("/{user_id}", response_model=UserResponse)
async def update_user(user_id: str, data: UserUpdate, db: DBDep, current_user: CurrentUser, tenant_id: TenantID):
    """更新用户"""
    user = await db.get(User, user_id)
    if not user or user.tenant_id != tenant_id or user.is_deleted:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="用户不存在")
    
    update_data = data.dict(exclude_unset=True)
    for key, value in update_data.items():
        setattr(user, key, value)
    user.updated_by = current_user.id
    
    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(user)
    return _user_to_response(user)


@router.delete("/{user_id}")
async def delete_user(user_id: str, db: DBDep, current_user: CurrentUser, tenant_id: TenantID):
    """删除用户（软删除）"""
    user = await db.get(User, user_id)
    if not user or user.tenant_id != tenant_id or user.is_deleted:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="用户不存在")
    
    user.is_deleted = True
    user.updated_by = current_user.id
    await db.commit()
    
    return {"message": "删除成功"}


@router.post("/batch-import")
async def batch_import_users(file: UploadFile, db: DBDep, current_user: CurrentUser, tenant_id: TenantID):
    """批量导入用户（Excel/CSV）"""
    import pandas as pd
    content = await file.read()
    df = pd.read_excel(content) if file.filename.endswith('.xlsx') else pd.read_csv(content)
    
    success = 0
    errors = []
    
    for _, row in df.iterrows():
        try:
            # 创建用户逻辑
            pass
        except Exception as e:
            errors.append({"row": row.to_dict(), "error": str(e)})
    
    return {"success": success, "errors": errors}


def _user_to_response(user: User) -> dict:
    return {
        "id": str(user.id),
        "username": user.username,
        "email": user.email,
        "full_name": user.full_name,
        "phone": user.phone,
        "avatar_url": user.avatar_url,
        "status": user.status.value if user.status else None,
        "is_tenant_admin": user.is_tenant_admin,
        "last_login_at": user.last_login_at.isoformat() if user.last_login_at else None,
        "created_at": user.created_at.isoformat() if user.created_at else None
    }

"""
模板设计接口
"""

from typing import List, Optional
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query, UploadFile
from pydantic import BaseModel

from app.api.deps import DBDep, CurrentUser, TenantID
from app.services.template_service import TemplateService

router = APIRouter()
template_service = TemplateService()


class TemplateCreate(BaseModel):
    name: str
    code: str
    category_id: Optional[str] = None
    template_type: str = "form"
    description: Optional[str] = None


class TemplateUpdate(BaseModel):
    name: Optional[str] = None
    description: Optional[str] = None
    category_id: Optional[str] = None


class FieldCreate(BaseModel):
    name: str
    code: str
    field_type: str = "string"
    sheet_id: Optional[str] = None
    is_required: bool = False
    is_unique: bool = False
    data_norm_type: Optional[str] = None
    data_norm_config: Optional[dict] = None


class FormulaCreate(BaseModel):
    name: str
    code: str
    formula_type: str
    expression: str
    trigger_type: str = "manual"
    description: Optional[str] = None


@router.get("/categories")
async def get_categories(tenant_id: TenantID):
    """获取模板分类树"""
    return await template_service.get_category_tree(tenant_id)


@router.post("/categories")
async def create_category(
    name: str,
    code: str,
    parent_id: Optional[str] = None,
    tenant_id: TenantID = None,
    current_user: CurrentUser = None
):
    """创建模板分类"""
    return await template_service.create_category(tenant_id, name, code, parent_id)


@router.get("/")
async def list_templates(
    tenant_id: TenantID,
    category_id: Optional[str] = None,
    keyword: Optional[str] = None,
    page: int = 1,
    page_size: int = 20,
    db: DBDep = None
):
    """获取模板列表"""
    from sqlalchemy import select, func
    from app.models.template import Template
    
    stmt = select(Template).where(Template.tenant_id == tenant_id, Template.is_deleted == False)
    if category_id:
        stmt = stmt.where(Template.category_id == category_id)
    if keyword:
        stmt = stmt.where((Template.name.contains(keyword)) | (Template.code.contains(keyword)))
    
    count_stmt = select(func.count()).select_from(stmt.subquery())
    total = await db.scalar(count_stmt)
    
    stmt = stmt.offset((page - 1) * page_size).limit(page_size)
    result = await db.execute(stmt)
    templates = result.scalars().all()
    
    return {
        "total": total,
        "items": [
            {
                "id": str(t.id),
                "name": t.name,
                "code": t.code,
                "type": t.type.value if t.type else None,
                "is_published": t.is_published,
                "created_at": t.created_at.isoformat() if t.created_at else None
            }
            for t in templates
        ]
    }


@router.post("/")
async def create_template(data: TemplateCreate, tenant_id: TenantID, current_user: CurrentUser):
    """创建模板"""
    return await template_service.create_template(
        tenant_id=tenant_id,
        name=data.name,
        code=data.code,
        category_id=data.category_id,
        template_type=data.template_type,
        description=data.description,
        user_id=str(current_user.id)
    )


@router.get("/{template_id}")
async def get_template(template_id: str, tenant_id: TenantID):
    """获取模板详情"""
    template = await template_service.get_template_detail(template_id)
    if not template:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="模板不存在")
    return template


@router.put("/{template_id}")
async def update_template(template_id: str, data: TemplateUpdate, current_user: CurrentUser, db: DBDep = None):
    """更新模板"""
    from app.models.template import Template
    template = await db.get(Template, template_id)
    if not template:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="模板不存在")
    for k, v in data.dict(exclude_unset=True).items():
        setattr(template, k, v)
    template.updated_by = current_user.id
    await db.commit()
    return {"message": "更新成功"}


@router.delete("/{template_id}")
async def delete_template(template_id: str, current_user: CurrentUser, db: DBDep = None):
    """删除模板"""

    if not template:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="模板不存在")
    template.is_deleted = True
    template.updated_by = current_user.id
    await db.commit()
    return {"message": "删除成功"}

@router.get("/{template_id}/fields")
async def get_fields(template_id: str, db: DBDep = None):
    """获取模板字段列表"""
    from sqlalchemy import select
    from app.models.template import TemplateField
    
    stmt = select(TemplateField).where(
        TemplateField.template_id == template_id,
        TemplateField.is_deleted == False
    ).order_by(TemplateField.sort_order)
    result = await db.execute(stmt)
    return result.scalars().all()

    )


@router.put("/fields/{field_id}")
async def update_field(field_id: str, data: dict, current_user: CurrentUser, db: DBDep = None):
    """更新字段"""
    from app.models.template import TemplateField
    field = await db.get(TemplateField, field_id)
    if not field:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="字段不存在")
    for k, v in data.items():
        setattr(field, k, v)
    field.updated_by = current_user.id
    await db.commit()
    return field


@router.delete("/fields/{field_id}")
async def delete_field(field_id: str, current_user: CurrentUser):
    """删除字段"""
    await template_service.delete_field(field_id)
    return {"message": "删除成功"}


@router.post("/{template_id}/formulas")
async def create_formula(template_id: str, data: FormulaCreate, current_user: CurrentUser):

        template_id=template_id,

        code=data.code,

        expression=data.expression,
        trigger_type=data.trigger_type,

@router.post("/ai/generate")
async def ai_generate_template(description: str, name: Optional[str] = None, tenant_id: TenantID = None, current_user: CurrentUser = None):

        user_id=str(current_user.id),
        name=name
    )
v1/workflows.py

"""
流程审批接口
"""

from typing import List, Optional
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query
from pydantic import BaseModel

from app.api.deps import DBDep, CurrentUser, TenantID
from app.services.workflow_service import WorkflowService

router = APIRouter()
workflow_service = WorkflowService()


class WorkflowCreate(BaseModel):
    name: str
    code: str
    template_id: str
    description: Optional[str] = None


class WorkflowDesign(BaseModel):
    nodes: List[dict]
    edges: List[dict]
    bpmn_xml: Optional[str] = None


class StartProcessRequest(BaseModel):
    workflow_code: str
    form_data: dict
    title: Optional[str] = None


class ApprovalRequest(BaseModel):
    comment: Optional[str] = None
    attachments: Optional[List[str]] = None


@router.get("/")
async def list_workflows(tenant_id: TenantID, db: DBDep = None, page: int = 1, page_size: int = 20):
    """获取流程列表"""
    from sqlalchemy import select, func
    from app.models.workflow import Workflow
    
    stmt = select(Workflow).where(Workflow.tenant_id == tenant_id, Workflow.is_deleted == False)
    count_stmt = select(func.count()).select_from(stmt.subquery())
    total = await db.scalar(count_stmt)
    
    stmt = stmt.offset((page - 1) * page_size).limit(page_size)
    result = await db.execute(stmt)
    workflows = result.scalars().all()
    
    return {
        "total": total,
        "items": [
            {"id": str(w.id), "name": w.name, "code": w.code, "status": w.status.value if w.status else None}
            for w in workflows
        ]
    }


@router.post("/")
async def create_workflow(data: WorkflowCreate, tenant_id: TenantID, current_user: CurrentUser):
    """创建流程"""
    return await workflow_service.create_workflow(
        tenant_id=tenant_id,
        name=data.name,
        code=data.code,
        template_id=data.template_id,
        description=data.description,
        user_id=str(current_user.id)
    )


@router.get("/{workflow_id}")
async def get_workflow(workflow_id: str):
    """获取流程详情"""
    detail = await workflow_service.get_workflow_detail(workflow_id)
    if not detail:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="流程不存在")
    return detail


@router.put("/{workflow_id}/design")
async def save_design(workflow_id: str, data: WorkflowDesign):
    """保存流程图设计"""
    return await workflow_service.save_workflow_design(
        workflow_id=workflow_id,
        nodes=data.nodes,
        edges=data.edges,
        bpmn_xml=data.bpmn_xml
    )


@router.post("/{workflow_id}/publish")
async def publish_workflow(workflow_id: str):
    """发布流程"""
    return await workflow_service.publish_workflow(workflow_id)


@router.post("/start")
async def start_process(data: StartProcessRequest, tenant_id: TenantID, current_user: CurrentUser):
    """发起流程"""
    return await workflow_service.start_process(
        workflow_code=data.workflow_code,
        form_data=data.form_data,
        initiator_id=str(current_user.id),
        tenant_id=tenant_id,
        title=data.title
    )


@router.get("/tasks/pending")
async def get_pending_tasks(tenant_id: TenantID, current_user: CurrentUser, limit: int = 20):
    """获取待办任务"""
    tasks = await workflow_service.get_pending_tasks(str(current_user.id), tenant_id, limit)
    return [
        {
            "id": str(t.id),
            "node_name": t.node_name,
            "process_title": t.instance.title if t.instance else None,
            "created_at": t.created_at.isoformat() if t.created_at else None
        }
        for t in tasks
    ]


@router.get("/tasks/{task_id}")
async def get_task_detail(task_id: str, db: DBDep = None):
    """获取任务详情"""
    from app.models.workflow import ProcessTask
    task = await db.get(ProcessTask, task_id)
    if not task:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="任务不存在")
    return {
        "id": str(task.id),
        "node_name": task.node_name,
        "status": task.status.value,
        "form_snapshot": task.form_snapshot,
        "ai_suggestions": task.ai_suggestions
    }


@router.post("/tasks/{task_id}/approve")
async def approve_task(task_id: str, data: ApprovalRequest, current_user: CurrentUser):
    """审批通过"""
    return await workflow_service.approve_task(task_id, str(current_user.id), data.comment)


@router.post("/tasks/{task_id}/reject")
async def reject_task(task_id: str, data: ApprovalRequest, current_user: CurrentUser):
    """审批拒绝"""
    return await workflow_service.reject_task(task_id, str(current_user.id), data.comment)


@router.post("/tasks/{task_id}/transfer")
async def transfer_task(task_id: str, target_user_id: str, comment: Optional[str] = None, current_user: CurrentUser = None):
    """转交任务"""
    return await workflow_service.transfer_task(task_id, str(current_user.id), target_user_id, comment)


@router.post("/tasks/batch-approve")
async def batch_approve(task_ids: List[str], comment: Optional[str] = None, current_user: CurrentUser = None):
    """批量审批"""
    return await workflow_service.batch_approve(task_ids, str(current_user.id), comment)


@router.get("/tasks/{task_id}/ai-suggestion")
async def get_ai_suggestion(task_id: str, tenant_id: TenantID):
    """获取 AI 审批建议"""
    return await workflow_service.generate_approval_suggestion(task_id, tenant_id)

from typing import Optional
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends

class DataSourceCreate(BaseModel):
    name: str
    code: str
    source_type: str
    connection_config: dict


class APIIntegrationCreate(BaseModel):
    name: str
    code: str
    base_url: str
    auth_type: str = "none"
    auth_config: Optional[dict] = None
    headers: Optional[dict] = None


@router.get("/datasources")
async def list_datasources(tenant_id: TenantID, db: DBDep = None):
    """获取数据源列表"""
    from sqlalchemy import select
    from app.models.integration import ExternalDataSource

        ExternalDataSource.tenant_id == tenant_id,
        ExternalDataSource.is_deleted == False
    )
    result = await db.execute(stmt)
    sources = result.scalars().all()
    return [{"id": str(s.id), "name": s.name, "code": s.code, "type": s.source_type.value} for s in sources]

        tenant_id=tenant_id,
        name=data.name,
        code=data.code,
        source_type=data.source_type,
        connection_config=data.connection_config,
        user_id=str(current_user.id)
    )

    return await system_service.optimize_schedule(task_id, tenant_id)


@router.get("/logs")
async def get_logs(tenant_id: TenantID, level: str = None, limit: int = 100, db: DBDep = None):
    """获取系统日志"""
    logs = await system_service.get_recent_logs(tenant_id, level, limit)
    return [{"time": l.created_at, "level": l.log_level, "message": l.message} for l in logs]


@router.post("/diagnose")
async def diagnose(description: str, tenant_id: TenantID):
    """智能诊断"""
    return await system_service.diagnose_issue(description, tenant_id)
v1/migration.py
python
"""
数据迁移接口
"""

from fastapi import APIRouter, BackgroundTasks
from pydantic import BaseModel

from app.services.migration_service import MigrationService

router = APIRouter()
migration_service = MigrationService()


class MigrationConfig(BaseModel):
    source_url: str
    target_url: str


_tasks = {}


@router.post("/preview")
async def preview(config: MigrationConfig):
    """预览迁移"""
    return await migration_service.preview(config.source_url, config.target_url)


@router.post("/execute")
async def execute(config: MigrationConfig, background_tasks: BackgroundTasks):
    """执行迁移"""
    import uuid
    task_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    _tasks[task_id] = {"status": "running", "progress": 0}
    
    def progress_callback(p):
        _tasks[task_id]["progress"] = p
    
    background_tasks.add_task(
        migration_service.execute,
        config.source_url,
        config.target_url,
        progress_callback
    )
    return {"task_id": task_id}


@router.get("/status/{task_id}")
async def get_status(task_id: str):
    """获取迁移状态"""
    return _tasks.get(task_id, {"status": "not_found"})
v1/ai.py
python
"""
AI 服务接口
"""

from typing import List, Optional
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends
from fastapi.responses import StreamingResponse
from pydantic import BaseModel

from app.api.deps import CurrentUser, TenantID
from app.services.ai_service import AIService

router = APIRouter()
ai_service = AIService()


class ChatRequest(BaseModel):
    messages: List[dict]
    model: Optional[str] = None
    stream: bool = True
    context_type: Optional[str] = None
    context_ref: Optional[str] = None


class AgentRequest(BaseModel):
    request: str
    conversation_id: Optional[str] = None


@router.post("/chat/stream")
async def chat_stream(
    data: ChatRequest,
    tenant_id: TenantID,
    current_user: CurrentUser
):
    """流式对话"""
    return StreamingResponse(
        ai_service.chat(
            messages=data.messages,
            tenant_id=tenant_id,
            user_id=str(current_user.id),
            context_type=data.context_type,
            context_ref=data.context_ref,
            stream=data.stream,
            model=data.model
        ),
        media_type="text/event-stream"
    )


@router.post("/chat")
async def chat_sync(data: ChatRequest, tenant_id: TenantID, current_user: CurrentUser):
    """同步对话"""
    full_response = ""
    async for chunk in ai_service.chat(
        messages=data.messages,
        tenant_id=tenant_id,
        user_id=str(current_user.id),
        context_type=data.context_type,
        stream=False,
        model=data.model
    ):
        full_response += chunk
    return {"response": full_response}


@router.post("/agent/execute")
async def execute_agent(data: AgentRequest, tenant_id: TenantID, current_user: CurrentUser):
    """执行智能体"""
    return await ai_service.execute_agent(
        user_request=data.request,
        tenant_id=tenant_id,
        user_id=str(current_user.id),
        conversation_id=data.conversation_id
    )


@router.get("/knowledge/search")
async def search_knowledge(query: str, tenant_id: TenantID, context_type: Optional[str] = None):
    """知识库检索"""
    results = await ai_service.search_knowledge(query, tenant_id, context_type)
    return {"results": results}

frontend/src/main.ts
typescript
import { isMobile } from '@shared/utils/device'

async function bootstrap() {
  const useMobile = isMobile()

  if (useMobile) {
    // 移动端
    const { default: MobileApp } = await import('@app/App.vue')
    const { default: mobileRouter } = await import('@app/router')
    const { createPinia } = await import('pinia')
    const { createApp } = await import('vue')
    
    const app = createApp(MobileApp)
    app.use(createPinia())
    app.use(mobileRouter)
    app.mount('#app')
    document.documentElement.classList.add('mobile-mode')
  } else {
    // PC 端
    const { default: PCApp } = await import('@pc/App.vue')
    const { default: pcRouter } = await import('@pc/router')
    const { createPinia } = await import('pinia')
    const { createApp } = await import('vue')
    
    // 引入 Element Plus 样式
    await import('element-plus/dist/index.css')
    await import('@pc/assets/styles/index.scss')
    
    const app = createApp(PCApp)
    app.use(createPinia())
    app.use(pcRouter)
    app.mount('#app')
    document.documentElement.classList.add('pc-mode')
  }
}

bootstrap()
frontend/src/shared/utils/device.ts
typescript
export function isMobile(): boolean {
  const ua = navigator.userAgent.toLowerCase()
  const mobileRegex = /android|webos|iphone|ipad|ipod|blackberry|iemobile|opera mini/i
  const uaMobile = mobileRegex.test(ua)
  const screenMobile = window.innerWidth < 768
  return uaMobile || screenMobile
}

export function getDeviceType(): 'mobile' | 'tablet' | 'desktop' {
  const width = window.innerWidth
  if (width < 768) return 'mobile'
  if (width < 1024) return 'tablet'
  return 'desktop'
}
共享模块（API、Store、Types）
frontend/src/shared/api/request.ts
typescript
import axios from 'axios'
import type { AxiosInstance, AxiosRequestConfig, AxiosResponse } from 'axios'
import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'
import { useUserStore } from '@shared/stores/user'

const service: AxiosInstance = axios.create({
  baseURL: import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL,
  timeout: 30000
})

// 请求拦截器
service.interceptors.request.use(
  (config) => {
    const userStore = useUserStore()
    if (userStore.token) {
      config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${userStore.token}`
    }
    if (userStore.tenantId) {
      config.headers['X-Tenant-Id'] = userStore.tenantId
    }
    return config
  },
  (error) => {
    return Promise.reject(error)
  }
)

// 响应拦截器
service.interceptors.response.use(
  (response: AxiosResponse) => {
    const res = response.data
    if (response.status !== 200) {
      ElMessage.error(res.message || '请求失败')
      return Promise.reject(new Error(res.message || 'Error'))
    }
    return res
  },
  (error) => {
    if (error.response?.status === 401) {
      const userStore = useUserStore()
      userStore.logout()
      window.location.href = '/login'
    } else {
      ElMessage.error(error.response?.data?.detail || error.message || '网络错误')
    }
    return Promise.reject(error)
  }
)

export default service
frontend/src/shared/api/auth.ts
typescript
import request from './request'

export interface LoginParams {
  username: string
  password: string
  tenant_id?: string
}

export interface LoginResponse {
  access_token: string
  refresh_token: string
  token_type: string
  expires_in: number
  user: {
    id: string
    username: string
    email: string
    full_name: string
    avatar_url?: string
    tenant_id: string
    is_superuser: boolean
    is_tenant_admin: boolean
  }
}

export const authApi = {
  login(data: LoginParams) {
    return request.post<LoginResponse>('/auth/login', data, {
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' },
      transformRequest: [(data) => new URLSearchParams(data as any).toString()]
    })
  },
  logout() {
    return request.post('/auth/logout')
  },
  refreshToken(refreshToken: string) {
    return request.post<{ access_token: string }>('/auth/refresh', { refresh_token: refreshToken })
  },
  getCurrentUser() {
    return request.get('/auth/me')
  },
  changePassword(oldPassword: string, newPassword: string) {
    return request.post('/auth/change-password', { old_password: oldPassword, new_password: newPassword })
  }
}
frontend/src/shared/api/template.ts
typescript
import request from './request'

export interface Template {
  id: string
  name: string
  code: string
  type: string
  description?: string
  is_published: boolean
  created_at: string
}

export interface TemplateField {
  id: string
  name: string
  code: string
  field_type: string
  is_required: boolean
  sort_order: number
}

export const templateApi = {
  // 分类
  getCategories() {
    return request.get('/templates/categories')
  },
  createCategory(data: { name: string; code: string; parent_id?: string }) {
    return request.post('/templates/categories', null, { params: data })
  },
  
  // 模板
  list(params: { page?: number; page_size?: number; keyword?: string; category_id?: string }) {
    return request.get('/templates', { params })
  },
  get(id: string) {
    return request.get(`/templates/${id}`)
  },
  create(data: { name: string; code: string; category_id?: string; template_type?: string }) {
    return request.post('/templates', data)
  },
  update(id: string, data: Partial<Template>) {
    return request.put(`/templates/${id}`, data)
  },
  delete(id: string) {
    return request.delete(`/templates/${id}`)
  },
  publish(id: string) {
    return request.post(`/templates/${id}/publish`)
  },
  copy(id: string, newName: string) {
    return request.post(`/templates/${id}/copy`, null, { params: { new_name: newName } })
  },
  
  // 字段
  getFields(templateId: string) {
    return request.get(`/templates/${templateId}/fields`)
  },
  addField(templateId: string, data: any) {
    return request.post(`/templates/${templateId}/fields`, data)
  },
  updateField(fieldId: string, data: any) {
    return request.put(`/templates/fields/${fieldId}`, data)
  },
  deleteField(fieldId: string) {
    return request.delete(`/templates/fields/${fieldId}`)
  },
  
  // AI 生成
  aiGenerate(description: string, name?: string) {
    return request.post('/templates/ai/generate', null, { params: { description, name } })
  }
}
frontend/src/shared/api/ai.ts
typescript
import request from './request'

export interface ChatMessage {
  role: 'user' | 'assistant' | 'system'
  content: string
}

export const aiApi = {
  // 同步对话
  chat(messages: ChatMessage[], contextType?: string, model?: string) {
    return request.post('/ai/chat', {
      messages,
      context_type: contextType,
      model,
      stream: false
    })
  },
  
  // 流式对话（返回 fetch Response）
  async chatStream(messages: ChatMessage[], contextType?: string, model?: string) {
    const userStore = (await import('@shared/stores/user')).useUserStore()
    const response = await fetch(`${import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL}/ai/chat/stream`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${userStore.token}`,
        'X-Tenant-Id': userStore.tenantId
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        messages,
        context_type: contextType,
        model,
        stream: true
      })
    })
    return response
  },
  
  // 执行智能体
  executeAgent(request: string, conversationId?: string) {
    return request.post('/ai/agent/execute', { request, conversation_id: conversationId })
  },
  
  // 知识库检索
  searchKnowledge(query: string, contextType?: string) {
    return request.get('/ai/knowledge/search', { params: { query, context_type: contextType } })
  }
}
frontend/src/shared/stores/user.ts
typescript
import { defineStore } from 'pinia'
import { ref, computed } from 'vue'
import { authApi } from '@shared/api/auth'
import { storage } from '@shared/utils/storage'

export const useUserStore = defineStore('user', () => {
  const token = ref<string>(storage.get('token') || '')
  const refreshToken = ref<string>(storage.get('refreshToken') || '')
  const userInfo = ref<any>(storage.get('userInfo') || null)
  
  const isLoggedIn = computed(() => !!token.value)
  const tenantId = computed(() => userInfo.value?.tenant_id || 'default')
  const userId = computed(() => userInfo.value?.id || '')
  
  async function login(params: { username: string; password: string; tenant_id?: string }) {
    const res = await authApi.login(params)
    token.value = res.access_token
    refreshToken.value = res.refresh_token
    userInfo.value = res.user
    
    storage.set('token', res.access_token)
    storage.set('refreshToken', res.refresh_token)
    storage.set('userInfo', res.user)
    
    return res
  }
  
  async function logout() {
    try {
      await authApi.logout()
    } finally {
      token.value = ''
      refreshToken.value = ''
      userInfo.value = null
      storage.clear()
    }
  }
  
  async function fetchUserInfo() {
    const res = await authApi.getCurrentUser()
    userInfo.value = res
    storage.set('userInfo', res)
    return res
  }
  
  return {
    token,
    refreshToken,
    userInfo,
    isLoggedIn,
    tenantId,
    userId,
    login,
    logout,
    fetchUserInfo
  }
})
frontend/src/shared/stores/app.ts
typescript
import { defineStore } from 'pinia'
import { ref } from 'vue'

export const useAppStore = defineStore('app', () => {
  const sidebarCollapsed = ref(false)
  const theme = ref<'light' | 'dark'>('light')
  const locale = ref('zh-CN')
  
  function toggleSidebar() {
    sidebarCollapsed.value = !sidebarCollapsed.value
  }
  
  function setTheme(val: 'light' | 'dark') {
    theme.value = val
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', val)
  }
  
  return {
    sidebarCollapsed,
    theme,
    locale,
    toggleSidebar,
    setTheme
  }
})
PC 端主应用
frontend/src/pc/App.vue
vue
<template>
  <el-config-provider :locale="zhCn">
    <router-view />
  </el-config-provider>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ElConfigProvider } from 'element-plus'
import zhCn from 'element-plus/es/locale/lang/zh-cn'

import { createRouter, createWebHistory } from 'vue-router'
import type { RouteRecordRaw } from 'vue-router'
import DefaultLayout from '@pc/layouts/DefaultLayout.vue'
import BlankLayout from '@pc/layouts/BlankLayout.vue'
import { useUserStore } from '@shared/stores/user'

export const routes: RouteRecordRaw[] = [
  {
    path: '/login',
    component: BlankLayout,
    children: [
      {
        path: '',
        name: 'Login',
        component: () => import('@pc/views/login/index.vue'),
        meta: { title: '登录', requiresAuth: false }
      }
    ]
  },
  {
    path: '/',
    component: DefaultLayout,
    redirect: '/dashboard',
    children: [
      {
        path: 'dashboard',
        name: 'Dashboard',
        component: () => import('@pc/views/dashboard/index.vue'),
        meta: { title: '工作台', icon: 'HomeFilled' }
      },
      {
        path: 'template',
        name: 'Template',
        component: () => import('@pc/views/template/index.vue'),
        meta: { title: '模板设计', icon: 'Document' }
      },
      {
        path: 'template/design/:id?',
        name: 'TemplateDesign',
        component: () => import('@pc/views/template/design.vue'),
        meta: { title: '模板设计器', icon: 'Edit', hidden: true }
      },
      {
        path: 'workflow',
        name: 'Workflow',
        component: () => import('@pc/views/workflow/index.vue'),
        meta: { title: '流程审批', icon: 'Share' }
      },
      {
        path: 'workflow/design/:id?',
        name: 'WorkflowDesign',
        component: () => import('@pc/views/workflow/design.vue'),
        meta: { title: '流程设计器', icon: 'Edit', hidden: true }
      },
      {
        path: 'workflow/tasks',
        name: 'WorkflowTasks',
        component: () => import('@pc/views/workflow/tasks.vue'),
        meta: { title: '我的待办', icon: 'List' }
      },
      {
        path: 'analytics',
        name: 'Analytics',
        component: () => import('@pc/views/analytics/index.vue'),
        meta: { title: '决策分析', icon: 'PieChart' }
      },
      {
        path: 'analytics/design/:id?',
        name: 'AnalyticsDesign',
        component: () => import('@pc/views/analytics/design.vue'),
        meta: { title: '仪表盘设计器', icon: 'Edit', hidden: true }
      },
      {
        path: 'integration',
        name: 'Integration',
        component: () => import('@pc/views/integration/index.vue'),
        meta: { title: '数据集成', icon: 'Connection' }
      },
      {
        path: 'system',
        name: 'System',
        redirect: '/system/users',
        meta: { title: '系统管理', icon: 'Setting' },
        children: [
          {
            path: 'users',
            name: 'Users',
            component: () => import('@pc/views/system/users.vue'),
            meta: { title: '用户管理' }
          },
          {
            path: 'roles',
            name: 'Roles',
            component: () => import('@pc/views/system/roles.vue'),
            meta: { title: '角色权限' }
          },
          {
            path: 'organizations',
            name: 'Organizations',
            component: () => import('@pc/views/system/organizations.vue'),
            meta: { title: '组织架构' }
          },
          {
            path: 'settings',
            name: 'Settings',
            component: () => import('@pc/views/system/settings.vue'),
            meta: { title: '系统设置' }
          },
          {
            path: 'logs',
            name: 'Logs',
            component: () => import('@pc/views/system/logs.vue'),
            meta: { title: '系统日志' }
          }
        ]
      },
      {
        path: 'migration',
        name: 'Migration',
        component: () => import('@pc/views/migration/index.vue'),
        meta: { title: '数据迁移', icon: 'Upload' }
      }
    ]
  },
  {
    path: '/:pathMatch(.*)*',
    component: BlankLayout,
    children: [
      {
        path: '',
        name: 'NotFound',
        component: () => import('@pc/views/error/404.vue'),
        meta: { title: '404' }
      }
    ]
  }
]

const router = createRouter({
  history: createWebHistory(),
  routes
})

// 路由守卫
router.beforeEach((to, _from, next) => {
  const userStore = useUserStore()
  const requiresAuth = to.matched.some(record => record.meta.requiresAuth !== false)
  
  if (requiresAuth && !userStore.isLoggedIn) {
    next({ name: 'Login', query: { redirect: to.fullPath } })
  } else {
    next()
  }
})

export default router

<template>
  <el-menu
    :default-active="activeMenu"
    :collapse="collapsed"
    :unique-opened="true"
    background-color="#001529"
    text-color="#bfcbd9"
    active-text-color="#409eff"
    router
  >
    <template v-for="item in menuRoutes" :key="item.path">
      <template v-if="!item.meta?.hidden">
        <el-sub-menu v-if="item.children && item.children.length" :index="item.path">
          <template #title>
            <el-icon><component :is="item.meta?.icon" /></el-icon>
            <span>{{ item.meta?.title }}</span>
          </template>
          <el-menu-item v-for="child in item.children" :key="child.path" :index="item.path + '/' + child.path">
            <el-icon><component :is="child.meta?.icon" /></el-icon>
            <span>{{ child.meta?.title }}</span>
          </el-menu-item>
        </el-sub-menu>
        <el-menu-item v-else :index="item.path">
          <el-icon><component :is="item.meta?.icon" /></el-icon>
          <span>{{ item.meta?.title }}</span>
        </el-menu-item>
      </template>
    </template>
  </el-menu>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed } from 'vue'
import { useRoute } from 'vue-router'
import { routes } from '@pc/router'

defineProps<{ collapsed: boolean }>()

const route = useRoute()
const activeMenu = computed(() => route.path)

// 过滤出需要显示在菜单中的路由
const menuRoutes = computed(() => {
  return routes.find(r => r.path === '/')?.children?.filter(r => !r.meta?.hidden) || []
})
</script>

<template>
  <div class="app-header">
    <div class="left">
      <el-icon class="collapse-btn" @click="emit('toggle-sidebar')">
        <Fold v-if="!appStore.sidebarCollapsed" />
        <Expand v-else />
      </el-icon>
      <el-breadcrumb separator="/">
        <el-breadcrumb-item :to="{ path: '/' }">首页</el-breadcrumb-item>
        <el-breadcrumb-item v-if="route.meta.title">{{ route.meta.title }}</el-breadcrumb-item>
      </el-breadcrumb>
    </div>
    <div class="right">
      <el-dropdown @command="handleCommand">
        <span class="user-info">
          <el-avatar :size="32" :src="userStore.userInfo?.avatar_url" />
          <span class="username">{{ userStore.userInfo?.full_name }}</span>
        </span>
        <template #dropdown>
          <el-dropdown-menu>
            <el-dropdown-item command="profile">个人中心</el-dropdown-item>
            <el-dropdown-item command="password">修改密码</el-dropdown-item>
            <el-dropdown-item divided command="logout">退出登录</el-dropdown-item>
          </el-dropdown-menu>
        </template>
      </el-dropdown>
    </div>
  </div>

<script setup lang="ts">
import { useRoute, useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { useUserStore } from '@shared/stores/user'
import { useAppStore } from '@shared/stores/app'
import { ElMessageBox } from 'element-plus'

const emit = defineEmits<{ (e: 'toggle-sidebar'): void }>()

const route = useRoute()
const router = useRouter()
const userStore = useUserStore()
const appStore = useAppStore()

async function handleCommand(command: string) {
  switch (command) {
    case 'profile':
      // 跳转个人中心
      break
    case 'password':
      // 修改密码弹窗
      break
    case 'logout':
      await ElMessageBox.confirm('确定要退出登录吗？', '提示', { type: 'warning' })
      await userStore.logout()
      router.push('/login')
      break
  }
}
</script>

frontend/src/pc/layouts/components/AIChatFloat.vue
vue
<template>
  <div class="ai-chat-float" :class="{ expanded }">
    <div class="chat-header" @click="toggle">
      <div class="header-left">
        <el-icon><ChatDotRound /></el-icon>
        <span>Kflower AI 助手</span>
      </div>
      <el-icon><ArrowDown v-if="expanded" /><ArrowUp v-else /></el-icon>
    </div>
    <div v-show="expanded" class="chat-body">
      <div class="messages" ref="messagesRef">
        <div v-for="(msg, idx) in messages" :key="idx" :class="['message', msg.role]">
          <el-avatar v-if="msg.role === 'assistant'" :size="32" src="/ai-avatar.png" />
          <div class="content" v-html="formatContent(msg.content)"></div>
          <el-avatar v-if="msg.role === 'user'" :size="32" :src="userStore.userInfo?.avatar_url" />
        </div>
        <div v-if="streaming" class="message assistant">
          <el-avatar :size="32" src="/ai-avatar.png" />
          <div class="content">
            {{ streamingContent }}
            <span class="cursor">|</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="chat-input">
        <el-input
          v-model="inputText"
          placeholder="输入消息，例如：帮我创建一个客户管理模板"
          @keyup.enter="sendMessage"
          :disabled="streaming"
          clearable
        />
        <el-button type="primary" :icon="Promotion" @click="sendMessage" :loading="streaming" />
      </div>
      <div class="quick-actions">
        <el-tag v-for="action in quickActions" :key="action" @click="inputText = action" size="small">
          {{ action }}
        </el-tag>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref, nextTick, watch, computed } from 'vue'
import { useUserStore } from '@shared/stores/user'
import { aiApi } from '@shared/api/ai'
import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'
import { ChatDotRound, ArrowDown, ArrowUp, Promotion } from '@element-plus/icons-vue'
import { marked } from 'marked'

const expanded = ref(false)
const messages = ref<Array<{ role: string; content: string }>>([
  { role: 'assistant', content: '你好！我是 Kflower AI 助手。我可以帮你：\n- 创建业务模板\n- 查询数据\n- 设计流程\n- 分析报表\n有什么我可以帮你的吗？' }
])
const inputText = ref('')
const streaming = ref(false)
const streamingContent = ref('')
const messagesRef = ref<HTMLElement>()

const userStore = useUserStore()

const quickActions = [
  '创建一个客户管理模板',
  '查询本月销售额',
  '我的待办任务',
  '分析销售趋势'
]

function toggle() {
  expanded.value = !expanded.value
  if (expanded.value) {
    nextTick(() => scrollToBottom())
  }
}

function formatContent(content: string) {
  return marked(content)
}

async function sendMessage() {
  if (!inputText.value.trim() || streaming.value) return
  
  const userMsg = inputText.value.trim()
  messages.value.push({ role: 'user', content: userMsg })
  inputText.value = ''
  await nextTick()
  scrollToBottom()
  
  streaming.value = true
  streamingContent.value = ''
  
  try {
    const response = await aiApi.chatStream(messages.value)
    const reader = response.body?.getReader()
    const decoder = new TextDecoder()
    
    while (reader) {
      const { done, value } = await reader.read()
      if (done) break
      
      const chunk = decoder.decode(value)
      const lines = chunk.split('\n')
      
      for (const line of lines) {
        if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {
          try {
            const data = JSON.parse(line.slice(6))
            if (data.delta) {
              streamingContent.value += data.delta
              await nextTick()
              scrollToBottom()
            } else if (data.done) {
              messages.value.push({ role: 'assistant', content: streamingContent.value })
              streamingContent.value = ''
              streaming.value = false
            } else if (data.tool_call) {
              // 显示工具调用
            } else if (data.tool_result) {
              // 显示工具结果
            }
          } catch (e) {
            // ignore parse error
          }
        }
      }
    }
  } catch (error) {
    ElMessage.error('AI 服务连接失败')
    streaming.value = false
  }
  
  scrollToBottom()
}

function scrollToBottom() {
  if (messagesRef.value) {
    messagesRef.value.scrollTop = messagesRef.value.scrollHeight
  }
}

watch(messages, () => {
  nextTick(() => scrollToBottom())
}, { deep: true })
</script>

      }
    }
  }
}

@keyframes blink {

            placeholder="描述你想创建的模板，例如：我需要一个采购管理系统，包含供应商管理、采购申请、采购订单、入库验收功能"
          />
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="模板名称">
          <el-input v-model="aiTemplateName" placeholder="可选，留空则自动生成" />
        </el-form-item>
      </el-form>
      <template #footer>
        <el-button @click="showAIGenerate = false">取消</el-button>
        <el-button type="primary" @click="handleAIGenerate" :loading="aiGenerating">开始生成</el-button>
      </template>
    </el-dialog>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { templateApi } from '@shared/api/template'
import { ElMessage, ElMessageBox } from 'element-plus'
import dayjs from 'dayjs'

const router = useRouter()

const loading = ref(false)
const tableData = ref([])
const total = ref(0)
const page = ref(1)
const pageSize = ref(20)
const keyword = ref('')
const categoryId = ref('')
const categories = ref([])

const showAIGenerate = ref(false)
const aiDescription = ref('')
const aiTemplateName = ref('')
const aiGenerating = ref(false)

async function fetchCategories() {
  const res = await templateApi.getCategories()
  categories.value = res
}

async function fetchData() {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    const res = await templateApi.list({
      page: page.value,
      page_size: pageSize.value,
      keyword: keyword.value,
      category_id: categoryId.value
    })
    tableData.value = res.items
    total.value = res.total
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

function goToDesign(id: string) {
  router.push(`/template/design/${id}`)
}

function handleCreate() {
  router.push('/template/design')
}

async function handlePublish(row: any) {
  await templateApi.publish(row.id)
  ElMessage.success('发布成功')
  fetchData()
}

async function handleCopy(row: any) {
  const { value } = await ElMessageBox.prompt('请输入新模板名称', '复制模板', {
    confirmButtonText: '确定',
    cancelButtonText: '取消',
    inputValue: `${row.name}_副本`
  })
  if (value) {
    await templateApi.copy(row.id, value)
    ElMessage.success('复制成功')
    fetchData()
  }
}

async function handleDelete(row: any) {
  await ElMessageBox.confirm('确定要删除该模板吗？', '提示', { type: 'warning' })
  await templateApi.delete(row.id)
  ElMessage.success('删除成功')
  fetchData()
}

async function handleAIGenerate() {
  if (!aiDescription.value) {
    ElMessage.warning('请输入模板描述')
    return
  }
  aiGenerating.value = true
  try {
    const res = await templateApi.aiGenerate(aiDescription.value, aiTemplateName.value)
    ElMessage.success('模板生成成功！')
    showAIGenerate.value = false
    aiDescription.value = ''
    aiTemplateName.value = ''
    router.push(`/template/design/${res.id}`)
  } catch (error) {
    ElMessage.error('生成失败')
  } finally {
    aiGenerating.value = false
  }
}

function formatDate(date: string) {
  return dayjs(date).format('YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm')
}

onMounted(() => {
  fetchCategories()
  fetchData()
})
</script>

<style lang="scss" scoped>
.template-page {
  .card-header {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
  }
  
  .filter-bar {
    display: flex;
    gap: 12px;
    margin-bottom: 20px;
  }
  
  .pagination {
    margin-top: 20px;
    display: flex;
    justify-content: flex-end;
  }
}
</style>
批次六部分核心代码已提供。 由于篇幅限制，其他视图组件（如流程设计器、仪表盘设计器、数据迁移页面）结构类似，可在后续按需展开。

项目已具备：

完整的项目配置和构建脚本

设备检测与 PC/移动端分发

PC 端布局、路由、状态管理

AI 对话浮窗组件

模板管理页面（含 AI 生成）

是否继续输出批次七（移动端代码）和批次八（部署配置）？

继续输出
我们已完成了六批代码输出，现在用户要求继续输出。接下来应该是批次七（移动端代码）和批次八（部署配置）。用户明确说“继续输出”，我需要提供移动端完整代码和部署配置。

考虑到移动端需要独立设计，采用 Vant 组件库，并放置在 app 目录下。同时要包含数据迁移页面、组织架构管理、权限管理等功能的移动端适配。

我将按以下结构输出批次七内容：

移动端主应用入口和路由

移动端布局组件

移动端主要视图（工作台、模板、流程、我的等）

移动端 AI 助手组件

由于在 App 环境中，内容需要精炼但完整。

Kflower 企业智能管理低代码平台 —— 批次七：移动端完整代码
本批次提供移动端独立代码，基于 Vue 3 + TypeScript + Vant 4 + Pinia，放置在 src/app/ 目录下。
