typescript
import { createRouter, createWebHistory } from 'vue-router'
import TabbarLayout from '@app/layouts/TabbarLayout.vue'
import NavBarLayout from '@app/layouts/NavBarLayout.vue'

const routes = [
  {
    path: '/login',
    component: NavBarLayout,
    children: [
      {
        path: '',
        name: 'Login',
        component: () => import('@app/views/login/index.vue'),
        meta: { title: '登录', requiresAuth: false }
      }
    ]
  },
  {
    path: '/',
    component: TabbarLayout,
    redirect: '/home',
    children: [
      {
        path: 'home',
        name: 'Home',
        component: () => import('@app/views/home/index.vue'),
        meta: { title: '工作台', icon: 'home-o' }
      },
      {
        path: 'workflow',
        name: 'Workflow',
        component: () => import('@app/views/workflow/index.vue'),
        meta: { title: '流程', icon: 'todo-list-o' }
      },
      {
        path: 'template',
        name: 'Template',
        component: () => import('@app/views/template/index.vue'),
        meta: { title: '模板', icon: 'apps-o' }
      },
      {
        path: 'analytics',
        name: 'Analytics',
        component: () => import('@app/views/analytics/index.vue'),
        meta: { title: '分析', icon: 'chart-trending-o' }
      },
      {
        path: 'profile',
        name: 'Profile',
        component: () => import('@app/views/profile/index.vue'),
        meta: { title: '我的', icon: 'user-o' }
      }
    ]
  },
  {
    path: '/workflow/tasks',
    component: NavBarLayout,
    children: [
      {
        path: '',
        name: 'Tasks',
        component: () => import('@app/views/workflow/tasks.vue'),
        meta: { title: '我的待办' }
      }
    ]
  },
  {
    path: '/workflow/detail/:id',
    component: NavBarLayout,
    children: [
      {
        path: '',
        name: 'WorkflowDetail',
        component: () => import('@app/views/workflow/detail.vue'),
        meta: { title: '流程详情' }
      }
    ]
  },
  {
    path: '/template/detail/:id',
    component: NavBarLayout,
    children: [
      {
        path: '',
        name: 'TemplateDetail',
        component: () => import('@app/views/template/detail.vue'),
        meta: { title: '模板详情' }
      }
    ]
  },
  {
    path: '/migration',
    component: NavBarLayout,
    children: [
      {
        path: '',
        name: 'Migration',
        component: () => import('@app/views/migration/index.vue'),
        meta: { title: '数据迁移' }
      }
    ]
  }
]

const router = createRouter({
  history: createWebHistory(),
  routes
})

router.beforeEach((to, _from, next) => {
  const userStore = useUserStore()
  if (to.meta.requiresAuth !== false && !userStore.isLoggedIn) {
    next('/login')
  } else {
    next()
  }
})

export default router

          v-model="form.password"
          type="password"
          name="password"
          label="密码"

        />
        <van-field
          v-model="form.tenant_id"
          name="tenant_id"
          label="租户"
          placeholder="可选"
        />
      </van-cell-group>
      <div class="submit-btn">
        <van-button type="primary" native-type="submit" :loading="loading" block round>
          登录
        </van-button>
      </div>
    </van-form>

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { useUserStore } from '@shared/stores/user'
import { showToast } from 'vant'

const router = useRouter()
const userStore = useUserStore()
const loading = ref(false)
const rememberMe = ref(false)
const version = import.meta.env.VITE_APP_VERSION || '1.0.0'

const form = ref({
  username: '',
  password: '',
  tenant_id: ''
})

async function handleLogin() {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    await userStore.login(form.value)
    showToast('登录成功')
    router.push('/home')
  } catch (error) {
    showToast('登录失败')
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

<template>
  <div class="home-page">
    <van-nav-bar title="工作台">
      <template #right>

      <van-grid-item icon="records-o" text="已办" />
      <van-grid-item icon="send-o" text="发起" @click="showStartFlow = true" />

流程待办 frontend/src/app/views/workflow/tasks.vue
vue
<template>
  <div class="tasks-page">
    <van-tabs v-model:active="activeTab">
      <van-tab title="待办">
        <van-pull-refresh v-model="refreshing" @refresh="onRefresh">
          <van-list v-model:loading="loading" :finished="finished" @load="onLoad">
            <ApprovalCard
              v-for="task in tasks"
              :key="task.id"
              :task="task"
              @click="goToDetail(task)"
              @approve="handleApprove(task)"
              @reject="handleReject(task)"
            />
          </van-list>
        </van-pull-refresh>
      </van-tab>
      <van-tab title="已办">
        <van-list v-model:loading="loadingDone" :finished="finishedDone" @load="onLoadDone">
          <ApprovalCard v-for="task in doneTasks" :key="task.id" :task="task" readonly />
        </van-list>
      </van-tab>
    </van-tabs>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import ApprovalCard from '@app/components/ApprovalCard/index.vue'
import { workflowApi } from '@shared/api/workflow'
import { showConfirmDialog, showToast } from 'vant'

const router = useRouter()
const activeTab = ref(0)
const refreshing = ref(false)
const loading = ref(false)
const finished = ref(false)
const tasks = ref([])
const doneTasks = ref([])
const loadingDone = ref(false)
const finishedDone = ref(false)

async function onRefresh() {
  refreshing.value = true
  finished.value = false
  await loadTasks()
  refreshing.value = false
}

async function onLoad() {
  await loadTasks()
}

async function loadTasks() {
  const res = await workflowApi.getPendingTasks()
  tasks.value = res
  finished.value = true
}

async function onLoadDone() {
  // 加载已办
}

function goToDetail(task: any) {
  router.push(`/workflow/detail/${task.id}`)
}

async function handleApprove(task: any) {
  await showConfirmDialog({ title: '审批通过', message: '确定通过此申请吗？' })
  await workflowApi.approveTask(task.id)
  showToast('审批成功')
  onRefresh()
}

async function handleReject(task: any) {
  await showConfirmDialog({ title: '审批拒绝', message: '确定拒绝此申请吗？' })
  await workflowApi.rejectTask(task.id)
  showToast('已拒绝')
  onRefresh()
}
</script>
AI 助手组件 frontend/src/app/components/AIChat/index.vue
vue
<template>
  <van-floating-bubble
    v-model:offset="offset"
    icon="chat-o"
    @click="showPopup = true"
  />
  <van-popup v-model:show="showPopup" position="bottom" :style="{ height: '70%' }" round>
    <div class="ai-chat-popup">
      <van-nav-bar title="AI 助手" @click-right="showPopup = false">
        <template #right>
          <van-icon name="cross" />
        </template>
      </van-nav-bar>
      <div class="messages" ref="messagesRef">
        <div v-for="(msg, idx) in messages" :key="idx" :class="['message', msg.role]">
          <div class="content">{{ msg.content }}</div>
        </div>
        <div v-if="streaming" class="message assistant">
          <div class="content">{{ streamingContent }}<span class="cursor">|</span></div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="input-bar">
        <van-field
          v-model="inputText"
          placeholder="输入消息..."
          :disabled="streaming"
          @keyup.enter="sendMessage"
        />
        <van-button type="primary" size="small" @click="sendMessage" :loading="streaming">发送</van-button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </van-popup>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref, nextTick } from 'vue'
import { aiApi } from '@shared/api/ai'

const showPopup = ref(false)
const offset = ref({ x: 20, y: 20 })
const messages = ref<Array<{ role: string; content: string }>>([
  { role: 'assistant', content: '你好！我是 Kflower AI 助手，有什么可以帮你？' }
])
const inputText = ref('')
const streaming = ref(false)
const streamingContent = ref('')
const messagesRef = ref<HTMLElement>()

async function sendMessage() {
  if (!inputText.value.trim() || streaming.value) return
  
  const userMsg = inputText.value
  messages.value.push({ role: 'user', content: userMsg })
  inputText.value = ''
  scrollToBottom()
  
  streaming.value = true
  streamingContent.value = ''
  
  try {
    const response = await aiApi.chatStream(messages.value)
    const reader = response.body?.getReader()
    const decoder = new TextDecoder()
    
    while (reader) {
      const { done, value } = await reader.read()
      if (done) break
      
      const chunk = decoder.decode(value)
      const lines = chunk.split('\n')
      for (const line of lines) {
        if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {
          const data = JSON.parse(line.slice(6))
          if (data.delta) {
            streamingContent.value += data.delta
            scrollToBottom()
          } else if (data.done) {
            messages.value.push({ role: 'assistant', content: streamingContent.value })
            streamingContent.value = ''
            streaming.value = false
          }
        }
      }
    }
  } catch (error) {
    streaming.value = false
  }
}

function scrollToBottom() {
  nextTick(() => {
    if (messagesRef.value) {
      messagesRef.value.scrollTop = messagesRef.value.scrollHeight
    }
  })
}
</script>

数据迁移页 frontend/src/app/views/migration/index.vue
vue
<template>
  <div class="migration-page">
    <van-cell-group title="源数据库">
      <van-field v-model="source.type" label="类型" readonly @click="showSourceType = true" />
      <van-field v-model="source.url" label="连接串" placeholder="sqlite:///./dev.db" />
    </van-cell-group>
    
    <van-cell-group title="目标数据库">
      <van-field v-model="target.type" label="类型" readonly @click="showTargetType = true" />
      <van-field v-model="target.url" label="连接串" placeholder="mysql://user:pass@host/db" />
    </van-cell-group>
    
    <div class="actions">
      <van-button type="primary" @click="preview" :loading="previewing">预览</van-button>
      <van-button type="success" @click="execute" :loading="migrating" :disabled="!previewData">开始迁移</van-button>
    </div>
    
    <van-divider v-if="previewData">迁移预览</van-divider>
    <van-cell-group v-if="previewData">
      <van-cell title="总表数" :value="previewData.total_tables" />
      <van-cell title="总记录数" :value="previewData.total_records" />
    </van-cell-group>
    
    <van-progress v-if="taskId" :percentage="progress" :status="progressStatus" />
    
    <van-action-sheet v-model:show="showSourceType" :actions="dbTypes" @select="onSelectSourceType" />
    <van-action-sheet v-model:show="showTargetType" :actions="dbTypes" @select="onSelectTargetType" />
  </div>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref, computed } from 'vue'
import { migrationApi } from '@shared/api/migration'
import { showToast } from 'vant'

const dbTypes = [
  { name: 'SQLite', value: 'sqlite' },
  { name: 'MySQL', value: 'mysql' },
  { name: 'PostgreSQL', value: 'postgresql' }
]

const source = ref({ type: 'sqlite', url: 'sqlite:///./kflower_dev.db' })
const target = ref({ type: 'mysql', url: '' })
const showSourceType = ref(false)
const showTargetType = ref(false)
const previewing = ref(false)
const migrating = ref(false)
const previewData = ref(null)
const taskId = ref('')
const progress = ref(0)
const error = ref('')

const progressStatus = computed(() => error.value ? 'exception' : '')

function onSelectSourceType(item: any) {
  source.value.type = item.value
  showSourceType.value = false
}

function onSelectTargetType(item: any) {
  target.value.type = item.value
  showTargetType.value = false
}

async function preview() {
  previewing.value = true
  try {
    previewData.value = await migrationApi.preview(source.value, target.value)
  } catch {
    showToast('预览失败')
  } finally {
    previewing.value = false
  }
}

async function execute() {
  migrating.value = true
  try {
    const res = await migrationApi.execute(source.value, target.value)
    taskId.value = res.task_id
    pollStatus()
  } catch {
    showToast('迁移失败')
    migrating.value = false
  }
}

function pollStatus() {
  const timer = setInterval(async () => {
    const res = await migrationApi.getStatus(taskId.value)
    progress.value = res.progress
    if (res.status === 'completed') {
      clearInterval(timer)
      migrating.value = false
      showToast('迁移完成')
    } else if (res.status === 'failed') {
      clearInterval(timer)
      migrating.value = false
      error.value = res.error
      showToast('迁移失败')
    }
  }, 2000)
}
</script>

<style lang="scss" scoped>
.migration-page {
  padding: 16px;
  
  .actions {
    display: flex;
    gap: 12px;
    margin: 20px 0;
  }
}
</style>

是否继续输出批次八（部署配置与 Docker）？

继续输出
我们继续输出第八批次：部署配置与Docker文件。这包括docker-compose配置、Dockerfile、nginx配置、环境变量示例等。需要确保前后端能够正确部署，包含数据库、Redis、AI服务等。输出内容要完整实用。

Kflower 企业智能管理低代码平台 —— 批次八：部署配置与 Docker
本批次提供完整的部署配置文件，包括 Docker Compose、Dockerfile、Nginx 配置及环境变量模板。

2. Docker Compose 配置
docker-compose.yml（生产环境）
yaml
version: '3.8'

networks:
  kflower-network:
    driver: bridge

volumes:
  postgres_data:
  redis_data:
  uploads_data:
  model_cache:

services:
  # ========== PostgreSQL + pgvector ==========
  postgres:
    image: pgvector/pgvector:pg16
    container_name: kflower-postgres
    restart: unless-stopped
    environment:
      POSTGRES_USER: ${POSTGRES_USER:-kflower}
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ${POSTGRES_PASSWORD:-kflower123}
      POSTGRES_DB: ${POSTGRES_DB:-kflower}
    volumes:
      - postgres_data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
      - ./scripts/init-db.sh:/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/init-db.sh
    ports:
      - "5432:5432"
    networks:
      - kflower-network
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U kflower"]
      interval: 10s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 5

  # ========== Redis ==========
  redis:
    image: redis:7-alpine
    container_name: kflower-redis
    restart: unless-stopped
    command: redis-server --appendonly yes --requirepass ${REDIS_PASSWORD:-kflower123}
    volumes:
      - redis_data:/data
    ports:
      - "6379:6379"
    networks:
      - kflower-network
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD", "redis-cli", "ping"]
      interval: 10s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 5

  # ========== 后端服务 ==========
  backend:
    build:
      context: ./backend
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    image: kflower-backend:latest
    container_name: kflower-backend
    restart: unless-stopped
    environment:
      - ENVIRONMENT=production
      - DATABASE_URL=${DATABASE_URL}
      - REDIS_URL=redis://:${REDIS_PASSWORD:-kflower123}@redis:6379/0
      - VECTOR_DB_TYPE=${VECTOR_DB_TYPE:-pgvector}
      - VECTOR_DB_URL=${VECTOR_DB_URL}
      - SECRET_KEY=${SECRET_KEY}
      - DEEPSEEK_API_KEY=${DEEPSEEK_API_KEY}
      - LOCAL_LLM_ENDPOINT=${LOCAL_LLM_ENDPOINT}
      - SMTP_HOST=${SMTP_HOST}
      - SMTP_PORT=${SMTP_PORT}
      - SMTP_USER=${SMTP_USER}
      - SMTP_PASSWORD=${SMTP_PASSWORD}
      - SMTP_FROM=${SMTP_FROM}
      - UPLOAD_DIR=/app/uploads
    volumes:
      - uploads_data:/app/uploads
      - model_cache:/root/.cache/huggingface
    ports:
      - "8000:8000"
    networks:
      - kflower-network
    depends_on:
      postgres:
        condition: service_healthy
      redis:
        condition: service_healthy
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD", "curl", "-f", "http://localhost:8000/health"]
      interval: 30s
      timeout: 10s
      retries: 3

  # ========== 前端服务 ==========
  frontend:
    build:
      context: ./frontend
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
      args:
        - VITE_API_BASE_URL=/api/v1
    image: kflower-frontend:latest
    container_name: kflower-frontend
    restart: unless-stopped
    ports:
      - "80:80"
      - "443:443"
    networks:
      - kflower-network
    depends_on:
      - backend
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD", "curl", "-f", "http://localhost:80/health"]
      interval: 30s
      timeout: 10s
      retries: 3

  # ========== vLLM 模型服务（可选，需要 GPU） ==========
  vllm:
    image: vllm/vllm-openai:latest
    container_name: kflower-vllm
    restart: unless-stopped
    command: >
      --model ${VLLM_MODEL:-Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct}
      --served-model-name local-llm
      --max-model-len 8192
      --gpu-memory-utilization 0.9
    deploy:
      resources:
        reservations:
          devices:
            - driver: nvidia
              count: 1
              capabilities: [gpu]
    volumes:
      - model_cache:/root/.cache/huggingface
    ports:
      - "8001:8000"
    networks:
      - kflower-network
    profiles:
      - gpu

3. Dockerfile
backend/Dockerfile
dockerfile
# 构建阶段
FROM python:3.11-slim as builder

WORKDIR /app

# 安装系统依赖
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
    gcc \
    g++ \
    libpq-dev \
    libmariadb-dev \
    pkg-config \
    curl \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

# 安装 Poetry 或直接使用 pip
COPY requirements.txt .
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir --user -r requirements.txt

# 运行阶段
FROM python:3.11-slim

WORKDIR /app

# 安装运行时依赖
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
    libpq5 \
    libmariadb3 \
    curl \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

# 复制 Python 包
COPY --from=builder /root/.local /root/.local
ENV PATH=/root/.local/bin:$PATH

# 复制应用代码
COPY . .

# 创建非 root 用户
RUN useradd -m -u 1000 appuser && chown -R appuser:appuser /app
USER appuser

# 创建上传目录
RUN mkdir -p /app/uploads

# 暴露端口
EXPOSE 8000

# 健康检查
HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=10s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \
    CMD curl -f http://localhost:8000/health || exit 1

# 启动命令
CMD ["uvicorn", "app.main:app", "--host", "0.0.0.0", "--port", "8000", "--workers", "4"]
backend/Dockerfile.dev
dockerfile
FROM python:3.11-slim

WORKDIR /app

RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
    gcc \
    libpq-dev \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

COPY requirements.txt .
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r requirements.txt

EXPOSE 8000

CMD ["uvicorn", "app.main:app", "--host", "0.0.0.0", "--port", "8000", "--reload"]
backend/entrypoint.sh
bash
#!/bin/bash
set -e

# 等待数据库就绪
echo "Waiting for database..."
while ! nc -z postgres 5432; do
  sleep 1
done
echo "Database is ready!"

# 运行数据库迁移
echo "Running database migrations..."
alembic upgrade head

# 启动应用
echo "Starting application..."
exec uvicorn app.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000 --workers 4

frontend/Dockerfile
dockerfile
# 构建阶段
FROM node:20-alpine as builder

WORKDIR /app

# 复制依赖文件
COPY package*.json ./
COPY pnpm-lock.yaml* ./

# 安装 pnpm 和依赖
RUN npm install -g pnpm && pnpm install --frozen-lockfile

# 复制源代码
COPY . .

# 构建参数
ARG VITE_API_BASE_URL=/api/v1
ARG VITE_APP_TITLE=Kflower

# 构建
RUN pnpm run build

# 运行阶段
FROM nginx:alpine

# 复制构建产物
COPY --from=builder /app/dist /usr/share/nginx/html

# 复制 nginx 配置
COPY nginx.conf /etc/nginx/conf.d/default.conf

# 健康检查
HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=10s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \
    CMD curl -f http://localhost:80/health || exit 1

EXPOSE 80 443

CMD ["nginx", "-g", "daemon off;"]
frontend/nginx.conf
nginx
server {
    listen 80;
    server_name _;
    root /usr/share/nginx/html;
    index index.html;

    # Gzip 压缩
    gzip on;
    gzip_vary on;
    gzip_min_length 1024;
    gzip_types text/plain text/css text/xml text/javascript application/javascript application/json application/xml+rss image/svg+xml;

    # 健康检查
    location /health {
        access_log off;
        return 200 "healthy\n";
        add_header Content-Type text/plain;
    }

    # API 代理
    location /api/ {
        proxy_pass http://backend:8000/api/;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Upgrade $http_upgrade;
        proxy_set_header Connection 'upgrade';
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
        proxy_cache_bypass $http_upgrade;
        proxy_read_timeout 300s;
        proxy_connect_timeout 75s;
    }

    # WebSocket 支持（用于流式响应）
    location /api/v1/ai/chat/stream {
        proxy_pass http://backend:8000/api/v1/ai/chat/stream;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Upgrade $http_upgrade;
        proxy_set_header Connection 'upgrade';
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_cache_bypass $http_upgrade;
        proxy_buffering off;
        proxy_read_timeout 300s;
    }

    # 静态资源缓存
    location ~* \.(js|css|png|jpg|jpeg|gif|ico|svg|woff|woff2|ttf|eot)$ {
        expires 1y;
        add_header Cache-Control "public, immutable";
    }

    # SPA 路由支持
    location / {
        try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html;
    }

    # 安全头
    add_header X-Frame-Options "SAMEORIGIN" always;
    add_header X-Content-Type-Options "nosniff" always;
    add_header X-XSS-Protection "1; mode=block" always;
}

4. 反向代理配置（可选）
nginx/nginx.conf
nginx
# 用于生产环境前置 Nginx
upstream backend {
    server backend:8000;
}

upstream frontend {
    server frontend:80;
}

server {
    listen 80;
    server_name kflower.example.com;
    return 301 https://$server_name$request_uri;
}

server {
    listen 443 ssl http2;
    server_name kflower.example.com;

    ssl_certificate /etc/nginx/ssl/fullchain.pem;
    ssl_certificate_key /etc/nginx/ssl/privkey.pem;
    ssl_protocols TLSv1.2 TLSv1.3;
    ssl_ciphers HIGH:!aNULL:!MD5;

    # 前端
    location / {
        proxy_pass http://frontend;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
    }

    # API
    location /api/ {
        proxy_pass http://backend/api/;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Upgrade $http_upgrade;
        proxy_set_header Connection 'upgrade';
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
        proxy_cache_bypass $http_upgrade;
        proxy_read_timeout 300s;
    }

    # 上传文件大小限制
    client_max_body_size 50M;
}
5. 脚本文件
scripts/init-db.sh
bash
#!/bin/bash
set -e

psql -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 --username "$POSTGRES_USER" --dbname "$POSTGRES_DB" <<-EOSQL
    -- 创建向量扩展
    CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS vector;
    
    -- 创建应用用户（如果使用不同用户）
    -- CREATE USER kflower_app WITH PASSWORD 'app_password';
    -- GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON DATABASE $POSTGRES_DB TO kflower_app;
    
    -- 初始化租户数据
    INSERT INTO tenants (id, code, name, is_active, created_at, updated_at)
    VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), 'default', '默认租户', true, NOW(), NOW())
    ON CONFLICT (code) DO NOTHING;
EOSQL

echo "Database initialization completed!"
scripts/backup.sh
bash
#!/bin/bash
# 数据库备份脚本

set -e

BACKUP_DIR="/backups"
TIMESTAMP=$(date +%Y%m%d_%H%M%S)
BACKUP_FILE="$BACKUP_DIR/kflower_backup_$TIMESTAMP.sql.gz"

# 创建备份目录
mkdir -p $BACKUP_DIR

# 执行备份
echo "Starting backup to $BACKUP_FILE..."
PGPASSWORD=${POSTGRES_PASSWORD} pg_dump \
    -h ${POSTGRES_HOST:-postgres} \
    -U ${POSTGRES_USER:-kflower} \
    -d ${POSTGRES_DB:-kflower} \
    | gzip > $BACKUP_FILE

echo "Backup completed: $BACKUP_FILE"

# 删除 30 天前的备份
find $BACKUP_DIR -name "kflower_backup_*.sql.gz" -mtime +30 -delete

echo "Old backups cleaned up."
6. 启动指南
开发环境启动
bash
# 1. 启动依赖服务
docker-compose -f docker-compose.dev.yml up -d

# 2. 复制环境变量
cp .env.example .env
# 编辑 .env 设置开发环境配置

# 3. 启动后端（本地）
cd backend
pip install -r requirements.txt
uvicorn app.main:app --reload --port 8000

# 4. 启动前端（新终端）
cd frontend
pnpm install
pnpm run dev

# 访问
# 前端：http://localhost:5173
# API 文档：http://localhost:8000/api/docs
生产环境部署
bash
# 1. 准备环境变量
cp .env.example .env
# 编辑 .env 设置生产环境配置（修改 SECRET_KEY、数据库密码等）

# 2. 构建并启动所有服务
docker-compose up -d --build

# 3. 查看日志
docker-compose logs -f

# 4. 初始化数据（首次运行）
docker-compose exec backend python scripts/init_data.py

# 5. 创建超级用户
docker-compose exec backend python scripts/create_superuser.py
仅启动 AI 模型服务（GPU）
bash
# 启动 vLLM 服务
docker-compose --profile gpu up -d vllm

# 配置 .env
# LOCAL_LLM_ENDPOINT=http://localhost:8001/v1
# DEFAULT_LLM_MODEL=local-llm
7. Kubernetes 部署（可选）
k8s/namespace.yaml
yaml
apiVersion: v1
kind: Namespace
metadata:
  name: kflower
k8s/configmap.yaml
yaml
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: kflower-config
  namespace: kflower
data:
  ENVIRONMENT: "production"
  DEFAULT_LLM_MODEL: "deepseek-chat"
  VECTOR_DB_TYPE: "pgvector"
k8s/secrets.yaml
yaml
apiVersion: v1
kind: Secret
metadata:
  name: kflower-secrets
  namespace: kflower
type: Opaque
stringData:
  SECRET_KEY: "your-secure-secret-key"
  DATABASE_URL: "postgresql+asyncpg://kflower:kflower123@postgres:5432/kflower"
  REDIS_URL: "redis://:kflower123@redis:6379/0"
  DEEPSEEK_API_KEY: "your-api-key"
k8s/postgres.yaml
yaml
apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
  name: postgres
  namespace: kflower
spec:
  selector:
    app: postgres
  ports:
    - port: 5432
---
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: StatefulSet
metadata:
  name: postgres
  namespace: kflower
spec:
  serviceName: postgres
  replicas: 1
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: postgres
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: postgres
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: postgres
          image: pgvector/pgvector:pg16
          env:
            - name: POSTGRES_USER
              value: kflower
            - name: POSTGRES_PASSWORD
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef:
                  name: kflower-secrets
                  key: POSTGRES_PASSWORD
            - name: POSTGRES_DB
              value: kflower
          ports:
            - containerPort: 5432
          volumeMounts:
            - name: data
              mountPath: /var/lib/postgresql/data
  volumeClaimTemplates:
    - metadata:
        name: data
      spec:
        accessModes: ["ReadWriteOnce"]
        resources:
          requests:
            storage: 50Gi
